Save this as `r2-hand-edits.mjs` in your temp folder and run it from the repo root, first as `node <temp>/r2-hand-edits.mjs --dry` (expected: `dry run: 8 lines in 3 files, nothing written`), then as `node <temp>/r2-hand-edits.mjs`. Each `from` string is quoted verbatim from the file after Step 4 (and, for c1, after Task 3). The script checks that every one occurs exactly once before it writes anything. The two settings strings carry their leading spaces, so they cannot match the settings tabs at :947.

```js
// R2 hand edits (UI phase 2A, Task 4). Run from the repo root AFTER codemod-r2.mjs: node <temp>/r2-hand-edits.mjs [--dry]
// Every `from` string is the text the codemod (and R7 in Task 3) left on that line. Checks every count first, writes only if all match.
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs"

const dry = process.argv.includes("--dry")
const dashboard = "src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx"
const roomList = "src/components/audit/audit-scan-room-list.tsx"
const settings = "src/components/admin/system-settings-form.tsx"
const edits = [
  // KPI link card: the mockup's a.stat:hover tints the card instead of lifting it
  [dashboard,
    'className="rounded-lg border border-border bg-surface p-5 transition-shadow hover:shadow-md"',
    'className="rounded-lg border border-border bg-surface p-5 transition-colors hover:bg-background"'],
  // Room list segmented control (mockup .seg): white frame with a line, 2px padding and gap
  [roomList,
    'className="flex gap-1 rounded-md bg-muted p-1"',
    'className="flex gap-0.5 rounded-md border border-border bg-surface p-0.5"'],
  // its buttons get the 6px control corner (I11)
  [roomList,
    '"flex min-h-11 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded px-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors',
    '"flex min-h-11 flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-control px-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors'],
  // pressed: primary-soft, primary-hover text, weight 600 (cn() lets font-semibold win over font-medium); others hover to the subtle fill
  [roomList,
    'tab === key ? "bg-surface text-foreground" : "text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground",',
    'tab === key ? "bg-primary-soft font-semibold text-primary-hover" : "text-muted-foreground hover:bg-background hover:text-foreground",'],
  // Settings label-name segmented control: same frame (R7 already turned bg-muted/20 into bg-background)
  [settings,
    '<div className="inline-flex rounded-md border border-border bg-background p-1" role="group" aria-label={label}>',
    '<div className="inline-flex gap-0.5 rounded-md border border-border bg-surface p-0.5" role="group" aria-label={label}>'],
  // 6px corner; the weight moves out of the base because this is a template string, not cn()
  [settings,
    "inline-flex h-8 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded px-3 text-sm font-medium transition-colors",
    "inline-flex h-8 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-control px-3 text-sm transition-colors"],
  // pressed (the Check icon stays) and not pressed; the indentation keeps these apart from the settings tabs at :947
  [settings,
    '                  ? "bg-primary text-white"',
    '                  ? "bg-primary-soft font-semibold text-primary-hover"'],
  [settings,
    '                  : "text-muted-foreground hover:bg-background hover:text-foreground"',
    '                  : "font-medium text-muted-foreground hover:bg-background hover:text-foreground"'],
]

const files = new Map()
const problems = []
for (const [path, from, to] of edits) {
  const source = files.get(path) ?? readFileSync(path, "utf8")
  const found = source.split(from).length - 1
  if (found !== 1) problems.push(`${path}: expected 1 of ${from}, found ${found}`)
  files.set(path, source.split(from).join(to))
}
if (problems.length > 0) {
  console.error(`ABORT: nothing was written\n${problems.join("\n")}`)
  process.exit(1)
}
if (dry) {
  console.log(`dry run: ${edits.length} lines in ${files.size} files, nothing written`)
  process.exit(0)
}
for (const [path, source] of files) writeFileSync(path, source)
console.log(`hand edits: ${edits.length} lines in ${files.size} files`)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Run the codemod**

Save this as `codemod-r3.mjs` in your temp folder (not in the repo). From the repo root, run it first as `node <temp>/codemod-r3.mjs --dry` (counts only, writes nothing), then as `node <temp>/codemod-r3.mjs`. The script counts every match first and writes nothing unless every per-file count matches. It replaces text in the whole file, so CRLF and LF line endings stay as they are.

```js
// R3: lift on-screen text below 12px to text-xs. Run from the repo root: node <temp>/codemod-r3.mjs [--dry]. Writes nothing unless every count matches.
import { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs"
import { join } from "node:path"

const dry = process.argv.includes("--dry")
const pattern = /text-\[1[01]px\]/g
const labelPrint = "src/components/assets/asset-label-print.tsx"
const expected = {
  "src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/disposal/page.tsx": 1,
  "src/components/admin/IntegrationClientManager.tsx": 1,
  "src/components/admin/system-settings-form.tsx": 1,
  "src/components/assets/asset-batch-form.tsx": 4,
  "src/components/layout/topbar.tsx": 1,
  "src/components/master-data/supplier-list-view.tsx": 1,
  "src/components/ui/file-dropzone.tsx": 1,
}

function walk(dir, out = []) {
  for (const entry of readdirSync(dir)) {
    const path = join(dir, entry)
    if (statSync(path).isDirectory()) walk(path, out)
    else if (/\.(ts|tsx|css)$/.test(entry)) out.push(path.replace(/\\/g, "/"))
  }
  return out
}

const found = {}
let skipped = 0
for (const path of walk("src")) {
  const count = (readFileSync(path, "utf8").match(pattern) ?? []).length
  if (count === 0) continue
  if (path === labelPrint) skipped = count
  else found[path] = count
}

const lines = (record) => Object.keys(record).sort().map((path) => `${path}: ${record[path]}`)
console.log(lines(found).join("\n"))
console.log(`skipped ${labelPrint}: ${skipped}`)
if (JSON.stringify(lines(found)) !== JSON.stringify(lines(expected)) || skipped !== 1) {
  console.error(`count mismatch, nothing written. Expected:\n${lines(expected).join("\n")}\nskipped ${labelPrint}: 1`)
  process.exit(1)
}

const total = Object.values(found).reduce((sum, count) => sum + count, 0)
if (dry) {
  console.log(`dry run: ${total} in ${Object.keys(found).length} files, nothing written`)
  process.exit(0)
}
// Whole-file replace: line endings (CRLF or LF) stay as they are.
for (const path of Object.keys(found)) {
  writeFileSync(path, readFileSync(path, "utf8").replace(pattern, "text-xs"))
}
console.log(`total ${total} in ${Object.keys(found).length} files`)
```

Expected output of the `--dry` run (exit code 0): the 8 lines below, then `dry run: 10 in 7 files, nothing written`.

Expected output of the real run (exit code 0):

```
src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/disposal/page.tsx: 1
src/components/admin/IntegrationClientManager.tsx: 1
src/components/admin/system-settings-form.tsx: 1
src/components/assets/asset-batch-form.tsx: 4
src/components/layout/topbar.tsx: 1
src/components/master-data/supplier-list-view.tsx: 1
src/components/ui/file-dropzone.tsx: 1
skipped src/components/assets/asset-label-print.tsx: 1
total 10 in 7 files
```

If the script prints `count mismatch, nothing written.` and exits 1, stop and report its output. Do not commit. (A second run on a tree it already changed also stops here, which is how you know the self-check works.)

- `git diff --ignore-cr-at-eol --stat -- src | tail -1` shows the same line, so no line ending changed.

### Task 6: Buttons: variants, sizes, busy, linkVariants, safe link helper, asset detail header links

Spec section 4 (`04-buttons.md`) with these rulings applied:
- **I15** replaces ย1 and the `rounded-control` in the 4.3 base string. The radius moves out of the base into each size: `sm` and `icon-sm` get `rounded-control` (6px); `md`, `lg`, `default` and `icon` get `rounded-md` (8px). Each size string ends with its radius class, so the existing regexes at `design-system.test.ts:95-96` still match.
- **I5** replaces ย3: `outline` and `ghost` press to `active:bg-muted`, not `active:bg-accent`.

Ruling for 4.5 (spinner placement): the spinner replaces the first child only when that child is an icon. An icon here means a component element that is not a `Fragment` (lucide icons are components), or an inline `<svg>`. Otherwise the spinner goes in front. Reason: a first child such as `<span>{label}</span>` is an element too, and replacing it would delete the label.

This task changes only the shared primitives and the two hand-written links in the asset detail header (ย27). The 31 call sites in spec 4.7 change look without code changes. **Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 1):
  - utilities `text-faint-foreground`, `border-control-border`, `rounded-control` and `text-meta`. Tailwind emits no CSS for these until Task 1's tokens exist.
  - `cn()` in `src/lib/utils.ts` built on `extendTailwindMerge`. Plain tailwind-merge drops `text-primary-foreground`, `text-foreground` and the other text colors from all six `sm` combinations, because it treats `text-meta` as a color. The new round-trip assertion `cn(classes) === classes` fails on those six combinations without Task 1.

    - sizes `sm | md | lg | default | icon-sm | icon`. `default` equals `lg` (40px from `md` up) until 2D, and every size is at least 44px below `md`.
  - `ButtonVariantProps` (unchanged name).
  - `linkVariants({ size?: "meta" | "inherit" })`, default `meta`.
  - `Button` prop `busy?: boolean`. It cannot be combined with `asChild`. A busy button gets `aria-busy`, `aria-disabled`, `data-busy`, a spinner and a click guard, and never the `disabled` attribute.
  - `getSafeActionLinkClasses(variant)`, now built from `buttonVariants` + `cn`.
  - `getTouchIconButtonClasses` no longer exists.

```ts
const buttonVariantNames = ["default", "outline", "ghost", "danger", "destructive", "warning"] as const
const buttonSizeNames = ["sm", "md", "lg", "default", "icon-sm", "icon"] as const
const classList = (value: string) => value.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean)

test("button variants keep 44px mobile touch targets and AA hover fills", () => {
  const primary = buttonVariants({ variant: "default" })
  assert.match(primary, /bg-primary text-primary-foreground/)
  assert.match(primary, /hover:bg-primary-hover/)
  assert.match(primary, /min-h-11/)
  assert.match(primary, /md:h-10 md:min-h-0/)
  assert.match(buttonVariants({ variant: "destructive" }), /bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground hover:bg-danger-hover/)
  assert.match(buttonVariants({ variant: "warning" }), /bg-warning text-warning-foreground hover:bg-warning-hover/)
  assert.match(buttonVariants({ variant: "outline" }), /border-control-border bg-surface/)
  assert.ok(classList(buttonVariants({ variant: "outline" })).includes("border"), "the base draws a 1px border")
  assert.match(buttonVariants({ size: "sm" }), /min-h-11 px-2\.5 text-meta md:h-8 md:min-h-0/)
  assert.match(buttonVariants({ size: "icon" }), /min-h-11 min-w-11 md:size-10 md:min-h-0 md:min-w-0/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(`${primary} ${buttonVariants({ variant: "destructive" })}`, /dark:/)
})

test("every button variant and size keeps 44px below md, a 1px border, no opacity and no sm: breakpoint", () => {
  for (const variant of buttonVariantNames) {
    for (const size of buttonSizeNames) {
      const classes = buttonVariants({ variant, size })
      const label = `${variant}/${size}`
      assert.ok(classList(classes).includes("min-h-11"), `${label}: min-h-11`)
      assert.ok(classList(classes).includes("border"), `${label}: border`)
      assert.doesNotMatch(classes, /opacity|(?<![\w-])sm:(?:h-|min-h|size-)|dark:/, label)
      assert.equal(cn(classes), classes, `${label}: cn() must not drop a class (Button merges through cn)`)
    }
  }
})

test("the disabled look wins over every variant without opacity, and busy shares it through aria-disabled", () => {
  const classes = buttonVariants({ variant: "outline", size: "md" })
  assert.match(classes, /cursor-pointer/)
  assert.match(classes, /gap-1\.5/)
  assert.match(classes, /disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:border-border disabled:bg-background disabled:text-faint-foreground/)
  assert.match(classes, /aria-disabled:pointer-events-none aria-disabled:border-border aria-disabled:bg-background aria-disabled:text-faint-foreground/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(classes, /(?<![\w-])disabled:pointer-events-none/, "a disabled button must still show the not-allowed cursor")
})

test("button variants follow the mockup: outlined danger in pages, solid destructive for dialog confirms", () => {
  const danger = buttonVariants({ variant: "danger" })
  assert.match(danger, /border-danger-border bg-surface text-danger/)
  assert.match(danger, /hover:bg-danger-soft/)
  assert.match(danger, /active:border-danger/)
  assert.ok(!classList(danger).some((name) => name === "bg-danger" || name === "bg-destructive"), "danger is outlined, not solid")
  assert.match(buttonVariants({ variant: "default" }), /active:border-brand-navy active:bg-brand-navy/)
  assert.match(buttonVariants({ variant: "outline" }), /hover:border-input hover:bg-background active:border-input active:bg-muted/)
  assert.match(buttonVariants({ variant: "ghost" }), /border-transparent bg-transparent text-muted-foreground hover:bg-background hover:text-foreground active:bg-muted/)
  for (const variant of buttonVariantNames) {
    assert.doesNotMatch(buttonVariants({ variant }), /active:bg-accent/, variant)
  }
  assert.doesNotMatch(buttonVariants({ variant: "secondary" as never }), /bg-secondary/, "secondary was removed")
  assert.doesNotMatch(buttonVariants({ variant: "link" as never }), /underline/, "link moved to linkVariants")
})

test("button sizes: sm 32, md 36, lg 40 from md up, default stays lg until phase 2D, radius per spec I15", () => {
  assert.match(buttonVariants({ size: "md" }), /min-h-11 px-3\.5 text-sm md:h-9 md:min-h-0/)
  assert.match(buttonVariants({ size: "lg" }), /min-h-11 px-4 text-sm md:h-10 md:min-h-0/)
  assert.equal(buttonVariants({ size: "default" }), buttonVariants({ size: "lg" }), "phase 2D switches default to md")
  assert.match(buttonVariants({ size: "icon-sm" }), /min-h-11 min-w-11 md:size-8 md:min-h-0 md:min-w-0/)
  for (const size of ["sm", "icon-sm"] as const) {
    assert.ok(classList(buttonVariants({ size })).includes("rounded-control"), `${size} is a small control (6px)`)
    assert.doesNotMatch(buttonVariants({ size }), /rounded-md/, size)
  }
  for (const size of ["md", "lg", "default", "icon"] as const) {
    assert.ok(classList(buttonVariants({ size })).includes("rounded-md"), `${size} matches the fields (8px)`)
    assert.doesNotMatch(buttonVariants({ size }), /rounded-control/, size)

})

test("linkVariants draws a text link that underlines on hover and focus, with no button box", () => {
  const link = linkVariants()
  assert.match(link, /text-primary/)
  assert.match(link, /text-meta/)
  assert.match(link, /decoration-transparent/)
  assert.match(link, /hover:decoration-current/)
  assert.match(link, /focus-visible:decoration-current/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(link, /min-h-|rounded|border/)

test("safe action links look like a default Button but let long labels wrap", () => {

  assert.match(link, /border-control-border/)
  assert.match(link, /whitespace-normal/)
  assert.match(link, /md:min-h-10/)
  assert.ok(classList(link).includes("rounded-md"))
  for (const gone of ["whitespace-nowrap", "leading-snug", "md:h-10", "md:min-h-0"]) {
    assert.ok(!classList(link).includes(gone), `${gone} must be merged away`)
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Create `tests/button-busy-ui.test.ts`**

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict"
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs"
import test from "node:test"

// node --test cannot load .tsx, so these tests read the source of src/components/ui/button.tsx.
const button = () => readFileSync("src/components/ui/button.tsx", "utf8").replace(/\r\n/g, "\n")

test("Button still renders in server components: no client directive and no hooks", () => {
  const source = button()
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /^\s*["']use client["']/m)
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /\buse(State|Effect|LayoutEffect|Ref|Reducer|Context|Transition|Id)\b/)
})

test("busy cannot be combined with asChild", () => {
  assert.match(button(), /\(\{ asChild\?: false; busy\?: boolean \} \| \{ asChild: true; busy\?: never \}\)/)
  assert.match(button(), /const isBusy = busy && !asChild/)
})

test("a busy Button keeps focus: aria-disabled and a click guard, never the disabled attribute", () => {
  const source = button()
  assert.match(source, /aria-busy=\{isBusy \|\| ariaBusy\}/)
  assert.match(source, /aria-disabled=\{isBusy \|\| ariaDisabled\}/)
  assert.match(source, /data-busy=\{isBusy \? "" : undefined\}/)
  assert.match(source, /function preventBusyClick\(event: React\.MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>\) \{\n  event\.preventDefault\(\)\n\}/)
  assert.match(source, /onClick=\{isBusy \? preventBusyClick : onClick\}/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /(?<![\w-])disabled=\{/, "busy must not set the disabled attribute; a disabled button drops focus to <body>")
  const attributes = source.slice(source.indexOf("<Comp"), source.indexOf("</Comp>"))
  assert.ok(attributes.indexOf("{...props}") < attributes.indexOf("aria-disabled="), "the busy attributes must come after the caller's props")
  assert.ok(attributes.indexOf("{...props}") < attributes.indexOf("onClick="), "the click guard must come after the caller's props")
})

test("the busy spinner is decorative, stops under reduced motion, and takes the place of a leading icon", () => {
  const source = button()
  assert.match(source, /import \{ Loader2 \} from "lucide-react"/)
  assert.match(source, /<Loader2 key="button-spinner" data-slot="button-spinner" aria-hidden="true" className="animate-spin motion-reduce:animate-none" \/>/)
  assert.match(source, /first\.type !== React\.Fragment && \(typeof first\.type !== "string" \|\| first\.type === "svg"\)/)
  assert.match(source, /return leadsWithIcon \? \[spinner, \.\.\.rest\] : \[spinner, \.\.\.items\]/)
  assert.match(source, /\{isBusy \? withBusySpinner\(children\) : children\}/)
})

test("Button keeps its default type and data attributes", () => {
  const source = button()
  assert.match(source, /data-slot="button"/)
  assert.match(source, /data-variant=\{variant\}/)
  assert.match(source, /data-size=\{size\}/)
  assert.match(source, /type=\{asChild \? type : \(type \?\? "button"\)\}/)
  assert.match(source, /className=\{cn\(buttonVariants\(\{ variant, size \}\), className\)\}/)
})
```

```ts
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"

// Size strings live in constants so phase 2D can switch `default` and `icon` to 36px by editing one line each.
// Below md every size is at least 44px tall. Radius follows the mockup (spec I15): sm and icon-sm are small
// controls (rounded-control, 6px); md, lg, default and icon match the fields (rounded-md, 8px).
const sizeSm = "min-h-11 px-2.5 text-meta md:h-8 md:min-h-0 rounded-control"
const sizeMd = "min-h-11 px-3.5 text-sm md:h-9 md:min-h-0 rounded-md"
const sizeLg = "min-h-11 px-4 text-sm md:h-10 md:min-h-0 rounded-md"
const sizeIconSm = "min-h-11 min-w-11 md:size-8 md:min-h-0 md:min-w-0 rounded-control"
const sizeIcon = "min-h-11 min-w-11 md:size-10 md:min-h-0 md:min-w-0 rounded-md"

// Every variant draws a 1px border, so all variants share one box size. The disabled look wins over every
// variant and state because Tailwind emits hover:, active:, disabled: and aria-disabled: rules in that order.
// Nothing fades through transparency. aria-disabled covers busy buttons and asChild links, which cannot take a real disabled.
export const buttonVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex shrink-0 cursor-pointer items-center justify-center gap-1.5 border font-medium whitespace-nowrap transition-colors outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-2 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:border-border disabled:bg-background disabled:text-faint-foreground aria-disabled:pointer-events-none aria-disabled:border-border aria-disabled:bg-background aria-disabled:text-faint-foreground [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:shrink-0 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "border-primary bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:border-primary-hover hover:bg-primary-hover active:border-brand-navy active:bg-brand-navy",
        outline: "border-control-border bg-surface text-foreground hover:border-input hover:bg-background active:border-input active:bg-muted",
        ghost: "border-transparent bg-transparent text-muted-foreground hover:bg-background hover:text-foreground active:bg-muted",
        danger: "border-danger-border bg-surface text-danger hover:bg-danger-soft active:border-danger",
        destructive: "border-destructive bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground hover:bg-danger-hover hover:border-danger-hover",
        warning: "border-warning bg-warning text-warning-foreground hover:bg-warning-hover hover:border-warning-hover",
      },
      size: {
        sm: sizeSm,
        md: sizeMd,
        lg: sizeLg,
        default: sizeLg,
        "icon-sm": sizeIconSm,
        icon: sizeIcon,
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      size: "default",
    },
  },
)

export type ButtonVariantProps = VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants>

// Text links (mockup `.link`): underline appears on hover and keyboard focus. `meta` is for a stand-alone command
// link ("ล้างทั้งหมด"); `inherit` is for a link inside a sentence. No 44px box of its own: a stand-alone link on
// mobile adds `inline-flex min-h-11 items-center md:min-h-0` at the call site.
export const linkVariants = cva(
  "inline font-medium text-primary underline decoration-transparent decoration-1 underline-offset-3 transition-colors hover:decoration-current focus-visible:decoration-current",
  {
    variants: {
      size: {
        meta: "text-meta whitespace-nowrap",
        inherit: "",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      size: "meta",
    },
  },
)
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Replace `src/components/ui/button.tsx`**

Replace the whole file with:

```tsx
import * as React from "react"
import { Loader2 } from "lucide-react"
import { Slot } from "radix-ui"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { buttonVariants, type ButtonVariantProps } from "@/components/ui/button-variants"

// `busy` means "working on it". The button keeps keyboard focus (no disabled attribute), is announced as busy and
// unavailable, shows a spinner and ignores clicks. Slot cannot insert a spinner, so busy and asChild never mix.
type ButtonProps = React.ComponentProps<"button"> &
  ButtonVariantProps &
  ({ asChild?: false; busy?: boolean } | { asChild: true; busy?: never })

// Cancelling the click stops the caller's handler and a form submit. It also covers Enter in a form field, because
// implicit submission clicks the submit button. There is no hook, so Button still renders on the server; `busy`
// itself must come from client state, because a server file cannot hand this handler to a <button>.
function preventBusyClick(event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) {
  event.preventDefault()
}

// The spinner takes the place of a leading icon, so the width stays the same, or goes in front of a text label.
function withBusySpinner(children: React.ReactNode) {
  const spinner = (
    <Loader2 key="button-spinner" data-slot="button-spinner" aria-hidden="true" className="animate-spin motion-reduce:animate-none" />
  )
  const items = React.Children.toArray(children)
  const [first, ...rest] = items
  const leadsWithIcon =
    React.isValidElement(first) && first.type !== React.Fragment && (typeof first.type !== "string" || first.type === "svg")
  return leadsWithIcon ? [spinner, ...rest] : [spinner, ...items]
}

function Button({
  className,
  variant = "default",
  size = "default",
  asChild = false,
  busy = false,
  type,
  onClick,
  children,
  "aria-busy": ariaBusy,
  "aria-disabled": ariaDisabled,
  ...props
}: ButtonProps) {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot.Root : "button"
  const isBusy = busy && !asChild

  return (
    <Comp
      data-slot="button"
      data-variant={variant}
      data-size={size}
      type={asChild ? type : (type ?? "button")}
      className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size }), className)}
      {...props}
      data-busy={isBusy ? "" : undefined}
      aria-busy={isBusy || ariaBusy}
      aria-disabled={isBusy || ariaDisabled}
      onClick={isBusy ? preventBusyClick : onClick}
    >
      {isBusy ? withBusySpinner(children) : children}
    </Comp>
  )
}

export { Button, buttonVariants }
```

How the guard and the existing call sites behave:
- **The click guard** covers mouse clicks, Enter and Space on the focused button, and Enter in a form field. Enter in a field triggers implicit submission, which fires `click` on the submit button. `aria-disabled:pointer-events-none` also blocks the mouse.
- **When `busy` is false** the element gets the caller's `onClick`, `aria-busy` and `aria-disabled` unchanged.
- **With `asChild`**, `Slot` still composes the caller's `onClick` with the child's, as before.
- **`AlertDialogAction` and `AlertDialogCancel`** use `Pick<React.ComponentProps<typeof Button>, "variant" | "size">`. That still type-checks against the union props, and so does `ActionButton`'s `{...props}` (checked with `tsc` on a copy).

```ts
export function getSafeActionLinkClasses(variant: Extract<UiButtonVariant, "primary" | "secondary" | "ghost"> = "secondary") {
  const variantClass = {
    primary: "bg-primary text-white hover:bg-primary-hover",
    secondary: "border border-border bg-surface text-foreground hover:bg-accent",
    ghost: "text-foreground hover:bg-accent",
  }[variant]

  return `inline-flex min-h-11 w-full min-w-0 max-w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-center text-sm font-medium leading-snug transition-colors focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-2 sm:w-fit ${variantClass}`
}
```
→
```ts
// A link that looks like a default-size Button but lets a long label wrap and grow taller. cn() is required:
// Tailwind emits whitespace-normal before whitespace-nowrap, so appending the string would keep the nowrap.
// Phase 2D changes md:min-h-10 to md:min-h-9 when the default button becomes 36px.
export function getSafeActionLinkClasses(variant: Extract<UiButtonVariant, "primary" | "secondary" | "ghost"> = "secondary") {
  return cn(
    buttonVariants({ variant: legacyButtonVariant[variant] }),
    "h-auto w-full min-w-0 max-w-full whitespace-normal py-2 text-center sm:w-fit md:h-auto md:min-h-10",
  )
}
```

`legacyButtonVariant` is the existing `const` above `getActionButtonClasses`. Checked against tailwind-merge 3.5.0 with Task 1's `cn()`, `getSafeActionLinkClasses("secondary")` returns the outline button classes with these changes:
- `whitespace-nowrap`, `md:h-10` and `md:min-h-0` are removed.
- `min-h-11 px-4 text-sm rounded-md` stays.
- `h-auto w-full min-w-0 max-w-full whitespace-normal py-2 text-center sm:w-fit md:h-auto md:min-h-10` is added.

4. Delete the unused touch icon helper (0 callers in `src` and `tests`). Replace

```ts
export function getMobileSafeBottomPaddingClasses() {
  return "pb-[calc(6rem+max(0.75rem,env(safe-area-inset-bottom)))] sm:pb-0"
}

export function getTouchIconButtonClasses() {
  return "inline-flex min-h-11 min-w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-md transition-colors focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-2"
}
```
→
```ts
export function getMobileSafeBottomPaddingClasses() {
  return "pb-[calc(6rem+max(0.75rem,env(safe-area-inset-bottom)))] sm:pb-0"
}
```

Leave `uiButtonVariants`, `uiButtonSizes`, their types and `legacyButtonSize` as they are; `ActionButton` and `getActionButtonClasses` still use them.


### Task 7: Field primitives and wiring into getFieldControlClasses, SearchableSelect, ScannerTextInput, FileDropzone

Build the full set of field primitives (`fieldControlVariants`, `Input`, `NativeSelect`, `Textarea`, `Field`, `SearchField`, `checkboxVariants`) and wire them into the only four shared places spec §6.7 names: `getFieldControlClasses()` (10 fields in 6 files), `SearchableSelect` (29 call sites in 13 files, none edited), `ScannerTextInput` (5 call sites in 4 files) and `FileDropzone` (17 call sites in 14 files, none edited). Spec §6, I9, I10, I15. One commit.

Rulings this task applies (they override `06-fields.md`):
- **I15:** every field (`fieldControlVariants`, `Textarea`, `SearchField`, the `SearchableSelect` trigger, `FileDropzone`) uses `rounded-md` (8px). `06-fields.md` §6.4, §6.5 and §6.8 row 5 say `rounded-control`; that is superseded. Only the small clear buttons inside a field use `rounded-control`.
- **I9:** the native select is `NativeSelect` in `native-select.tsx`.
- **I10:** in `searchable-select.tsx` this task edits only the label, the trigger, the clear button, the chevron and the new hint and error lines. The list (`<PopoverContent>` through `</Popover>`) is Task 8's.
- **Error on the `SearchableSelect` trigger (ruled in this plan):** §6.7 says `error` sets `aria-invalid` on the trigger. The trigger is a `<button>`, and `aria-invalid` is not a supported property of role button: `npm run lint` reports `jsx-a11y/role-supports-aria-props` (a new warning in `src/`, checked by linting this exact code). This is the same reason §6.8 row 21 gives for leaving out `aria-required`. So the trigger shows the error look through classes (`border-danger ring-1 ring-danger shadow-field-error`; the base `focus:` and `aria-expanded:` rules still turn it teal while focused or open), and screen readers get the message through `aria-describedby` plus the `role="alert"` of `FieldError`.

**Files:**

Save this script as `t7-precheck.mjs` in your temp folder (never in the repo):

```js
// Task 7 precheck: run from the repo root. Confirms Tasks 1, 5 and 6 landed the names this task consumes. Never commit this file.
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs"
import { resolve } from "node:path"
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url"

const read = (path) => readFileSync(path, "utf8")
const css = read("src/app/globals.css")
const { buttonVariants } = await import(pathToFileURL(resolve("src/components/ui/button-variants.ts")).href)
const { cn } = await import(pathToFileURL(resolve("src/lib/utils.ts")).href)
const cameraButton = buttonVariants({ variant: "outline", size: "lg" })
const checks = {
  "Task 1: --color-faint-foreground": css.includes("--color-faint-foreground: var(--faint-foreground);"),
  "Task 1: --shadow-field-focus and --shadow-field-error": /--shadow-field-focus: 0 0 0 4px rgb\(/.test(css) && /--shadow-field-error: 0 0 0 4px rgb\(/.test(css),
  "Task 1: --text-meta": css.includes("--text-meta: 0.8125rem;"),
  "Task 1: @utility select-chevron": css.includes("@utility select-chevron {"),
  "Task 1: ::placeholder base rule": /::placeholder \{\s*color: var\(--faint-foreground\);/.test(css),
  "Task 1: cn() keeps text-meta next to a color": cn("text-meta text-faint-foreground") === "text-meta text-faint-foreground",
  "Task 5: dropzone hint is text-xs without leading-4": read("src/components/ui/file-dropzone.tsx").includes('<span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-muted-foreground">{tCommon("imageOptimizationHint")}</span>'),
  "Task 6: outline lg button is 44px below md, 40px from md, 8px corners": ["min-h-11", "md:h-10", "md:min-h-0", "rounded-md", "border-control-border"].every((name) => cameraButton.split(" ").includes(name)) && !/\bsm:/.test(cameraButton),
  "no field primitive exists yet": !["field-variants.ts", "input.tsx", "textarea.tsx", "native-select.tsx", "field.tsx", "search-field.tsx"].some((name) => {
    try { read(`src/components/ui/${name}`); return true } catch { return false }
  }),
}
let missing = 0
for (const [name, ok] of Object.entries(checks)) {
  console.log(`${ok ? "ok     " : "MISSING"} ${name}`)
  if (!ok) missing += 1
}
if (missing > 0) {
  console.error(`ABORT: ${missing} precondition(s) failed. Do not start Task 7; report which.`)
  process.exit(1)
}
console.log("Task 7 preconditions hold")
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing test `tests/field-primitives.test.ts`**

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict"
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs"
import test from "node:test"
import { checkboxVariants, fieldControlVariants, fieldReadOnlyClasses, textareaClasses } from "../src/components/ui/field-variants.ts"
import { getFieldControlClasses } from "../src/lib/design-system.ts"
import { cn } from "../src/lib/utils.ts"

const read = (path: string) => readFileSync(path, "utf8").replace(/\r\n/g, "\n")
const classList = (value: string) => value.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean)

test("fields share one look: input border, white fill, 8px corners and 44px below md", () => {
  const field = fieldControlVariants()
  for (const name of ["w-full", "min-w-0", "rounded-md", "border", "border-input", "bg-surface", "px-3", "text-foreground", "outline-none", "h-11", "text-base", "md:h-10", "md:text-sm", "motion-reduce:transition-none"]) {
    assert.ok(classList(field).includes(name), `fieldControlVariants() is missing ${name}`)
  }
  // Corners follow the mockup (spec I15): fields are 8px; rounded-control (6px) is for small controls only.
  assert.doesNotMatch(field, /opacity|hover:|read-only:|placeholder:|rounded-control|\bsm:/)
  assert.equal(fieldControlVariants({ fieldSize: "lg" }), field, "lg (40px) is the default size")
})

test("focus, error and disabled use the single ring color and no opacity", () => {
  const field = fieldControlVariants()
  assert.match(field, /focus:border-ring focus:ring-1 focus:ring-ring focus:shadow-field-focus/)
  assert.match(field, /aria-invalid:border-danger aria-invalid:ring-1 aria-invalid:ring-danger aria-invalid:shadow-field-error/)
  // aria-* comes after focus in Tailwind's variant order, so a focused invalid field needs this row to get the teal ring back.
  assert.match(field, /aria-invalid:focus:border-ring aria-invalid:focus:ring-ring aria-invalid:focus:shadow-field-focus/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(field, /focus:(?:border|ring)-danger/)
  assert.match(field, /disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:border-border disabled:bg-background disabled:text-faint-foreground/)
})

test("field sizes switch at md like the buttons, and the scan field keeps 16px text everywhere", () => {
  assert.match(fieldControlVariants({ fieldSize: "md" }), /\bh-11 text-base md:h-9 md:text-sm\b/)
  assert.match(fieldControlVariants({ fieldSize: "lg" }), /\bh-11 text-base md:h-10 md:text-sm\b/)
  const xl = fieldControlVariants({ fieldSize: "xl" })
  assert.match(xl, /\bh-12 text-base\b/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(xl, /md:text-sm|md:h-/, "iOS zooms into a field whose text is under 16px")
})

test("getFieldControlClasses is the field recipe, and callers change height through fieldSize", () => {
  assert.equal(getFieldControlClasses(), fieldControlVariants())
  assert.equal(getFieldControlClasses({ fieldSize: "xl" }), fieldControlVariants({ fieldSize: "xl" }))
  assert.doesNotMatch(getFieldControlClasses(), /select-chevron|read-only:/, "the helper also styles inputs and button triggers")
  // An h-12 override cannot beat md:h-10, which is why the audit scan field passes fieldSize "xl" instead.
  const scan = classList(cn(getFieldControlClasses({ fieldSize: "xl" }), "pl-9 pr-24"))
  for (const name of ["h-12", "text-base", "pl-9", "pr-24"]) assert.ok(scan.includes(name), `scan field is missing ${name}`)
  assert.deepEqual(scan.filter((name) => name.startsWith("md:")), [])
})

test("textarea, read-only and checkbox helpers", () => {
  assert.match(textareaClasses, /\bborder-input\b/)
  assert.match(textareaClasses, /\bbg-surface\b/)
  assert.match(textareaClasses, /\brounded-md\b/)
  assert.match(textareaClasses, /min-h-24 py-2 text-base md:text-sm resize-y$/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(textareaClasses, /\bh-1[0-2]\b|read-only:/)
  assert.equal(fieldReadOnlyClasses, "read-only:border-border read-only:bg-background")
  assert.equal(checkboxVariants(), "shrink-0 cursor-pointer disabled:cursor-not-allowed size-4")
  assert.match(checkboxVariants({ size: "lg" }), /\bsize-5$/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(checkboxVariants({ size: "lg" }), /outline-none|rounded|border/, "native boxes take accent-color and the base focus outline")
})

const primitiveFiles = ["field-variants.ts", "input.tsx", "textarea.tsx", "native-select.tsx", "field.tsx", "search-field.tsx"].map((name) => `src/components/ui/${name}`)

test("field primitives work in server components", () => {
  for (const path of primitiveFiles) {
    assert.doesNotMatch(read(path), /"use client"|\buse(?:State|Effect|LayoutEffect|Context)\b|\bcreateContext\b/, path)
  }
  assert.doesNotMatch(read("src/components/ui/field-variants.ts"), /from "@\//, "tests and design-system.ts import this file without the @/ alias")
})

test("Input and NativeSelect take fieldSize, never the HTML size attribute", () => {
  const input = read("src/components/ui/input.tsx")
  assert.match(input, /Omit<React\.ComponentProps<"input">, "size" \| "type"> & \{ fieldSize\?: FieldSize; type\?: InputType \}/)
  assert.match(input, /cn\(fieldControlVariants\(\{ fieldSize \}\), fieldReadOnlyClasses, /)
  assert.match(input, /\["number", "date", "datetime-local", "time"\]/)
  const select = read("src/components/ui/native-select.tsx")
  assert.match(select, /Omit<React\.ComponentProps<"select">, "size" \| "multiple"> & \{ fieldSize\?: FieldSize \}/)
  assert.match(select, /"select-chevron cursor-pointer pr-9"/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(select, /fieldReadOnlyClasses/, ":read-only always matches a <select>")
  assert.match(read("src/components/ui/textarea.tsx"), /cn\(textareaClasses, fieldReadOnlyClasses, className\)/)
})

test("Field wires label, hint and error to its control without starting browser validation", () => {
  const field = read("src/components/ui/field.tsx")
  assert.match(field, /htmlFor=\{controlId\}/)
  assert.match(field, /<span aria-hidden="true" className="ml-1 text-warning">\*<\/span>/)
  assert.match(field, /"aria-required": true/)
  assert.match(field, /"aria-invalid": true/)
  assert.match(field, /role="alert"/)
  assert.match(field, /\[element\?\.props\["aria-describedby"\], errorId, hintId\]/, "the error is read before the hint")
  assert.match(field, /React\.cloneElement\(element, control\)/)
  assert.match(field, /visuallyHidden && "sr-only"/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(field, /\brequired(?::|=)\s*(?:true|\{true\})/, "a real required attribute would start the browser's own validation")
})

test("SearchField hides the WebKit clear button and keeps its spinner still for reduced motion", () => {
  const source = read("src/components/ui/search-field.tsx")
  assert.match(source, /type = "search"/)
  assert.match(source, /"pl-9 \[&::-webkit-search-cancel-button\]:appearance-none"/)
  assert.match(source, /<Loader2 className="size-4 animate-spin motion-reduce:animate-none" \/>/)
  assert.match(source, /<button type="button" aria-label=\{clearLabel\} onClick=\{onClear\}/)
  assert.match(source, /trailingCount === 1 && "pr-12", trailingCount === 2 && "pr-24"/)
})

test("SearchableSelect uses the field look, a real label, and announces the chosen value", () => {
  const source = read("src/components/ui/searchable-select.tsx")
  assert.match(source, /<FieldLabel id=\{labelId\} htmlFor=\{triggerId\} required=\{required\}>/)
  const trigger = source.match(/fieldControlVariants\(\{ fieldSize \}\),\n\s*"([^"]+)"/)?.[1]
  assert.ok(trigger, "trigger class string not found")
  assert.match(trigger, /aria-expanded:border-ring aria-expanded:ring-1 aria-expanded:ring-ring aria-expanded:shadow-field-focus/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(trigger, /hover:/, "fields have no hover state")
  assert.match(source, /showClear \? "pr-24 md:pr-20" : "pr-9"/)
  assert.match(source, /aria-labelledby=\{label \? `\$\{labelId\} \$\{valueId\}` : undefined\}/)
  assert.match(source, /aria-describedby=\{describedBy\}/)
  // A button does not support aria-invalid (jsx-a11y/role-supports-aria-props), so the trigger gets the error look
  // from classes and screen readers get the message through aria-describedby and the alert role.
  assert.match(source, /error \? "border-danger ring-1 ring-danger shadow-field-error" : undefined/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /aria-invalid/)
  assert.match(source, /<ChevronDown className="pointer-events-none absolute right-2\.5 top-1\/2 size-4 -translate-y-1\/2 text-faint-foreground" aria-hidden="true" \/>/)
  assert.match(source, /<FieldHint id=\{hintId\}>/)
  assert.match(source, /<FieldError id=\{errorId\}>/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /ChevronsUpDown|disabled:bg-muted|sm:min-h-0|sm:pr-20|sm:w-10/)
})

test("FileDropzone shows keyboard focus and a disabled look without opacity", () => {
  const source = read("src/components/ui/file-dropzone.tsx")
  assert.match(source, /has-focus-visible:outline-2 has-focus-visible:outline-offset-2 has-focus-visible:outline-ring/)
  assert.match(source, /"border-input bg-surface hover:bg-background"/)
  assert.match(source, /"pointer-events-none border-border bg-background"/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /opacity-/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /rounded-md bg-primary-soft text-primary/, "no tinted icon tile")
  assert.equal(source.match(/"mt-1 block text-meta text-faint-foreground"/g)?.length, 2)
})
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Create `src/components/ui/field-variants.ts`**

```ts
import { cva } from "class-variance-authority"

// One look for every field: <input>, <select>, <textarea> and the SearchableSelect trigger (spec 2A section 6.4).
// Plain TS with no React and no "@/" imports, so tests and src/lib/design-system.ts can import this file directly.
export type FieldSize = "md" | "lg" | "xl"

// Corners are rounded-md (8px), like the mockup's .input (spec I15). Tailwind emits aria-* after focus, so without
// the aria-invalid:focus:* row a focused invalid field would get a red ring; that row brings back the one focus color.
// No read-only: here, because :read-only matches every <select> and <button>; Input and Textarea add
// fieldReadOnlyClasses themselves. No placeholder color: the ::placeholder base rule in globals.css sets it.
const fieldBase = [
  "w-full min-w-0 rounded-md border border-input bg-surface px-3 text-foreground outline-none",
  "transition-[color,background-color,border-color,box-shadow] motion-reduce:transition-none",
  "focus:border-ring focus:ring-1 focus:ring-ring focus:shadow-field-focus",
  "aria-invalid:border-danger aria-invalid:ring-1 aria-invalid:ring-danger aria-invalid:shadow-field-error",
  "aria-invalid:focus:border-ring aria-invalid:focus:ring-ring aria-invalid:focus:shadow-field-focus",
  "disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:border-border disabled:bg-background disabled:text-faint-foreground",
].join(" ")

// Same size names as the buttons, switching at md. Below md every size is at least 44px with 16px text, so iOS
// does not zoom when a field is tapped. xl (the audit scan search) stays 48px and 16px at every width.
// Change the height through fieldSize: a className like h-12 cannot beat md:h-10.
export const fieldControlVariants = cva(fieldBase, {
  variants: {
    fieldSize: {
      md: "h-11 text-base md:h-9 md:text-sm",
      lg: "h-11 text-base md:h-10 md:text-sm",
      xl: "h-12 text-base",
    },
  },
  defaultVariants: {
    fieldSize: "lg",
  },
})

export const textareaClasses = `${fieldBase} min-h-24 py-2 text-base md:text-sm resize-y`

// A read-only value stays readable and copyable: quiet box, normal text color, same focus ring.
export const fieldReadOnlyClasses = "read-only:border-border read-only:bg-background"

// Native checkbox and radio. Color comes from accent-color and focus from the base :focus-visible outline
// (phase 1), so no outline-none. The 44px hit area belongs to the <label> row around the box.
export const checkboxVariants = cva("shrink-0 cursor-pointer disabled:cursor-not-allowed", {
  variants: {
    size: {
      md: "size-4",
      lg: "size-5",
    },
  },
  defaultVariants: {
    size: "md",
  },
})
```

Do not write `focus:border-danger` or `aria-invalid:focus:ring-danger`: the focus guard (`tests/visual-foundation-guards.test.ts:11`) fails on any `focus:` before a `danger` ring or border.

- [ ] **Step 6: Create `input.tsx`, `textarea.tsx` and `native-select.tsx` in `src/components/ui/`**

`src/components/ui/input.tsx`:

```tsx
import * as React from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { fieldControlVariants, fieldReadOnlyClasses, type FieldSize } from "@/components/ui/field-variants"

// The types a one-line field takes. A plain union on purpose: Exclude<HTMLInputTypeAttribute, ...> keeps
// `string & {}` and would accept anything. checkbox, radio, file, hidden, range and color are not fields.
export type InputType = "text" | "search" | "email" | "password" | "tel" | "url" | "number" | "date" | "datetime-local" | "time" | "month"

// Digits in these types line up in columns (the num utility).
const numericInputTypes: ReadonlySet<InputType> = new Set(["number", "date", "datetime-local", "time"])

// No directive and no hooks, so server pages can render it. The size prop is fieldSize: <input size> is an HTML attribute.
function Input({
  className,
  fieldSize,
  type,
  ...props
}: Omit<React.ComponentProps<"input">, "size" | "type"> & { fieldSize?: FieldSize; type?: InputType }) {
  return (
    <input
      data-slot="input"
      type={type}
      className={cn(fieldControlVariants({ fieldSize }), fieldReadOnlyClasses, type !== undefined && numericInputTypes.has(type) && "num", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export { Input }
```

`src/components/ui/textarea.tsx`:

```tsx
import * as React from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { fieldReadOnlyClasses, textareaClasses } from "@/components/ui/field-variants"

// Server-safe. Callers set a taller box with min-h-28 / min-h-32 or rows.
function Textarea({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"textarea">) {
  return <textarea data-slot="textarea" className={cn(textareaClasses, fieldReadOnlyClasses, className)} {...props} />
}

export { Textarea }
```

`src/components/ui/native-select.tsx`:

```tsx
import * as React from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { fieldControlVariants, type FieldSize } from "@/components/ui/field-variants"

// The browser's own <select> with the field look. Named NativeSelect because four forms already declare a local
// `function Select` (spec I9). select-chevron draws the arrow (the system arrow returns in forced-colors mode) and
// pr-9 leaves room for it. No read-only look: :read-only always matches a <select>. Server-safe.
function NativeSelect({
  className,
  fieldSize,
  ...props
}: Omit<React.ComponentProps<"select">, "size" | "multiple"> & { fieldSize?: FieldSize }) {
  return <select data-slot="native-select" className={cn(fieldControlVariants({ fieldSize }), "select-chevron cursor-pointer pr-9", className)} {...props} />
}

export { NativeSelect }
```

`disabled:cursor-not-allowed` beats `cursor-pointer` on a disabled select (Tailwind emits it later and with higher specificity; Step 13 checks the order).

- [ ] **Step 7: Create `src/components/ui/field.tsx`**

```tsx
import * as React from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

// What Field hands to its control. aria-required, never required: a real required attribute would start the
// browser's own form validation and could block submits that our code validates itself.
export type FieldControlProps = {
  id: string
  "aria-describedby"?: string
  "aria-invalid"?: true
  "aria-required"?: true
}

type FieldProps = {
  label: React.ReactNode
  hideLabel?: boolean
  required?: boolean
  hint?: React.ReactNode
  error?: React.ReactNode
  id?: string
  className?: string
  // One element that accepts id and aria-* (Input, NativeSelect, Textarea, ScannerTextInput), or a function for a
  // control built from several parts. Never wrap SearchableSelect: it renders its own label.
  children: React.ReactElement<{ id?: string; "aria-describedby"?: string }> | ((control: FieldControlProps) => React.ReactNode)
}

// Label, control, hint, error. Server-safe: useId is the only hook and there is no context. Never put a Field
// inside a <label>.
function Field({ label, hideLabel, required, hint, error, id, className, children }: FieldProps) {
  const generatedId = React.useId()
  const renderControl = typeof children === "function" ? children : null
  const element = typeof children === "function" ? null : children
  const controlId = element?.props.id ?? id ?? generatedId
  const hintId = hint ? `${controlId}-hint` : undefined
  const errorId = error ? `${controlId}-error` : undefined
  const describedBy = [element?.props["aria-describedby"], errorId, hintId].filter(Boolean).join(" ")
  const control: FieldControlProps = {
    id: controlId,
    ...(describedBy ? { "aria-describedby": describedBy } : {}),
    ...(error ? { "aria-invalid": true } : {}),
    ...(required ? { "aria-required": true } : {}),
  }

  return (
    <div data-slot="field" className={cn("grid min-w-0 gap-1.5", className)}>
      <FieldLabel htmlFor={controlId} required={required} visuallyHidden={hideLabel}>
        {label}
      </FieldLabel>
      {renderControl ? renderControl(control) : element ? React.cloneElement(element, control) : null}
      {hint ? <FieldHint id={hintId}>{hint}</FieldHint> : null}
      {error ? <FieldError id={errorId}>{error}</FieldError> : null}
    </div>
  )
}

// visuallyHidden, not hidden: hidden is an HTML attribute that would remove the label for screen readers too.
function FieldLabel({
  className,
  required,
  visuallyHidden,
  children,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"label"> & { required?: boolean; visuallyHidden?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <label data-slot="field-label" className={cn("text-meta font-medium text-foreground", visuallyHidden && "sr-only", className)} {...props}>
      {children}
      {required ? <span aria-hidden="true" className="ml-1 text-warning">*</span> : null}
    </label>
  )
}

function FieldHint({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"p">) {
  return <p data-slot="field-hint" className={cn("text-meta text-faint-foreground", className)} {...props} />
}

function FieldError({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"p">) {
  return <p data-slot="field-error" role="alert" className={cn("text-meta text-danger", className)} {...props} />
}

export { Field, FieldError, FieldHint, FieldLabel }
```

Rendered on the server (checked with `renderToStaticMarkup`), `<Field label="ชื่อ" required hint="…" error="…"><Input id="name" aria-describedby="outer" /></Field>` gives the input `id="name" aria-describedby="outer name-error name-hint" aria-invalid="true" aria-required="true"` and no `required` attribute.

- [ ] **Step 8: Create `src/components/ui/search-field.tsx`**

```tsx
import * as React from "react"
import { Loader2, Search, X } from "lucide-react"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import type { FieldSize } from "@/components/ui/field-variants"

type SearchFieldProps = Omit<React.ComponentProps<"input">, "size" | "type"> & {
  type?: "search" | "text"
  fieldSize?: FieldSize
  loading?: boolean
  // At most one extra icon button, 44px wide or less (the audit scan camera button, for example).
  trailing?: React.ReactNode
} & ({ onClear?: undefined; clearLabel?: undefined } | { onClear: () => void; clearLabel: string })

// A search box: icon laid over an Input with pl-9, so border, focus, error and disabled stay on one element.
// className goes on the wrapper (layout); every other prop goes to the Input. Server pages may use it without
// onClear (a server page cannot pass a function). The WebKit clear button is always hidden so every browser matches.
function SearchField({ className, type = "search", fieldSize, loading = false, trailing, onClear, clearLabel, value, ...props }: SearchFieldProps) {
  const showClear = onClear !== undefined && value !== undefined && String(value) !== ""
  const trailingCount = (showClear ? 1 : 0) + (trailing ? 1 : 0)

  return (
    <div data-slot="search-field" className={cn("relative min-w-0", className)}>
      <span className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-y-0 left-3 flex items-center text-faint-foreground" aria-hidden="true">
        {loading ? <Loader2 className="size-4 animate-spin motion-reduce:animate-none" /> : <Search className="size-4" />}
      </span>
      <Input
        type={type}
        fieldSize={fieldSize}
        value={value}
        className={cn("pl-9 [&::-webkit-search-cancel-button]:appearance-none", trailingCount === 1 && "pr-12", trailingCount === 2 && "pr-24")}
        {...props}
      />
      {trailingCount > 0 ? (
        <div className="absolute inset-y-0 right-0.5 flex items-center gap-0.5">
          {showClear ? (
            <button type="button" aria-label={clearLabel} onClick={onClear} className="inline-flex h-full w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-faint-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring">
              <X className="size-4" aria-hidden="true" />
            </button>
          ) : null}
          {trailing}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  )
}

export { SearchField }
```

2A has no caller of `SearchField`, `Textarea` or `checkboxVariants` (spec appendix A); their first users come in 2B and 2C.

In the same file replace:

```ts
export function getFieldControlClasses() {
  return "min-h-11 w-full rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-ring focus:ring-1 focus:ring-ring sm:h-10 sm:min-h-0"
}
```

with:

```ts
// The field recipe as a class string, for elements that cannot use a primitive (a <button> trigger, a <select>
// that keeps its own markup). Change the height with fieldSize, not a className. <select> callers get no chevron here.
export function getFieldControlClasses(options?: { fieldSize?: FieldSize }) {
  return fieldControlVariants(options)
}
```

The 10 fields in 6 files that call it (`asset-register-toolbar.tsx:31` ×3, `asset-register-filter-sheet.tsx:219`, `asset-register-search-field.tsx:104`, `audit-scan-room-picker.tsx:81, :89`, `audit-scan-search.tsx:73`, `report-filter-panel.tsx:65, :106`) now get the white fill, the halo, the error and disabled looks, and 44px below `md` (it switched at `sm` before). `tests/design-system.test.ts:35, :37` (`focus:border-ring`, `border-input`) keep passing.

→ `          className={cn(getFieldControlClasses({ fieldSize: "xl" }), "pl-9 pr-24")}`

This change is required: with the old className, tailwind-merge keeps the new `md:h-10 md:text-sm`, which beats `sm:h-12`, and the scan field would shrink to 40px / 14px at 768px and wider.

Then save this script as `t7-css-check.mjs` in your temp folder and run it from the repo root. It catches the silent failure where Tailwind emits no CSS for a misspelled class or a missing token:

```js
// Task 7 CSS check: run from the repo root after Step 12. Compiles src/app/globals.css in memory and confirms every
// class the field primitives write produces CSS, and that the overrides land after the base classes. Never commit this file.
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs"
import { createRequire } from "node:module"
import { resolve } from "node:path"
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url"

const load = (path) => import(pathToFileURL(resolve(path)).href)
const { checkboxVariants, fieldControlVariants, fieldReadOnlyClasses, textareaClasses } = await load("src/components/ui/field-variants.ts")
const require = createRequire(resolve("package.json"))
const { compile } = require("@tailwindcss/node")
const compiler = await compile(readFileSync("src/app/globals.css", "utf8"), { base: resolve("src/app"), onDependency() {} })

const sources = [
  fieldControlVariants({ fieldSize: "md" }),
  fieldControlVariants({ fieldSize: "lg" }),
  fieldControlVariants({ fieldSize: "xl" }),
  textareaClasses,
  fieldReadOnlyClasses,
  checkboxVariants({ size: "lg" }),
  // NativeSelect, SearchField, SearchableSelect trigger and clear button, Field parts
  "select-chevron cursor-pointer pr-9 num sm:flex-1 pl-9 pr-12 pr-24 md:pr-20 [&::-webkit-search-cancel-button]:appearance-none",
  "flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-left aria-expanded:border-ring aria-expanded:ring-1 aria-expanded:ring-ring aria-expanded:shadow-field-focus border-danger ring-1 ring-danger shadow-field-error",
  "rounded-control md:min-h-0 md:w-10 right-2.5 size-4 text-faint-foreground animate-spin motion-reduce:animate-none",
  "grid gap-1.5 text-meta font-medium text-foreground sr-only ml-1 text-warning text-danger",
  // FileDropzone
  "has-focus-visible:outline-2 has-focus-visible:outline-offset-2 has-focus-visible:outline-ring border-dashed hover:bg-background size-5",
]
const classes = [...new Set(sources.flatMap((value) => value.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean)))]
const css = compiler.build(classes)
const escape = (name) => name.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, (char) => `\\${char}`)
const at = (name) => css.indexOf(`.${escape(name)}`)
const missing = classes.filter((name) => at(name) === -1)
let failures = missing.length
if (missing.length > 0) console.error("MISSING CSS:", missing.join(" "))
for (const [before, after] of [["px-3", "pr-9"], ["px-3", "pl-9"], ["pr-24", "md:pr-20"], ["cursor-pointer", "disabled:cursor-not-allowed"], ["read-only:border-border", "focus:border-ring"], ["focus:border-ring", "disabled:border-border"]]) {
  const ok = at(before) !== -1 && at(before) < at(after)
  if (!ok) failures += 1
  console.log(`${ok ? "ok   " : "WRONG"} ${before} before ${after}`)
}
if (failures > 0) {
  console.error(`ABORT: ${failures} problem(s). A Task 1 token is missing or a class name is misspelled.`)
  process.exit(1)
}
console.log(`all ${classes.length} field classes compile`)
```

Run: `node <temp>/t7-css-check.mjs`
Expected: 6 `ok` lines and `all 83 field classes compile` (plus the harmless `MODULE_TYPELESS_PACKAGE_JSON` warning).

### Task 8: Menus, popover, command, Kbd, global search and the topbar menus

The dropdown, popover and command primitives take the mockup's menu look (12px panel, 36/44px rows, `primary-soft` highlight, inner focus line, fade-and-slide motion that stops under reduced motion). A menu item can be `unavailable`: still reachable by arrow keys and read with its reason, but it does nothing. `Kbd` is new and first used in the global-search footer. The language menu becomes a radio group with each language named in its own language and no flag, and the five English `aria-label`/`title` values in the topbar and sidebar move to messages. Spec §5 with rulings I6, I7, I8, I10, I14, I15 and I20. One commit.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/components/ui/kbd.tsx`, `tests/menu-primitives-ui.test.ts`
- Replace (whole file): `src/components/ui/dropdown-menu.tsx`, `src/components/ui/command.tsx`
- Modify: `src/components/ui/popover.tsx` (the `PopoverContent` class string, line 32)
- Modify: `src/components/ui/searchable-select.tsx` (the list rows only, lines 87-95; I10)
- Modify: `src/components/layout/global-search.tsx` (imports and the results panel, lines 187-234)
- Modify: `src/components/layout/topbar.tsx` (imports, three constants, lines 130, 139-140, 236-251, 261, 271-274), `src/components/layout/sidebar.tsx` (line 246)
- Modify: `src/components/assets/asset-register-row-actions.tsx`, `asset-register-column-picker.tsx`, `asset-register-sort-menu.tsx`, `asset-register-export-menu.tsx` (menu callers)
- Modify: `messages/th.json`, `messages/en.json` (only through `scripts/messages-edit.mjs`)
- Modify: `tests/visual-foundation-guards.test.ts` (the overlay test at lines 63-67, and one appended test), `tests/dropdown-menus-ui.test.ts`, `tests/global-search-ui.test.ts`
- Leave alone: `src/components/assets/asset-detail-action-menu.tsx` (keeps `w-72`), the menu trigger buttons (2D moves them to `Button`), the notification popover's inner list (2D), `tests/design-tokens-contrast.test.ts` (Task 1 already holds every pair this task needs: faint on `popover`/`primary-soft`, ring on `primary-soft`/`danger-soft`, input on `popover`; I4), `DESIGN.md` (Task 13, I19).

Every file above is CRLF (`git ls-files --eol <file>` prints `w/crlf`). Edit with exact-string replacement; Step 9 converts the files you write whole.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 1: the tokens behind `rounded-control`, `text-meta`, `text-faint-foreground`, `bg-border-soft` and `focus-within:shadow-field-focus`; `cn()` built on `extendTailwindMerge`, so `cn("text-meta text-faint-foreground", className)` keeps both classes; the `::placeholder` base rule; and Task 1's guard that no class string holds both `bg-(success|warning|danger|info)-soft` and `text-faint-foreground`. That guard is why the destructive item rules live in their own string. Task 1 also removed ` placeholder:text-muted-foreground` from `command.tsx`; this task replaces that file whole.
  - Task 2 (I6): `dropdown-menu.tsx:76` now holds `data-[variant=destructive]:focus:bg-danger-soft`. This task replaces the whole file, so that line is overwritten. The R1 guard (`TONE_OPACITY`) must stay green: write no `-{tone}/NN` class.
  - Task 3 (R7d): `global-search.tsx:202` now reads `selected ? "bg-accent" : "hover:bg-row-hover",`. Step 12 quotes that text.
  - Task 5: the notification count at `topbar.tsx:172` reads `text-xs font-semibold leading-none`. This task does not touch that line.
  - Task 7 (I10): Task 7 rewrote the `searchable-select.tsx` label, trigger, hint and clear button. It left the list rows alone, so the lines Step 11 quotes are still the `081d538` text. `tests/searchable-select-accessibility.test.ts` and Task 7's `tests/field-primitives.test.ts` must still pass.
  - Task 9 comes later and deletes `size="xs"` from `<StatusBadge size="xs" tone="neutral" label={result.badge.label} />` in `global-search.tsx` (its codemod counts 52 attributes in 19 files). Keep that tag byte for byte.
  - In `tests/visual-foundation-guards.test.ts`: the module-level `sources`, `read` and `findMatches` (already imported).
- Produces:
  - `DropdownMenuItem` with `unavailable?: boolean`. The type is `{ unavailable?: false } | { unavailable: true; asChild?: never; onSelect?: never }`. It sets `aria-disabled` and `data-unavailable` and cancels `onSelect`, but never passes `disabled` to Radix. `DropdownMenuItemText` and `DropdownMenuItemDescription` are exported next to it. 2B's RowActions must keep using all three.
  - `Kbd` (`src/components/ui/kbd.tsx`). It has no client directive, so server components can use it.
  - Message keys `nav.openMenu`, `nav.closeMenu`, `nav.toggleSidebar`, `nav.languageMenu` (`{code}`), `nav.userMenu` (`{name}`), `globalSearch.keyboardSelect`, `globalSearch.keyboardOpen` and `globalSearch.keyboardClose`. `globalSearch.keyboardHint` is deleted.
  - Guards: the popover and dropdown panels match `/shadow-overlay[^"]*motion-reduce:animate-none!/` and never zoom. No emoji appears in `src/**/*.{ts,tsx}` or `messages/*.json`.
  - `CommandDialog` is gone (it had 0 users). Phase 3 builds Ctrl K fresh, with a Thai title.

Spec points this task settles (the task wins over `05-menus.md` where they differ):
- **Unavailable items cancel `onSelect`, not `onClick`** (§5.4 says `onClick`). Both keep the menu open: Radix (`@radix-ui/react-menu` 2.1.25, `handleSelect` in `dist/index.mjs:405-417`) closes only when the select event is not cancelled. Cancelling `onSelect` also resets Radix's pointer-down flag. This matches the plan header's Review Focus 5 and the `onSelect?: never` in the type.
- **Corners (I15 follows the real mockup):**
  - Menu panels, popovers and the command panel use `rounded-lg` (12px).
  - Menu rows, command rows, global-search rows and the search box inside a menu use `rounded-control` (6px). In `base.css`, `.menu-item` (:422) and `.menu-search` (:437) use `--r-sm`, which is 6px.
  - The in-menu search box is part of the menu, not a form field, so the "every field is `rounded-md`" part of I15 does not apply to it.
- **No hover fill on global-search rows** (§5.9): `onMouseEnter` already moves the selection, so a hover fill would light two rows. The row loses `transition-colors`, so it highlights at once, like a command row.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write `tests/menu-primitives-ui.test.ts`**

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict"
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs"
import test from "node:test"

const read = (path: string) => readFileSync(path, "utf8").replace(/\r\n/g, "\n")
const dropdown = () => read("src/components/ui/dropdown-menu.tsx")
const command = () => read("src/components/ui/command.tsx")

// The classes of a named class-string constant, such as `const menuItemClasses = "…"`.
function classesOf(source: string, name: string): Set<string> {
  const value = source.match(new RegExp(`const ${name} =\\s*"([^"]+)"`))?.[1]
  assert.ok(value, `${name} not found`)
  return new Set(value.split(" "))
}

function assertHasClasses(classes: Set<string>, expected: string[], label: string) {
  assert.deepEqual(expected.filter((name) => !classes.has(name)), [], `${label} is missing classes`)
}

// The source of one top-level function, up to the line that holds only its closing brace.
function functionSource(source: string, name: string): string {
  const body = source.match(new RegExp(`function ${name}\\([\\s\\S]*?\\n\\}\\n`))?.[0]
  assert.ok(body, `function ${name} not found`)
  return body
}

test("the tokens the menus use exist, so Tailwind generates their CSS", () => {
  // Without them rounded-control, text-meta, text-faint-foreground and bg-border-soft produce no CSS, silently.
  const css = read("src/app/globals.css")
  assert.match(css, /--radius-control: 6px;/)
  assert.match(css, /--text-meta: 0\.8125rem;/)
  assert.match(css, /--color-faint-foreground: var\(--faint-foreground\);/)
  assert.match(css, /--color-border-soft: var\(--border-soft\);/)
})

test("menu panels have 12px corners, 6px padding, a 240px minimum and fade-and-slide motion", () => {
  const source = dropdown()
  assertHasClasses(
    classesOf(source, "menuPanelClasses"),
    [
      "grid",
      "gap-px",
      "min-w-60",
      "rounded-lg",
      "border",
      "bg-popover",
      "p-1.5",
      "shadow-overlay",
      "outline-none",
      "duration-160",
      "ease-out",
      "data-[state=open]:fade-in-0",
      "data-[side=bottom]:slide-in-from-top-1",
      "data-[side=top]:slide-in-from-bottom-1",
      "data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0",
      "data-[state=closed]:duration-100",
      "motion-reduce:animate-none!",
    ],
    "menuPanelClasses",
  )
  assert.equal(source.match(/className=\{cn\(menuPanelClasses, className\)\}/g)?.length, 2, "DropdownMenuContent and DropdownMenuSubContent share the panel")
})

test("menu items are 36px from md up and 44px below, highlight with primary-soft and draw focus inside the row", () => {
  const source = dropdown()
  const item = classesOf(source, "menuItemClasses")
  assertHasClasses(
    item,
    [
      "min-h-11",
      "md:min-h-9",
      "cursor-pointer",
      "gap-2.5",
      "rounded-control",
      "px-2.5",
      "py-1.5",
      "text-sm",
      "transition-colors",
      "focus:bg-primary-soft",
      "focus:duration-0",
      "focus-visible:-outline-offset-2",
      "active:bg-muted",
      "aria-disabled:cursor-not-allowed",
      "aria-disabled:bg-transparent",
      "aria-disabled:text-faint-foreground",
      "aria-disabled:*:[svg]:text-faint-foreground!",
    ],
    "menuItemClasses",
  )
  assert.equal(item.has("outline-hidden"), false, "the base focus outline must show on keyboard focus")
  assertHasClasses(
    classesOf(source, "menuItemDestructiveClasses"),
    [
      "data-[variant=destructive]:not-aria-disabled:text-danger",
      "data-[variant=destructive]:not-aria-disabled:focus:bg-danger-soft",
      "data-[variant=destructive]:not-aria-disabled:*:[svg]:text-danger!",
    ],
    "menuItemDestructiveClasses",
  )
  assert.match(functionSource(source, "DropdownMenuItem"), /className=\{cn\(menuItemClasses, menuItemDestructiveClasses, className\)\}/)
  for (const name of ["DropdownMenuCheckboxItem", "DropdownMenuRadioItem", "DropdownMenuSubTrigger"]) {
    assert.match(functionSource(source, name), /className=\{cn\(menuItemClasses, /, name)
  }
})

test("an unavailable item stays focusable, says why, and cannot be chosen", () => {
  const source = dropdown()
  assert.match(
    source,
    /const unavailableItemProps = \{\s*"aria-disabled": true,\s*"data-unavailable": "",\s*onSelect: \(event: Event\) => event\.preventDefault\(\),\s*\} as const/,
  )
  assert.match(source, /\| \{ unavailable: true; asChild\?: never; onSelect\?: never \}/, "the type forbids asChild and onSelect")
  const item = functionSource(source, "DropdownMenuItem")
  assert.match(item, /\{\.\.\.props\}\s*\{\.\.\.\(unavailable \? unavailableItemProps : \{\}\)\}/, "the unavailable props come after the caller's props")
  assert.doesNotMatch(item, /data-disabled|\bdisabled[=:]/, "Radix skips disabled items for arrow keys and typeahead")
  assert.match(functionSource(source, "DropdownMenuItemText"), /"grid min-w-0 flex-1"/)
  assert.match(functionSource(source, "DropdownMenuItemDescription"), /"text-meta text-faint-foreground"/)
  const exported = source.slice(source.lastIndexOf("export {"))
  for (const name of ["DropdownMenuItemText", "DropdownMenuItemDescription"]) {
    assert.match(exported, new RegExp(`\\b${name},`), name)
  }
})

test("check boxes sit in front of the text and the chosen radio has a check at the end", () => {
  const source = dropdown()
  const checkbox = functionSource(source, "DropdownMenuCheckboxItem")
  assert.match(checkbox, /"flex size-4 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-sm border-\[1\.5px\]"/)
  assert.match(checkbox, /!on && "border-input bg-surface"/)
  assert.match(checkbox, /on && !disabled && "border-primary bg-primary"/)
  assert.match(checkbox, /on && disabled && "border-border bg-muted"/)
  assert.match(checkbox, /<CheckIcon strokeWidth=\{3\} className=\{cn\("size-3", disabled \? "text-muted-foreground" : "text-primary-foreground"\)\} \/>/)
  const radio = functionSource(source, "DropdownMenuRadioItem")
  assert.match(radio, /\{children\}\s*<DropdownMenuPrimitive\.ItemIndicator className="ml-auto flex">\s*<CheckIcon className="size-4 text-primary" \/>/)
  for (const body of [checkbox, radio]) {
    assert.match(body, /"data-\[state=checked\]:font-medium"/)
    assert.doesNotMatch(body, /absolute|pl-8/)
  }
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /CircleIcon/)
})

test("labels, separators and trailing text follow the mockup", () => {
  const source = dropdown()
  assert.match(functionSource(source, "DropdownMenuLabel"), /"px-2\.5 pt-1\.5 pb-1 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground data-\[inset\]:pl-9"/)
  assert.match(functionSource(source, "DropdownMenuSeparator"), /"mx-1 my-\[5px\] h-px bg-border-soft"/)
  assert.match(functionSource(source, "DropdownMenuShortcut"), /"ml-auto pl-3 whitespace-nowrap text-meta text-faint-foreground"/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /tracking-widest/)
})

test("menu, popover and command primitives use no opacity, zoom, see-through tint or the old sm breakpoint", () => {
  for (const path of ["src/components/ui/dropdown-menu.tsx", "src/components/ui/popover.tsx", "src/components/ui/command.tsx"]) {
    const source = read(path)
    assert.doesNotMatch(source, /opacity-50|zoom-(?:in|out)|sm:min-h-9|(?:bg|text|border)-[a-z-]+\/\d+/, path)
  }
  assert.match(read("src/components/ui/popover.tsx"), /rounded-lg border bg-popover p-4 [^"]*shadow-overlay [^"]*motion-reduce:animate-none!/)
})

test("the command panel matches the menus, and its search box is 16px below md with the shared placeholder color", () => {
  const source = command()
  assert.match(functionSource(source, "Command"), /"flex h-full w-full flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-popover text-popover-foreground"/)
  const input = functionSource(source, "CommandInput")
  assert.match(input, /"mx-1\.5 mt-1\.5 mb-1 flex h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-control border border-border bg-background px-2\.5 focus-within:border-ring focus-within:ring-1 focus-within:ring-ring focus-within:shadow-field-focus md:h-9"/)
  assert.match(input, /<SearchIcon className="size-4 shrink-0 text-faint-foreground" \/>/)
  assert.match(input, /\btext-base\b[^"]*\bmd:text-sm\b/, "iOS Safari zooms into fields under 16px (I8)")
  assert.doesNotMatch(input, /placeholder:/, "the ::placeholder base rule colors the placeholder (I7)")
  const item = functionSource(source, "CommandItem")
  assert.match(item, /min-h-11 [^"]*md:min-h-9/)
  assert.match(item, /data-\[selected=true\]:bg-primary-soft/)
  assert.match(item, /data-\[disabled=true\]:text-faint-foreground/)
  assert.match(functionSource(source, "CommandEmpty"), /"px-2\.5 py-4 text-center text-meta text-faint-foreground"/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /CommandDialog|Command Palette|components\/ui\/dialog/)
})

test("Kbd works in server components and uses the sans font", () => {
  assert.ok(existsSync("src/components/ui/kbd.tsx"))
  const source = read("src/components/ui/kbd.tsx")
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /"use client"/)
  assert.match(source, /export function Kbd\(\{ className, \.\.\.props \}: React\.ComponentProps<"kbd">\)/)
  assert.match(source, /"inline-flex h-5 items-center rounded-sm border border-border bg-surface px-1\.5 font-sans text-xs leading-4 font-medium whitespace-nowrap text-muted-foreground"/)
})

test("searchable select marks the chosen option with weight and a trailing check", () => {
  const source = read("src/components/ui/searchable-select.tsx")
  assert.match(source, /className=\{option\.id === value \? "font-medium" : undefined\}/)
  assert.match(source, /<span className="min-w-0 truncate">\{option\.label\}<\/span>\s*\{option\.id === value \? <Check className="ml-auto size-4 text-primary" aria-hidden="true" \/> : null\}/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /text-transparent/)
})
```

The first test passes already (Task 1 added the tokens). It is there so a later reorder cannot land the menus before their tokens: without the tokens these classes produce no CSS, and nothing reports an error (spec §5.14).

```ts
test("no emoji in source or messages: icons come from lucide and languages are named in words", () => {
  // Emoji_Presentation covers flags and pictographs; U+FE0F turns a text symbol into an emoji. © ® ✓ ↑ ↓ · › ⋯ stay allowed.
  const messages = ["messages/th.json", "messages/en.json"].map((path) => ({ path, source: read(path) }))
  assert.deepEqual(findMatches([...sources, ...messages], /\p{Emoji_Presentation}|\uFE0F/gu), [])
})
```

`Emoji_Presentation` also matches each half of a flag, so on the current code the guard reports 4 characters on 2 lines (`topbar.tsx:246, 249`). It does not match © ® ✓ ↑ ↓ · › ⋯, which the app uses as text (checked with Node 24). There are only two message files, so they are listed by name, and the file needs no new `node:fs` import.

```ts
test("topbar language and user menus are Radix dropdowns", () => {
  const source = read("src/components/layout/topbar.tsx")
  assert.ok((source.match(/<DropdownMenu\b/g)?.length ?? 0) >= 2)
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /langMenuOpen|userMenuOpen/)
  // The language menu is a radio group; each language is named in its own language, with no flag.
  assert.match(source, /<DropdownMenuRadioGroup value=\{locale\}/)
  assert.match(source, /<span lang="th">ภาษาไทย<\/span>/)
  assert.match(source, /<span lang="en">English<\/span>/)
  // Accessible names come from messages and contain the text shown on the button (WCAG 2.5.3).
  assert.match(source, /aria-label=\{tNav\("languageMenu", \{ code: localeCode \}\)\}/)
  assert.match(source, /aria-label=\{tNav\("userMenu", \{ name: userDisplayLabel \}\)\}/)
  for (const path of ["src/components/layout/topbar.tsx", "src/components/layout/sidebar.tsx"]) {
    assert.doesNotMatch(read(path), /(?:aria-label|title)="[A-Z]/, path)
  }
  for (const locale of ["th", "en"] as const) {
    const nav = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`messages/${locale}.json`, "utf8")).nav
    for (const key of ["openMenu", "closeMenu", "toggleSidebar", "languageMenu", "userMenu"]) {
      assert.equal(typeof nav[key], "string", `${locale} nav.${key}`)
    }
    assert.match(nav.languageMenu, /\{code\}/, locale)
    assert.match(nav.userMenu, /\{name\}/, locale)
  }
})
```

`<DropdownMenuRadioGroup` does not count toward `<DropdownMenu\b` (there is no word boundary inside the name), and the topbar still has its two `<DropdownMenu>` roots.
