- [ ] **Step 6: Replace `src/components/ui/dropdown-menu.tsx`**

Replace the whole file with:

```tsx
"use client"

import * as React from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { CheckIcon, ChevronRightIcon } from "lucide-react"
import { DropdownMenu as DropdownMenuPrimitive } from "radix-ui"

// Floating panel (mockup .menu): 12px corners, 6px padding, at least 240px wide, 1px between items.
// Opens with a 160ms fade and a 4px slide, closes with a 100ms fade, never zooms, and stays still under reduced motion.
const menuPanelClasses =
  "z-50 grid max-h-(--radix-dropdown-menu-content-available-height) min-w-60 origin-(--radix-dropdown-menu-content-transform-origin) gap-px overflow-x-hidden overflow-y-auto rounded-lg border bg-popover p-1.5 text-popover-foreground shadow-overlay outline-none duration-160 ease-out data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=open]:fade-in-0 data-[side=bottom]:slide-in-from-top-1 data-[side=top]:slide-in-from-bottom-1 data-[side=left]:slide-in-from-right-1 data-[side=right]:slide-in-from-left-1 data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0 data-[state=closed]:duration-100 motion-reduce:animate-none!"

// One row (mockup .menu-item): 36px from md up, 44px below. The highlight shows at once and fades out in 150ms.
// Keyboard focus draws the base outline inside the row. An item that cannot be used (aria-disabled, set by Radix for
// disabled items and by `unavailable`) is faint with no fill; no opacity, so its reason line stays readable.
const menuItemClasses =
  "relative flex min-h-11 cursor-pointer select-none items-center gap-2.5 rounded-control px-2.5 py-1.5 text-sm text-foreground transition-colors md:min-h-9 focus:bg-primary-soft focus:duration-0 focus-visible:-outline-offset-2 active:bg-muted data-[disabled]:pointer-events-none aria-disabled:cursor-not-allowed aria-disabled:bg-transparent aria-disabled:text-faint-foreground aria-disabled:*:[svg]:text-faint-foreground! data-[inset]:pl-9 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:shrink-0 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4 [&_svg:not([class*='text-'])]:text-muted-foreground"

// Delete and sign-out rows. Kept in their own string: data-[…] rules come after aria-disabled: in the CSS, so each rule
// needs not-aria-disabled: or a disabled delete row would stay red; and the guard that keeps faint text off status fills
// reads one class string at a time.
const menuItemDestructiveClasses =
  "data-[variant=destructive]:not-aria-disabled:text-danger data-[variant=destructive]:not-aria-disabled:focus:bg-danger-soft data-[variant=destructive]:not-aria-disabled:*:[svg]:text-danger!"

// Radix skips a disabled item for arrow keys and typeahead. An unavailable item stays focusable and is announced with
// its reason, but choosing it (click, Enter, Space) does nothing and keeps the menu open.
const unavailableItemProps = {
  "aria-disabled": true,
  "data-unavailable": "",
  onSelect: (event: Event) => event.preventDefault(),
} as const

function DropdownMenu({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.Root>) {
  return <DropdownMenuPrimitive.Root data-slot="dropdown-menu" {...props} />
}

function DropdownMenuPortal({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.Portal>) {
  return (
    <DropdownMenuPrimitive.Portal data-slot="dropdown-menu-portal" {...props} />
  )
}

function DropdownMenuTrigger({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.Trigger>) {
  return (
    <DropdownMenuPrimitive.Trigger
      data-slot="dropdown-menu-trigger"
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function DropdownMenuContent({
  className,
  sideOffset = 4,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.Content>) {
  return (
    <DropdownMenuPrimitive.Portal>
      <DropdownMenuPrimitive.Content
        data-slot="dropdown-menu-content"
        sideOffset={sideOffset}
        className={cn(menuPanelClasses, className)}
        {...props}
      />
    </DropdownMenuPrimitive.Portal>
  )
}

function DropdownMenuGroup({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.Group>) {
  return (
    <DropdownMenuPrimitive.Group data-slot="dropdown-menu-group" {...props} />
  )
}

type DropdownMenuItemProps = React.ComponentProps<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.Item> & {
  inset?: boolean
  variant?: "default" | "destructive"
} & (
    | { unavailable?: false }
    // Not with asChild: a child <Link> would navigate before the selection is cancelled.
    | { unavailable: true; asChild?: never; onSelect?: never }
  )

function DropdownMenuItem({
  className,
  inset,
  variant = "default",
  unavailable,
  ...props
}: DropdownMenuItemProps) {
  return (
    <DropdownMenuPrimitive.Item
      data-slot="dropdown-menu-item"
      data-inset={inset}
      data-variant={variant}
      className={cn(menuItemClasses, menuItemDestructiveClasses, className)}
      {...props}
      {...(unavailable ? unavailableItemProps : {})}
    />
  )
}

// Two-line item text (mockup .stack): the item name, then DropdownMenuItemDescription.
function DropdownMenuItemText({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"span">) {
  return (
    <span
      data-slot="dropdown-menu-item-text"
      className={cn("grid min-w-0 flex-1", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

// The second line: why an item cannot be used, or a short hint. 4.99:1 on the primary-soft highlight.
function DropdownMenuItemDescription({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"span">) {
  return (
    <span
      data-slot="dropdown-menu-item-description"
      className={cn("text-meta text-faint-foreground", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

// A check box in front of the text (mockup .cbox). Its colors follow the checked and disabled props.
function DropdownMenuCheckboxItem({
  className,
  children,
  checked,
  disabled,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.CheckboxItem>) {
  const on = Boolean(checked)
  return (
    <DropdownMenuPrimitive.CheckboxItem
      data-slot="dropdown-menu-checkbox-item"
      className={cn(menuItemClasses, "data-[state=checked]:font-medium", className)}
      checked={checked}
      disabled={disabled}
      {...props}
    >
      <span
        aria-hidden="true"
        className={cn(
          "flex size-4 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-sm border-[1.5px]",
          !on && "border-input bg-surface",
          on && !disabled && "border-primary bg-primary",
          on && disabled && "border-border bg-muted",
        )}
      >
        <DropdownMenuPrimitive.ItemIndicator>
          <CheckIcon strokeWidth={3} className={cn("size-3", disabled ? "text-muted-foreground" : "text-primary-foreground")} />
        </DropdownMenuPrimitive.ItemIndicator>
      </span>
      {children}
    </DropdownMenuPrimitive.CheckboxItem>
  )
}

function DropdownMenuRadioGroup({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.RadioGroup>) {
  return (
    <DropdownMenuPrimitive.RadioGroup
      data-slot="dropdown-menu-radio-group"
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

// The chosen option carries a check at the end of the row (mockup .check) and medium weight, so it is not told by color alone.
function DropdownMenuRadioItem({
  className,
  children,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.RadioItem>) {
  return (
    <DropdownMenuPrimitive.RadioItem
      data-slot="dropdown-menu-radio-item"
      className={cn(menuItemClasses, "data-[state=checked]:font-medium", className)}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
      <DropdownMenuPrimitive.ItemIndicator className="ml-auto flex">
        <CheckIcon className="size-4 text-primary" />
      </DropdownMenuPrimitive.ItemIndicator>
    </DropdownMenuPrimitive.RadioItem>
  )
}

function DropdownMenuLabel({
  className,
  inset,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.Label> & {
  inset?: boolean
}) {
  return (
    <DropdownMenuPrimitive.Label
      data-slot="dropdown-menu-label"
      data-inset={inset}
      className={cn(
        "px-2.5 pt-1.5 pb-1 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground data-[inset]:pl-9",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function DropdownMenuSeparator({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.Separator>) {
  return (
    <DropdownMenuPrimitive.Separator
      data-slot="dropdown-menu-separator"
      className={cn("mx-1 my-[5px] h-px bg-border-soft", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

// Trailing text on a row (mockup .trail), such as a current value or a key name.
function DropdownMenuShortcut({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"span">) {
  return (
    <span
      data-slot="dropdown-menu-shortcut"
      className={cn(
        "ml-auto pl-3 whitespace-nowrap text-meta text-faint-foreground",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function DropdownMenuSub({
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.Sub>) {
  return <DropdownMenuPrimitive.Sub data-slot="dropdown-menu-sub" {...props} />
}

function DropdownMenuSubTrigger({
  className,
  inset,
  children,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.SubTrigger> & {
  inset?: boolean
}) {
  return (
    <DropdownMenuPrimitive.SubTrigger
      data-slot="dropdown-menu-sub-trigger"
      data-inset={inset}
      className={cn(menuItemClasses, "data-[state=open]:bg-primary-soft", className)}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
      <ChevronRightIcon className="ml-auto size-4" />
    </DropdownMenuPrimitive.SubTrigger>
  )
}

function DropdownMenuSubContent({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DropdownMenuPrimitive.SubContent>) {
  return (
    <DropdownMenuPrimitive.SubContent
      data-slot="dropdown-menu-sub-content"
      className={cn(menuPanelClasses, className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuPortal,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuGroup,
  DropdownMenuLabel,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuItemText,
  DropdownMenuItemDescription,
  DropdownMenuCheckboxItem,
  DropdownMenuRadioGroup,
  DropdownMenuRadioItem,
  DropdownMenuSeparator,
  DropdownMenuShortcut,
  DropdownMenuSub,
  DropdownMenuSubTrigger,
  DropdownMenuSubContent,
}
```

What changed and why:
- **Panel (spec §5.2).** `rounded-md p-1 min-w-[8rem]` → `rounded-lg p-1.5 min-w-60` with `grid gap-px`. The panel is one string constant shared by `DropdownMenuContent` and `DropdownMenuSubContent`; Radix sets the same `--radix-dropdown-menu-content-*` variables on both. `min-width` beats `width`, so callers with a narrower `w-*` grow to 240px (Steps 14 and 16 remove those `w-*` classes).
- **Motion (§5.6).** `zoom-*` is gone. `duration-160 ease-out` feeds `--tw-duration` and `--tw-ease`, which tw-animate-css 1.4 reads, and the slide is 4px (`-1`). `motion-reduce:animate-none!` needs `!` to beat `data-[state=open]:animate-in`, the same as the dialogs in phase 1.
- **Rows (§5.3).**
  - Height is `min-h-11 md:min-h-9`: the old `sm:` breakpoint moves to `md:` (D4).
  - The row drops `outline-hidden`, so keyboard focus shows the base `:focus-visible` outline 2px inside the row. Ring on `primary-soft` is 3.82:1, and 3.93:1 on `danger-soft`.
  - Rule order was checked by compiling: `aria-disabled:` comes after `focus:` and `active:`, so an unavailable row gets no fill. `data-[…]` comes after `aria-disabled:`, so each destructive rule carries `not-aria-disabled:`. Without it, the delete row would stay red while it is disabled during a delete.
- **`unavailable` (§5.4).** Radix spreads the caller's props over its own `aria-disabled` (`react-menu` `dist/index.mjs:475-477`), and it never sees `disabled`. So the row stays in the roving focus and in typeahead (`:470` skips only `disabled`), and screen readers hear it as dimmed. `onSelect` is cancelled, and Enter, Space and pointer-up all go through it. A Radix `disabled` stays right for short-lived states whose reason is already on screen: the delete row while deleting, and the last visible column. Radix sets `aria-disabled` for those too, so they look the same.
- **Check box and radio (§5.5).**
  - The check box is a 16px box in front of the text. Its colors follow `checked` and `disabled`: the checked white tick is 8.07:1 on `primary`, and the empty box border (`input`) is 3.47:1 on white.
  - The radio has no leading dot. The chosen option gets a trailing `CheckIcon` and medium weight, so it is not told by color alone. `CircleIcon` is no longer imported.
- **Label, separator, shortcut.** The label is 12px `font-medium text-muted-foreground`, so callers drop their own label classes (Step 16). The separator uses `bg-border-soft`. The shortcut takes the mockup's `.trail` look; it is not renamed.

- [ ] **Step 7: Replace `src/components/ui/command.tsx`**

Replace the whole file with:

```tsx
"use client"

import * as React from "react"
import { Command as CommandPrimitive } from "cmdk"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { SearchIcon } from "lucide-react"

// The panel sits inside a popover: same 12px corners, or its white fill would show past the popover edge.
function Command({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof CommandPrimitive>) {
  return (
    <CommandPrimitive
      data-slot="command"
      className={cn(
        "flex h-full w-full flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-popover text-popover-foreground",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

// Search box inside the panel (mockup .menu-search). The ::placeholder base rule colors the placeholder.
// 16px text below md, so iOS Safari does not zoom into the field.
function CommandInput({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof CommandPrimitive.Input>) {
  return (
    <div
      data-slot="command-input-wrapper"
      className="mx-1.5 mt-1.5 mb-1 flex h-11 items-center gap-2 rounded-control border border-border bg-background px-2.5 focus-within:border-ring focus-within:ring-1 focus-within:ring-ring focus-within:shadow-field-focus md:h-9"
    >
      <SearchIcon className="size-4 shrink-0 text-faint-foreground" />
      <CommandPrimitive.Input
        data-slot="command-input"
        className={cn(
          "h-full w-full min-w-0 bg-transparent text-base outline-hidden disabled:cursor-not-allowed md:text-sm",
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

function CommandList({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof CommandPrimitive.List>) {
  return (
    <CommandPrimitive.List
      data-slot="command-list"
      className={cn(
        "max-h-[300px] scroll-py-1.5 overflow-x-hidden overflow-y-auto px-1.5 pb-1.5",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function CommandEmpty({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof CommandPrimitive.Empty>) {
  return (
    <CommandPrimitive.Empty
      data-slot="command-empty"
      className={cn("px-2.5 py-4 text-center text-meta text-faint-foreground", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function CommandGroup({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof CommandPrimitive.Group>) {
  return (
    <CommandPrimitive.Group
      data-slot="command-group"
      className={cn(
        "overflow-hidden text-foreground [&_[cmdk-group-heading]]:px-2.5 [&_[cmdk-group-heading]]:pt-1.5 [&_[cmdk-group-heading]]:pb-1 [&_[cmdk-group-heading]]:text-xs [&_[cmdk-group-heading]]:font-medium [&_[cmdk-group-heading]]:text-muted-foreground",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function CommandSeparator({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof CommandPrimitive.Separator>) {
  return (
    <CommandPrimitive.Separator
      data-slot="command-separator"
      className={cn("mx-1 my-[5px] h-px bg-border-soft", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

// Same row as a menu item. Focus stays in the search box (combobox), so the highlighted row is data-selected,
// not :focus. cmdk skips disabled rows by itself.
function CommandItem({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof CommandPrimitive.Item>) {
  return (
    <CommandPrimitive.Item
      data-slot="command-item"
      className={cn(
        "relative flex min-h-11 cursor-pointer select-none items-center gap-2.5 rounded-control px-2.5 py-1.5 text-sm text-foreground outline-hidden md:min-h-9 data-[selected=true]:bg-primary-soft data-[disabled=true]:pointer-events-none data-[disabled=true]:text-faint-foreground [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:shrink-0 [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4 [&_svg:not([class*='text-'])]:text-muted-foreground",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function CommandShortcut({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"span">) {
  return (
    <span
      data-slot="command-shortcut"
      className={cn(
        "ml-auto pl-3 whitespace-nowrap text-meta text-faint-foreground",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export {
  Command,
  CommandInput,
  CommandList,
  CommandEmpty,
  CommandGroup,
  CommandItem,
  CommandShortcut,
  CommandSeparator,
}
```

- `Command` is `rounded-lg`, the same as the popover it sits in. If the two corners differed, its white fill would show past the popover's curve.
- The search box (`.menu-search`) is now a box inside the panel, not a full-width bottom border. It shows the same focus ring and halo as a field.
- The input has no `placeholder:` class (I7): the Task 1 base rule colors it.
- The input is `text-base md:text-sm` (I8): iOS Safari zooms into fields under 16px.
- Rows are 36px from `md` up and 44px below, like menu rows, and highlight with `data-[selected=true]:bg-primary-soft`. Disabled rows are faint and use no opacity; cmdk skips them by itself.
- `CommandDialog` and the `dialog` import are deleted (0 users; its default title was the English "Command Palette").

```tsx
import * as React from "react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

// A key name in a hint, such as the global-search footer (mockup .kbd). No client directive, so server components can use it.
// font-sans because preflight gives <kbd> the mono font. leading-4 (16 / 12 = 1.33) is under the Thai 1.44 rule on
// purpose: a Kbd holds only Latin key names and arrows, never Thai text (spec I23).
export function Kbd({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"kbd">) {
  return (
    <kbd
      data-slot="kbd"
      className={cn(
        "inline-flex h-5 items-center rounded-sm border border-border bg-surface px-1.5 font-sans text-xs leading-4 font-medium whitespace-nowrap text-muted-foreground",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}
```

Tailwind 4.2.4 drops a class whose token is missing and reports nothing (spec §15 risk 1). Save this as `menus-tw-probe.mjs` in your temp folder (never commit it), and run `node <temp>/menus-tw-probe.mjs` from the repo root:

```js
// Task 8: compile src/app/globals.css in memory and confirm every class this task writes produces CSS.
// Run from the repo root: node <temp>/menus-tw-probe.mjs   (never commit this file)
import { createRequire } from "node:module"
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs"
import { resolve } from "node:path"

const require = createRequire(resolve("package.json"))
const { compile } = require("@tailwindcss/node")
const css = readFileSync("src/app/globals.css", "utf8")
const fresh = () => compile(css, { base: resolve("src/app"), onDependency() {} })
const classes = {
  "src/components/ui/dropdown-menu.tsx": "grid max-h-(--radix-dropdown-menu-content-available-height) min-w-60 origin-(--radix-dropdown-menu-content-transform-origin) gap-px rounded-lg p-1.5 shadow-overlay duration-160 ease-out data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=open]:fade-in-0 data-[side=bottom]:slide-in-from-top-1 data-[side=top]:slide-in-from-bottom-1 data-[side=left]:slide-in-from-right-1 data-[side=right]:slide-in-from-left-1 data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0 data-[state=closed]:duration-100 motion-reduce:animate-none! min-h-11 md:min-h-9 cursor-pointer gap-2.5 rounded-control px-2.5 py-1.5 focus:bg-primary-soft focus:duration-0 focus-visible:-outline-offset-2 active:bg-muted data-[disabled]:pointer-events-none aria-disabled:cursor-not-allowed aria-disabled:bg-transparent aria-disabled:text-faint-foreground aria-disabled:*:[svg]:text-faint-foreground! data-[inset]:pl-9 data-[variant=destructive]:not-aria-disabled:text-danger data-[variant=destructive]:not-aria-disabled:focus:bg-danger-soft data-[variant=destructive]:not-aria-disabled:*:[svg]:text-danger! text-meta text-faint-foreground data-[state=checked]:font-medium size-4 border-[1.5px] border-input bg-surface border-primary bg-primary border-border bg-muted size-3 text-primary-foreground ml-auto pt-1.5 pb-1 mx-1 my-[5px] bg-border-soft pl-3 data-[state=open]:bg-primary-soft",
  "src/components/ui/command.tsx": "mx-1.5 mt-1.5 mb-1 h-11 focus-within:border-ring focus-within:ring-1 focus-within:ring-ring focus-within:shadow-field-focus md:h-9 text-base md:text-sm scroll-py-1.5 pb-1.5 py-4 data-[selected=true]:bg-primary-soft data-[disabled=true]:pointer-events-none data-[disabled=true]:text-faint-foreground [&_[cmdk-group-heading]]:px-2.5 [&_[cmdk-group-heading]]:pt-1.5 [&_[cmdk-group-heading]]:pb-1",
  "src/components/ui/kbd.tsx": "h-5 rounded-sm px-1.5 font-sans text-xs leading-4 font-medium whitespace-nowrap",
  "src/components/layout/global-search.tsx": "max-h-100 mt-[3px] text-meta gap-x-3 gap-y-1.5 px-3.5 bg-background",
}
const base = (await fresh()).build([]).length
let missing = 0
for (const [file, list] of Object.entries(classes)) {
  for (const name of list.split(" ")) {
    if (!readFileSync(file, "utf8").includes(name)) { console.log(`NOT IN FILE ${file}: ${name}`); missing += 1; continue }
    const out = (await fresh()).build([name])
    if (out.length === base) { console.log(`NO CSS ${name}`); missing += 1 }
  }
}
// Rule order: a later rule wins at equal specificity.
const order = ["focus:bg-primary-soft", "active:bg-muted", "aria-disabled:bg-transparent", "data-[variant=destructive]:not-aria-disabled:focus:bg-danger-soft"]
const all = (await fresh()).build(order)
const at = order.map((name) => all.indexOf("." + name.replace(/[[\]:=!*()/.]/g, (m) => "\\" + m)))
console.log("order", order.map((name, i) => `${name}@${at[i]}`).join("  "))
if (at.some((p) => p === -1) || !(at[0] < at[2] && at[1] < at[2] && at[2] < at[3])) { console.log("ORDER WRONG"); missing += 1 }
const important = (await fresh()).build(["motion-reduce:animate-none!"])
console.log(important.includes("animation: none !important") ? "motion-reduce important ok" : "motion-reduce NOT important")
console.log(missing === 0 ? "all classes compile" : `ABORT: ${missing} problem(s)`)
process.exit(missing === 0 ? 0 : 1)
```

Expected: 3 lines:

- `motion-reduce important ok`
- `all classes compile`

The exit code is 0. A `NO CSS …` line means a Task 1 token is missing or misnamed; a `NOT IN FILE …` line means a Step 6-12 edit did not land. In either case stop and report. Run against `081d538`'s `globals.css`, the probe prints 9 `NO CSS` lines and exits 1.

- [ ] **Step 2: Replace `tests/status-badge.test.ts`**

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict"
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs"
import test from "node:test"
import { cn } from "../src/lib/utils.ts"
import { getStatusTone } from "../src/lib/status-tone.ts"
import { statusBadgeVariants, statusMarkerVariants } from "../src/components/ui/badge-variants.ts"

const calmTones = ["success", "info", "primary", "neutral", "muted"] as const
const actionTones = ["warning", "danger"] as const
const allTones = [...calmTones, ...actionTones]

test("status tones keep the existing workflow mapping", () => {
  assert.equal(getStatusTone("closed"), "success")
  assert.equal(getStatusTone("in_progress"), "warning")
  assert.equal(getStatusTone("cancelled"), "danger")
  assert.equal(getStatusTone("open"), "info")
  assert.equal(getStatusTone("approved"), "primary")
  assert.equal(getStatusTone("unknown_status"), "muted")
  assert.equal(getStatusTone(null), "muted")
})

test("every status badge has one size: 22px tall, 13/20 text and 6px corners", () => {
  for (const tone of allTones) {
    const classes = statusBadgeVariants({ tone })
    assert.match(classes, /\bh-5\.5\b/, `${tone}: fixed 22px height`)
    assert.match(classes, /\btext-meta\b/, `${tone}: 13/20 text`)
    assert.match(classes, /\brounded-control\b/, `${tone}: 6px corners`)
    assert.doesNotMatch(classes, /\bpy-|\btext-(xs|sm)\b|\brounded-md\b/, `${tone}: no vertical padding, second text size or 8px corners`)
  }
  assert.equal(statusBadgeVariants(), statusBadgeVariants({ tone: "muted" }), "muted stays the default tone")
})

test("cn() keeps the badge size, corners and tone ink next to a caller's class", () => {
  for (const tone of allTones) {
    const merged = cn(statusBadgeVariants({ tone }), "shrink-0").split(" ")
    for (const name of ["h-5.5", "text-meta", "rounded-control", "shrink-0"]) {
      assert.ok(merged.includes(name), `${tone}: cn() dropped ${name}`)
    }
  }
  assert.ok(cn(statusBadgeVariants({ tone: "muted" }), "shrink-0").split(" ").includes("text-muted-foreground"), "muted ink kept")
  assert.ok(cn(statusBadgeVariants({ tone: "warning" }), "max-w-40 shrink-0").split(" ").includes("text-warning"), "warning ink kept")
})

test("calm statuses have no fill, an invisible border and no side padding", () => {
  for (const tone of calmTones) {
    const classes = statusBadgeVariants({ tone })
    assert.match(classes, /\bborder-transparent\b/, tone)
    assert.match(classes, /\btext-muted-foreground\b/, tone)
    assert.doesNotMatch(classes, /\bbg-|\bpx-/, `${tone} must not add a fill or side padding`)
  }
})

test("statuses that need action keep the soft fill, tone border, AA ink and 8px side padding", () => {
  assert.match(statusBadgeVariants({ tone: "warning" }), /border-warning-border bg-warning-soft text-warning/)
  assert.match(statusBadgeVariants({ tone: "danger" }), /border-danger-border bg-danger-soft text-danger/)
  for (const tone of actionTones) {
    assert.match(statusBadgeVariants({ tone }), /\bpx-2\b/, tone)
    assert.doesNotMatch(statusBadgeVariants({ tone }), /\bpx-2\.5\b/, tone)
  }
})

test("each meaning has its own marker shape, kept in high-contrast mode", () => {
  type Tone = (typeof calmTones)[number] | (typeof actionTones)[number]
  const marker = (tone: Tone) => statusMarkerVariants({ tone })
  const shapeOf = (tone: Tone) => marker(tone).replace(/\b(bg|border)-(info|success|primary|warning|danger|muted-foreground|neutral-ink)\b/g, "").replace(/forced-colors:\S+/g, "").trim()
  const shapeTones = ["info", "success", "warning", "danger", "neutral"] as const
  assert.match(marker("info"), /rounded-full border-\[1\.5px\] border-info/)
  assert.match(marker("success"), /rounded-full bg-success/)
  assert.match(marker("primary"), /rounded-full bg-primary/)
  assert.match(marker("warning"), /bg-warning \[clip-path:polygon\(50%_0,100%_100%,0_100%\)\]/)
  assert.match(marker("danger"), /rotate-45 rounded-\[1px\] bg-danger/)
  for (const tone of ["neutral", "muted"] as const) {
    assert.match(marker(tone), /h-0\.5 w-\[7px\] rounded-\[1px\] bg-neutral-ink/, `${tone} bar uses the neutral ink`)
    assert.doesNotMatch(marker(tone), /bg-muted-foreground/, tone)
  }
  assert.equal(new Set(shapeTones.map(shapeOf)).size, shapeTones.length, "ring, dot, triangle, diamond and bar must differ without their color")
  for (const tone of allTones) assert.match(marker(tone), /forced-color-adjust-none/)
  assert.match(marker("info"), /forced-colors:border-\[CanvasText\]/)
  for (const tone of ["success", "primary", "neutral", "muted", "warning", "danger"] as const) {
    assert.match(marker(tone), /forced-colors:bg-\[CanvasText\]/)
  }
})

test("StatusBadge takes no database color and gives the variants only its tone", () => {
  const source = readFileSync("src/components/ui/status-badge.tsx", "utf8").replace(/\r\n/g, "\n")
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /color\?:|getStatusDotColor|style=/)
  assert.match(source, /className=\{statusMarkerVariants\(\{ tone: resolvedTone \}\)\}/)
  assert.match(source, /className=\{cn\(statusBadgeVariants\(\{ tone: resolvedTone \}\), className\)\}/)
})
```

What changed from the old file:
- The `xs`/`sm` loops are gone, and `px-2.5` is now forbidden.
- New: a one-size test (`h-5.5`, `text-meta`, `rounded-control`; no `py-`, `text-xs`/`text-sm` or `rounded-md`).
- New: a `cn()` test. `"max-w-40 shrink-0"` is the widest `className` a caller passes (`audit-scan-search.tsx`, the "in another location" badge).
- `neutral-ink` joins the colors that `shapeOf` strips, and both bars must use `bg-neutral-ink`.
- The source test now pins `statusBadgeVariants({ tone: resolvedTone })`.

After the edits, `statusBadgeVariants` reads:

```ts
// Two tiers: calm states read as plain text with a marker; states that need action keep a soft fill and border.
// The border stays (transparent) on calm badges so both tiers have the same height side by side.
// One size everywhere: a fixed 22px box (1px border + one 20px line of 13px text) that matches a table line
// and never stretches in a flex row. 6px corners (rounded-control) because the badge is a small control.
export const statusBadgeVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex h-5.5 w-fit max-w-full shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-control border text-meta font-medium whitespace-nowrap",
  {
    variants: {
      tone: {
        success: "border-transparent text-muted-foreground",
        info: "border-transparent text-muted-foreground",
        primary: "border-transparent text-muted-foreground",
        neutral: "border-transparent text-muted-foreground",
        muted: "border-transparent text-muted-foreground",
        warning: "border-warning-border bg-warning-soft text-warning px-2",
        danger: "border-danger-border bg-danger-soft text-danger px-2",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: { tone: "muted" },
  },
)
```

Notes on these values:
- **Height:** `h-5.5` is `calc(var(--spacing) * 5.5)` = 1.375rem = 22px. Because it is in rem, it scales with the browser font size.
- **Thai pages:** `text-meta` keeps its 20/13 line height on Thai pages too (1.54 ≥ 1.44). The badge needs no `html:lang(th)` rule.
- **Old callers:** cva ignores the `size` key that the old call sites still pass in this commit.

- [ ] **Step 10: Remove the 52 attributes with a codemod**

Do not use a file-wide `sed`. `size="xs"` / `size="sm"` occurs 62 times in 25 files at `081d538`, and 10 of those belong to other components (`Button` and others). The script edits only inside `<StatusBadge … />` tags of the shared component. Save it as `statusbadge-size-codemod.mjs` in your temp folder:

```js
// One-off script: keep it in your temp folder, never in the repo. Run it from the repo root.
// Removes size="xs" / size="sm" from every shared <StatusBadge … /> call (spec §8.1.2, I17 commit b).
//   node <temp>/statusbadge-size-codemod.mjs --dry   counts only, writes nothing
//   node <temp>/statusbadge-size-codemod.mjs         writes, then checks that no size= is left
// It edits only the attribute text inside the tag and writes each file back with its other bytes untouched.
import { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs"

const dry = process.argv.includes("--dry")

// Attributes to remove per file, counted at 081d538: 50 × size="xs" + 2 × size="sm" = 52 in 19 files.
const expected = {
  "src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/admin/storage/page.tsx": 2,
  "src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/assets/[id]/page.tsx": 4,
  "src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/audit/findings/page.tsx": 4,
  "src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/audit/rounds/[id]/page.tsx": 5,
  "src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/disposal/page.tsx": 2,
  "src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/disposal/batches/page.tsx": 1,
  "src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/disposal/batches/[id]/page.tsx": 2,
  "src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/maintenance/page.tsx": 3,
  "src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/maintenance/pm/page.tsx": 3,
  "src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/master-data/employees/[id]/page.tsx": 5,
  "src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/my-assets/page.tsx": 4,
  "src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/my-assets/[id]/page.tsx": 2,
  "src/components/admin/asset-state-review-workspace.tsx": 2,
  "src/components/assets/asset-register-mobile-list.tsx": 3,
  "src/components/assets/asset-register-table.tsx": 3,
  "src/components/audit/audit-scan-lookup-card.tsx": 1,
  "src/components/audit/audit-scan-room-list.tsx": 1,
  "src/components/audit/audit-scan-search.tsx": 4,
  "src/components/layout/global-search.tsx": 1,
}
const EXPECTED_TOTAL = 52
const EXPECTED_SM = 2
const EXPECTED_TAGS = 59
const EXPECTED_TAG_FILES = 22

function walk(dir) {
  const out = []
  for (const entry of readdirSync(dir)) {
    const path = `${dir}/${entry}`
    if (statSync(path).isDirectory()) out.push(...walk(path))
    else if (/\.(ts|tsx)$/.test(entry)) out.push(path)
  }
  return out
}

// Every shared StatusBadge call is self-closing. A ">" inside an attribute would end the match early;
// there is none at 081d538, and the tag count check below stops the script if one appears.
const tagPattern = /<StatusBadge\b[^>]*?\/>/g
// The whitespace in front goes with the attribute: ' size="xs"' and the own-line '\r\n    size="xs"'
// in admin/storage/page.tsx both disappear without leaving a blank line or a double space.
const sizePattern = /\s+size="(xs|sm)"/g
// ui/status-badge.tsx, notifications/page.tsx and IntegrationClientManager.tsx declare their own
// StatusBadge; the last two are local components (2D), not calls of the shared one.
const isSharedCaller = (source) => !/\bfunction StatusBadge\b/.test(source)

const files = walk("src")
let tags = 0
let sm = 0
const tagFiles = new Set()
const removedByFile = {}
const nextByFile = new Map()

for (const file of files) {
  const source = readFileSync(file, "utf8")
  if (!isSharedCaller(source)) continue
  let removed = 0
  const next = source.replace(tagPattern, (tag) => {
    tags += 1
    tagFiles.add(file)
    return tag.replace(sizePattern, (_attribute, size) => {
      removed += 1
      if (size === "sm") sm += 1
      return ""
    })
  })
  if (removed > 0) {
    removedByFile[file] = removed
    nextByFile.set(file, next)
  }
}

const total = Object.values(removedByFile).reduce((sum, count) => sum + count, 0)
console.log(`shared <StatusBadge /> tags: ${tags} in ${tagFiles.size} files`)
console.log(`size attributes to remove: ${total} (sm: ${sm}) in ${nextByFile.size} files`)

const problems = []
if (tags !== EXPECTED_TAGS) problems.push(`expected ${EXPECTED_TAGS} tags, found ${tags}`)
if (tagFiles.size !== EXPECTED_TAG_FILES) problems.push(`expected tags in ${EXPECTED_TAG_FILES} files, found ${tagFiles.size}`)
if (total !== EXPECTED_TOTAL) problems.push(`expected ${EXPECTED_TOTAL} attributes, found ${total}`)
if (sm !== EXPECTED_SM) problems.push(`expected ${EXPECTED_SM} size="sm", found ${sm}`)
for (const [file, count] of Object.entries(expected)) {
  if (removedByFile[file] !== count) problems.push(`${file}: expected ${count}, found ${removedByFile[file] ?? 0}`)
}
for (const file of Object.keys(removedByFile)) {
  if (!(file in expected)) problems.push(`${file}: passes size= to StatusBadge but is not in the expected list`)
}
if (problems.length > 0) {
  console.error(`ABORT, nothing written:\n- ${problems.join("\n- ")}`)
  process.exit(1)
}
if (dry) {
  console.log("dry run OK, nothing written")
  process.exit(0)
}

for (const [file, next] of nextByFile) writeFileSync(file, next)

const left = files.flatMap((file) => {
  const source = readFileSync(file, "utf8")
  if (!isSharedCaller(source)) return []
  return [...source.matchAll(tagPattern)].filter((match) => /\bsize=/.test(match[0])).map((match) => `${file}: ${match[0]}`)
})
if (left.length > 0) {
  console.error(`size= is still passed to StatusBadge:\n${left.join("\n")}`)
  process.exit(1)
}
console.log(`written: ${total} attributes removed in ${nextByFile.size} files`)
```

Run: `node <temp>/statusbadge-size-codemod.mjs --dry`
Expected (exit 0):

```
shared <StatusBadge /> tags: 59 in 22 files
size attributes to remove: 52 (sm: 2) in 19 files
dry run OK, nothing written
```

On `ABORT, nothing written`, stop and report the listed differences. Do not edit by hand, and do not commit. Tasks 1-8 do not add or remove a `StatusBadge` call.

The whole file now reads:

```tsx
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { getStatusTone, type StatusTone } from "@/lib/status-tone"
import { statusBadgeVariants, statusMarkerVariants } from "@/components/ui/badge-variants"

export { getStatusTone, type StatusTone } from "@/lib/status-tone"

const knownTones = new Set<StatusTone>(["neutral", "muted", "primary", "info", "success", "warning", "danger"])

export function StatusBadge({
  label,
  status,
  tone,
  className,
}: {
  label: string
  status?: string | null
  tone?: StatusTone | string
  className?: string
}) {
  const resolvedTone: StatusTone =
    tone && knownTones.has(tone as StatusTone) ? (tone as StatusTone) : getStatusTone(status)

  return (
    <span data-slot="status-badge" data-tone={resolvedTone} className={cn(statusBadgeVariants({ tone: resolvedTone }), className)}>
      <span aria-hidden="true" className={statusMarkerVariants({ tone: resolvedTone })} />
      <span className="min-w-0 truncate">{label}</span>
    </span>
  )
}
```

**Files:**

- Modify (one line each, through a one-off script that stays in your temp folder), all under `src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/`:
  - CRLF: `assets/[id]/page.tsx:888`, `audit/rounds/[id]/page.tsx:415`, `disposal/[id]/page.tsx:177`, `maintenance/[id]/page.tsx:109`, `maintenance/pm/[id]/edit/page.tsx:28`, `master-data/employees/[id]/page.tsx:261`, `master-data/suppliers/[id]/page.tsx:157`
  - **LF**: `disposal/batches/[id]/page.tsx:55`
- Modify: `messages/th.json`, `messages/en.json` (CRLF; through `scripts/messages-edit.mjs`, which keeps the line endings)
- Not in this task:
  - `src/app/globals.css`. The `tag` utility comes from Task 1 and is not defined a second time (I1).
  - `DESIGN.md`. Task 13 adds the Status Badges, Navigation (Breadcrumbs) and empty-value/number sections, plus the I13 note that a 20px crumb link is an accepted target-size exception (spec §14, I19).
  - Any caller of `Nil`, `orNil`, `formatCount`, `formatMoney` or `common.baht`. 2A has none (spec appendix A); 2B adopts them. `formatCurrency`, `formatDate`, `formatDateTime` and the local `formatMoney` in `depreciation-policy-builder.tsx:447` stay as they are.
  - The spacing between crumbs and the page title (`mb-2` wrappers, `mt-3`, `space-y-5`). `PageHeader` owns that in 2B (spec §8.6 #17).

Line numbers are for HEAD `41eff3f` (Tasks 1-9 do not touch these lines); match on the quoted strings, not on the numbers.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 1:
    - `text-meta`, `text-faint-foreground`.
    - The updated `@utility tag`. Its nested `:where(a, button, label, summary) &` rule compiles to a descendant selector (`:where(a, …) .tag`). So a code inside a link goes on an inner `<span className="tag">`, never on the `<Link>` itself.
    - `cn()` with `extendTailwindMerge`, so `cn("min-w-0 text-meta text-faint-foreground", className)` keeps both the size and the color.
    - Guard set T in `tests/visual-foundation-guards.test.ts` must stay green. A class string with the `tag` class has no bold weight and no text size. No class string pairs `text-faint-foreground` with a status soft fill.
  - Phase 1:
    - The `--input` color (`text-input`).
    - The base `:focus-visible` outline, which covers the crumb links.
    - `readSourceFiles` in `tests/helpers/source-files.ts`.
  - `src/app/[locale]/layout.tsx` passes every message to `NextIntlClientProvider`, so `Nil` and `Breadcrumbs` work in client components too.
- Produces (fixed names):
  - `src/components/ui/breadcrumbs.tsx`:
    - `export type BreadcrumbItem = { label: string; href?: string; code?: boolean }`
    - `export function Breadcrumbs({ items, className }: { items: BreadcrumbItem[]; className?: string })`. A server component with no outer margin.
  - `src/components/ui/nil.tsx`:
    - `Nil({ label, className }: { label?: string; className?: string })`
    - `orNil(value: ReactNode, label?: string): ReactNode`
  - `src/lib/display-format.ts`. Import-free, so tests import it directly.
    - `isBlankValue(value: unknown): boolean`
    - `formatCount(value: number): string`, plus an overload that takes `number | null | undefined` and returns `string | null`
    - `formatMoney(value: number, options?: FormatMoneyOptions): string`, plus an overload that takes `number | null | undefined` and returns `string | null`
    - `export type FormatMoneyOptions = { unit?: string }`
  - Message keys, Thai / English:
    - `common.breadcrumb` "เส้นทางหน้า" / "Breadcrumb"
    - `common.noValue` "ไม่มีข้อมูล" / "None"
    - `common.baht` "บาท" / "THB"

Spec rulings applied here:
- I1: no `tag` definition here.
- I20: the `common.*` keys go in this task, separate from Task 8's `nav.*`. `common.noValue` stays separate from `common.noData` "ไม่พบข้อมูล".
- I13: noted for Task 13.
- The tables in §8.2.2, §8.3.2, §8.3.3 and §8.5 apply, with three rulings of this plan:
  1. **A code inside a link uses an inner `<span className="tag">`.** §8.2.2 writes `code && "tag"` on the `<Link>` className. The Task 1 utility resets `user-select` only for a `.tag` inside an `<a>`, and an `a.tag` would keep `user-select: all`. The current page item keeps `tag` on the same span as `font-medium text-foreground`, because `.tag` is emitted before `.font-medium` and gives weight 500, as in the mockup's `.crumbs .tag[aria-current]`. An inner span would reset the item to 400. None of the 11 call sites puts a code on a link today, so this only fixes the API.
  2. **Both formatters add `signDisplay: "negative"`, which §8.3.3 does not list.** Without it, `formatCount(-0)` prints `-0`, and `formatMoney(-0.001)` prints `-0.00`. Depreciation math can produce both. The option needs Safari 15.4+, and Next 16 already requires Safari 16.4+ (`node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/01-getting-started/01-installation.md:60`). TypeScript's `esnext` lib types it.
  3. **`formatMoney(x, { unit: "" })` returns the bare number.** An empty unit adds no space. A middle item with no `href` renders as a plain span, as the current component does.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `tests/display-format.test.ts`**

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict"
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs"
import test from "node:test"
import { formatCount, formatMoney, isBlankValue } from "../src/lib/display-format.ts"

const read = (path: string) => readFileSync(path, "utf8").replace(/\r\n/g, "\n")
const NBSP = "\u00A0"

test("formatCount groups thousands the same way on every machine", () => {
  assert.equal(formatCount(1751), "1,751")
  assert.equal(formatCount(1234567), "1,234,567")
  assert.equal(formatCount(0), "0")
  assert.equal(formatCount(-42), "-42")
  assert.equal(formatCount(-0), "0", "negative zero prints as 0")
  assert.equal(formatCount(1751), new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US").format(1751), "th-TH digits and grouping match en-US")
})

test("formatMoney always shows two decimals and puts the unit after a no-break space", () => {
  assert.equal(formatMoney(64900), "64,900.00")
  assert.equal(formatMoney(-1500.5), "-1,500.50")
  assert.equal(formatMoney(0), "0.00")
  assert.equal(formatMoney(-0.001), "0.00", "an amount that rounds to zero never prints -0.00")
  assert.equal(formatMoney(64900, { unit: "บาท" }), `64,900.00${NBSP}บาท`)
  assert.equal(formatMoney(12.3, { unit: "THB" }), `12.30${NBSP}THB`)
  assert.equal(formatMoney(5, { unit: "" }), "5.00")
})

test("missing numbers come back as null so the caller can show Nil", () => {
  assert.equal(formatCount(null), null)
  assert.equal(formatCount(undefined), null)
  assert.equal(formatMoney(undefined), null)
  assert.equal(formatMoney(null, { unit: "บาท" }), null)
})

test("isBlankValue treats only null, undefined and blank strings as empty", () => {
  for (const value of [null, undefined, "", "  ", "\t\n"]) {
    assert.equal(isBlankValue(value), true, `${JSON.stringify(value)} is blank`)
  }
  for (const value of [0, false, "0", "-", "GRL-COM-24-0003", [], {}]) {
    assert.equal(isBlankValue(value), false, `${JSON.stringify(value)} is a value`)
  }
})

test("display-format is import-free TypeScript with two fixed th-TH formatters", () => {
  const source = read("src/lib/display-format.ts")
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /^import /m, "tests import this file directly")
  assert.equal(source.match(/new Intl\.NumberFormat\("th-TH"/g)?.length, 2)
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /toLocaleString\(/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /style: "currency"|฿/)
})

  assert.match(source, /<span aria-hidden="true">—<\/span>/)
```

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict"
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs"
import test from "node:test"
import { readSourceFiles } from "./helpers/source-files.ts"

const read = (path: string) => readFileSync(path, "utf8").replace(/\r\n/g, "\n")
const breadcrumbs = () => read("src/components/ui/breadcrumbs.tsx")
const commonMessages = (locale: "th" | "en") =>
  (JSON.parse(readFileSync(`messages/${locale}.json`, "utf8")) as { common: Record<string, string> }).common
const escapeRegExp = (text: string) => text.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&")

test("breadcrumbs are a translated, server-rendered ordered list that marks the current page", () => {
  const source = breadcrumbs()
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /"use client"/)
  assert.match(source, /const t = useTranslations\("common"\)/)
  assert.match(source, /<nav aria-label=\{t\("breadcrumb"\)\}/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /"Breadcrumb"/)
  assert.match(source, /<ol className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1">/)
  assert.match(source, /<li key=\{`\$\{item\.label\}-\$\{index\}`\} className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1">/)
  assert.match(source, /<span aria-current="page"/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /\btruncate\b/, "long labels wrap and codes are never cut")
})

test("breadcrumbs use the meta size, faint ink, the tag style for codes and a thin chevron", () => {
  const source = breadcrumbs()
  assert.match(source, /export type BreadcrumbItem = \{\n  label: string\n  href\?: string\n[^}]*  code\?: boolean\n\}/)
  assert.match(source, /export function Breadcrumbs\(\{ items, className \}: \{ items: BreadcrumbItem\[\]; className\?: string \}\)/)
  assert.match(source, /className=\{cn\("min-w-0 text-meta text-faint-foreground", className\)\}/)
  assert.match(source, /<span aria-current="page" className=\{cn\("font-medium text-foreground", item\.code && "tag"\)\}>/)
  assert.match(source, /<Link href=\{item\.href\} className="hover:text-foreground hover:underline underline-offset-3">/)
  // Inside a link the tag sits on an inner span: the tag utility resets user-select only for a .tag inside <a>.
  assert.match(source, /\{item\.code \? <span className="tag">\{item\.label\}<\/span> : item\.label\}/)
  assert.match(source, /<ChevronRight className="size-3 shrink-0 text-input" strokeWidth=\{3\} \/>/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(source, /\btext-sm\b|text-muted-foreground|hover:text-primary|h-3\.5/)
})

test("every record page ends its breadcrumb trail on a code; the create and list pages do not", () => {
  const sites = readSourceFiles("src").flatMap((file) =>
    [...file.source.matchAll(/<Breadcrumbs\b[\s\S]*?\/>/g)].map((match) => ({ path: file.path, element: match[0].replace(/\s+/g, " ") })),
  )
  assert.equal(sites.length, 11, "Breadcrumbs call sites")
  assert.equal(sites.filter((site) => /code: true/.test(site.element)).length, 8, "call sites that mark a code")

  const base = "src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/"
  const lastCode: Record<string, string> = {
    "assets/[id]/page.tsx": "asset.assetTag",
    "audit/rounds/[id]/page.tsx": "round.auditNo",
    "disposal/[id]/page.tsx": "disposalRequest.disposalNo",
    "disposal/batches/[id]/page.tsx": "batch.batchNo",
    "maintenance/[id]/page.tsx": "ticket.repairNo",
    "maintenance/pm/[id]/edit/page.tsx": "plan.planNo",
    "master-data/employees/[id]/page.tsx": "employee.code",
    "master-data/suppliers/[id]/page.tsx": "supplier.code",
  }
  for (const [file, expression] of Object.entries(lastCode)) {
    const site = sites.find((candidate) => candidate.path === base + file)
    assert.ok(site, `no Breadcrumbs in ${file}`)
    assert.match(site.element, new RegExp(`\\{ label: ${escapeRegExp(expression)}, code: true \\},? ?\\]\\}`), file)
    assert.equal(site.element.match(/code: true/g)?.length, 1, `${file}: only the last item is a code`)
  }
  for (const file of ["maintenance/new/page.tsx", "maintenance/pm/new/page.tsx", "maintenance/pm/page.tsx"]) {
    const site = sites.find((candidate) => candidate.path === base + file)
    assert.ok(site, `no Breadcrumbs in ${file}`)
    assert.doesNotMatch(site.element, /code:/, `${file} ends on a page title, not a code`)
  }
})

  assert.equal(en.breadcrumb, "Breadcrumb")

  assert.equal(th.baht, "บาท")
  assert.equal(en.baht, "THB")
  assert.notEqual(th.noValue, th.noData, "noValue (nothing recorded) and noData (nothing found) stay separate")
})
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Create `src/lib/display-format.ts`**

```ts
// Display helpers for counts and money. Thai locale always, so the output never depends on the machine that renders it.
// th-TH uses Arabic digits and the same grouping as en-US, so English pages read the same.
// signDisplay negative: an amount that rounds to zero prints 0, never -0.
const countFormat = new Intl.NumberFormat("th-TH", { maximumFractionDigits: 0, signDisplay: "negative" })
const moneyFormat = new Intl.NumberFormat("th-TH", { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2, signDisplay: "negative" })

// Blank means nothing to show: null, undefined, or a string with only spaces. 0 and false are values.
export function isBlankValue(value: unknown): boolean {
  return value === null || value === undefined || (typeof value === "string" && value.trim() === "")
}

export function formatCount(value: number): string
export function formatCount(value: number | null | undefined): string | null
export function formatCount(value: number | null | undefined): string | null {
  return value == null ? null : countFormat.format(value)
}

export type FormatMoneyOptions = { unit?: string }

// Two decimals always. The unit (common.baht) follows the number after a no-break space; table cells leave it out.
export function formatMoney(value: number, options?: FormatMoneyOptions): string
export function formatMoney(value: number | null | undefined, options?: FormatMoneyOptions): string | null
export function formatMoney(value: number | null | undefined, options?: FormatMoneyOptions): string | null {
  if (value == null) return null
  const text = moneyFormat.format(value)
  return options?.unit ? `${text}\u00A0${options.unit}` : text
}
```

Notes:
- Overload signatures are erasable, so Node's type stripping runs this file as is.
- Callers convert a Prisma `Decimal` with `Number()` themselves.
- Number alignment comes from the `num` class on the wrapper (or the table base rule), not from this helper.
- 2B writes `formatMoney(x, { unit: t("baht") }) ?? <Nil />`.

Run: `node --test tests/display-format.test.ts`
Expected: the first five tests pass. `"Nil hides the dash …"` fails with `ENOENT … src/components/ui/nil.tsx`.

- [ ] **Step 7: Create `src/components/ui/nil.tsx`**

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import { useTranslations } from "next-intl"
import { isBlankValue } from "@/lib/display-format"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

// An empty value: a dash people see, and words a screen reader says instead of reading the dash.
// No client directive, so server pages render it too. Faint ink belongs on neutral surfaces only, never on a tinted status fill.
export function Nil({ label, className }: { label?: string; className?: string }) {
  const t = useTranslations("common")

  return (
    <span data-slot="nil" className={cn("text-faint-foreground", className)}>
      <span aria-hidden="true">—</span>
      <span className="sr-only">{label ?? t("noValue")}</span>
    </span>
  )
}

// Shows value, or Nil when it is null, undefined or only spaces. 0 and false are values and stay.
export function orNil(value: ReactNode, label?: string): ReactNode {
  return isBlankValue(value) ? <Nil label={label} /> : value
}
```

The dash is U+2014. Task 8's emoji guard (`\p{Emoji_Presentation}|\uFE0F`) does not match it. Callers that know the context pass their own `label`, for example "ไม่มีผู้ถือครอง". The default is `common.noValue`.

New content:

```tsx
import Link from "next/link"
import { ChevronRight } from "lucide-react"
import { useTranslations } from "next-intl"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

export type BreadcrumbItem = {
  label: string
  href?: string
  // A record code (asset tag, document number): shown in the mono tag style and never cut.
  code?: boolean
}

// No client directive: every caller is a server page. Spacing to the page title belongs to the caller.
export function Breadcrumbs({ items, className }: { items: BreadcrumbItem[]; className?: string }) {
  const t = useTranslations("common")

  return (
    <nav aria-label={t("breadcrumb")} className={cn("min-w-0 text-meta text-faint-foreground", className)}>
      <ol className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1">
        {items.map((item, index) => {
          const isLast = index === items.length - 1

          return (
            <li key={`${item.label}-${index}`} className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1">
              {isLast ? (
                <span aria-current="page" className={cn("font-medium text-foreground", item.code && "tag")}>
                  {item.label}
                </span>
              ) : item.href ? (
                <Link href={item.href} className="hover:text-foreground hover:underline underline-offset-3">
                  {item.code ? <span className="tag">{item.label}</span> : item.label}
                </Link>
              ) : (
                <span className={item.code ? "tag" : undefined}>{item.label}</span>
              )}
              {isLast ? null : <ChevronRight className="size-3 shrink-0 text-input" strokeWidth={3} />}
            </li>
          )
        })}
      </ol>
    </nav>
  )
}
```

What this gives (spec §8.2.2 and §8.6):
- **Text:** 13/20 in faint ink (5.09:1 on canvas). The current page is `font-medium text-foreground` (14.94:1 on canvas). With `tag`, a code is 12px mono at weight 500, and one click selects the whole code.
- **Links:** no underline at rest. On hover, foreground ink with an underline 3px below, clear of Thai lower vowels.
- **Chevron:** 12px with a 1.5px drawn stroke in `--input` (3.06:1 on canvas). lucide-react 1.14.0 adds `aria-hidden="true"` by itself when the icon gets no accessible prop (`node_modules/lucide-react/dist/esm/Icon.mjs`, the `!children && !hasA11yProp(rest)` branch).
- **List:** `ol > li`, so screen readers announce the item count. Preflight removes the list markers. Long labels wrap; nothing is truncated.
- **Translation:**
  - `useTranslations` in a non-async server component resolves to next-intl 4.11.0's `react-server` export.
  - This is its first use in this repo. Task 12 checks that the server HTML has `aria-label="เส้นทางหน้า"`.
- **Guards and classes:**
  - The three `tag` class strings are the bare `"tag"`, so Task 1's tag guard stays green.
  - `cn()` keeps `text-meta` next to `text-faint-foreground`, which `tests/class-merge.test.ts` pins.
  - Removing `hover:text-primary` here leaves 33 such lines in 26 files for 2D. No test counts them.

- [ ] **Step 9: Mark the record code at the end of 8 trails**

Save this script in your temp folder as `mark-breadcrumb-codes.mjs` (never commit it):

```js
// Marks the record code at the end of 8 breadcrumb trails (spec 8.2.3). One-off script: keep it in your temp folder.
// Run from the repo root: node <temp>/mark-breadcrumb-codes.mjs --dry   (counts only), then without --dry.
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs"

const dry = process.argv.includes("--dry")
const base = "src/app/[locale]/(dashboard)/"
const edits = [
  ["assets/[id]/page.tsx", "{ label: asset.assetTag },", "{ label: asset.assetTag, code: true },"],
  ["audit/rounds/[id]/page.tsx", "{ label: round.auditNo },", "{ label: round.auditNo, code: true },"],
  ["disposal/[id]/page.tsx", "{ label: disposalRequest.disposalNo },", "{ label: disposalRequest.disposalNo, code: true },"],
  ["disposal/batches/[id]/page.tsx", "{ label: batch.batchNo }]} />", "{ label: batch.batchNo, code: true }]} />"],
  ["maintenance/[id]/page.tsx", "{ label: ticket.repairNo }]} />", "{ label: ticket.repairNo, code: true }]} />"],
  ["maintenance/pm/[id]/edit/page.tsx", "{ label: plan.planNo }]} />", "{ label: plan.planNo, code: true }]} />"],
  ["master-data/employees/[id]/page.tsx", "{ label: employee.code },", "{ label: employee.code, code: true },"],
  ["master-data/suppliers/[id]/page.tsx", "{ label: supplier.code },", "{ label: supplier.code, code: true },"],
]

// Check every file before writing any, so a mismatch leaves the tree untouched.
const plan = edits.map(([file, from, to]) => {
  const path = base + file
  const source = readFileSync(path, "utf8")
  return { path, source, from, to, count: source.split(from).length - 1 }
})
const wrong = plan.filter((edit) => edit.count !== 1)
if (wrong.length > 0) {
  for (const edit of wrong) console.error(`MISMATCH ${edit.path}: expected 1 x ${edit.from}, found ${edit.count}`)
  console.error("nothing written")
  process.exit(1)
}
if (!dry) {
  for (const edit of plan) writeFileSync(edit.path, edit.source.replace(edit.from, () => edit.to))
}
console.log(`${dry ? "would mark" : "marked"} ${plan.length} breadcrumb codes`)
```

Run from the repo root: `node <temp>/mark-breadcrumb-codes.mjs --dry`
Expected: `would mark 8 breadcrumb codes`. On any `MISMATCH` line, stop and report; nothing has been written.

Run: `node <temp>/mark-breadcrumb-codes.mjs`
Expected: `marked 8 breadcrumb codes`. A second run prints 8 `MISMATCH … found 0` lines and `nothing written`. That is the self-check that the edit happened once.

How the script works:
- Each search string occurs exactly once in its file at HEAD, and none is on a line that Tasks 1-9 change.
- The script replaces text inside the file and writes the rest back untouched, so CRLF stays CRLF and the LF file `disposal/batches/[id]/page.tsx` stays LF.
- The first item of each trail is not touched, so `tests/operational-return-navigation.test.ts:28,43` (`{ label: t("title"), href: returnToHref }`) still pass.
- These three pages keep their last item as it is, because it repeats the h1 text, not a code: `maintenance/new/page.tsx:46` (`t("createTitle")`), `maintenance/pm/new/page.tsx:32` (`t("pmCreateTitle")`), `maintenance/pm/page.tsx:39` (`t("pmTitle")`).

### Task 11: Quiet toasts (AppToaster), frameless ActionEmptyState, EmptyNote, Notice and the stale-data banner

Spec section 7 (`07-feedback.md`) with these rulings applied:
- **I12** replaces the hand-written spinner of 7.5: the stale-data refresh button becomes `<Button variant="outline" size="md" busy={refreshing}>`. `disabled={refreshing}` and the spinning `RefreshCw` go. Task 6's spinner already has `motion-reduce:animate-none` and takes the place of the leading icon.
- **I3** removes the fallback `cn` row of 7.7. `tests/class-merge.test.ts` (Task 1) already pins `cn("text-meta text-faint-foreground")`.
- **I4**: the `foreground`/`muted-foreground` on `warning-soft`/`danger-soft` pairs of 7.7 went into `tests/design-tokens-contrast.test.ts` in Task 1. Do not touch that file.
- **I15**: toasts use 12px corners, like menus and popovers. The toast close button uses 6px, the control radius. The `Notice` box uses `rounded-md` (8px), from the mockup's `.notice`.
- **I19**: the documentation of spec 7.9 belongs to Task 13. This task edits no docs.
- **Plan header, Review Focus 3**: the new test file also holds the guard "every `<Button busy>` sits in a `"use client"` file and never also gets `disabled`". It needs at least one match, so it cannot pass vacuously. This task adds the first match.

Rulings made in this task (the spec is silent or loose here):
- `[data-icon]` is 16×22 with `margin: 0`. The svg keeps its `size-4` (16×16) and only loses sonner's margins. Sonner's `align-items: center` puts it in the middle of the first 22px line. The spec table gives the svg 16×22 as well, and the result on screen is the same.
- `ActionEmptyState`'s icon wrapper gets `aria-hidden="true"`. Icons passed by call sites are decorative too.
- `Notice`'s content column is `grid min-w-0 flex-1 justify-items-start`. `flex-1` lets inline notices use the full row. The title, the body and the action row are `<div>`s, so any `ReactNode` fits.
- `tests/visual-consistency-ui.test.ts:35-40` checks `/buttonVariants\(\{[^}]*size: "md"/` (spec table A #15). It also checks that `buttonVariants({ size: "md" })` is `min-h-11 … md:h-9 md:min-h-0`, so the test name ("thumb-friendly on mobile") stays true.

This task changes no toast call site. The 133 `toast.*` calls in 47 files and the 62 `apiError.toast(` calls in 42 files keep working. Of the toast options, only `description` is used (`use-api-error.ts:21`), and no toast has an action button. `ActionEmptyState` keeps every prop name and the strings that tests read. Its 29 call sites in 20 files change look without code changes. Create `tests/feedback-states-ui.test.ts`:

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict"
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs"
import test from "node:test"
import { findMatches, readSourceFiles } from "./helpers/source-files.ts"

// node --test cannot load .tsx, so these tests read the sources (spec 2A section 7.7, I12).
const sources = readSourceFiles("src")
const read = (path: string) => readFileSync(path, "utf8").replace(/\r\n/g, "\n")
const isClientModule = (source: string) => /^["']use client["']/.test(source.trimStart())

// The opening tag of every <Button …> in a source: from "<Button" to the first ">" outside braces and quotes,
// so arrow functions inside props (onClick={() => …}) do not end the tag early.
function buttonOpeningTags(source: string): string[] {
  const tags: string[] = []
  for (const match of source.matchAll(/<Button\b/g)) {
    let depth = 0
    let quote = ""
    for (let index = match.index + match[0].length; index < source.length; index += 1) {
      const char = source[index]
      if (quote) {
        if (char === quote) quote = ""
      } else if (char === '"' || char === "'" || char === "`") {
        quote = char
      } else if (char === "{") {
        depth += 1
      } else if (char === "}") {
        depth -= 1
      } else if (char === ">" && depth === 0) {
        tags.push(source.slice(match.index, index + 1))
        break
      }
    }
  }
  return tags
}

test("toast CSS: token colors, a soft fill only for warning and error, ratio line heights, a ring outline, hidden on paper", () => {
  const css = read("src/app/globals.css")
  // The phase 1 font rule stays its own block (visual-foundation-guards pins it as it is).
  assert.match(css, /:root \[data-sonner-toaster\] \{\s*font-family: var\(--font-sans\);\s*\}/)
  assert.match(css, /:root \[data-sonner-toaster\]\[data-sonner-theme="light"\] \{\s*--normal-bg: var\(--card\);\s*--normal-border: var\(--border\);\s*--normal-text: var\(--foreground\);\s*--border-radius: 12px;\s*\}/)
  assert.match(css, /:root \[data-sonner-toast\]\[data-styled="true"\] \{\s*padding: 12px 40px 12px 16px;\s*gap: 10px;\s*align-items: flex-start;\s*font-size: 14px;\s*line-height: calc\(22 \/ 14\);\s*box-shadow: var\(--shadow-overlay\);\s*\}/)
  assert.match(css, /:root \[data-sonner-toast\]\[data-styled="true"\] \[data-description\] \{\s*color: var\(--muted-foreground\);\s*font-size: 13px;\s*line-height: calc\(20 \/ 13\);\s*\}/)
  assert.match(css, /:root \[data-sonner-toast\]\[data-type="warning"\] \{\s*--normal-bg: var\(--warning-soft\);\s*--normal-border: var\(--warning-border\);\s*\}/)
  assert.match(css, /:root \[data-sonner-toast\]\[data-type="error"\] \{\s*--normal-bg: var\(--danger-soft\);\s*--normal-border: var\(--danger-border\);\s*\}/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(css, /\[data-type="(?:success|info)"\] \{[^}]*--normal-/, "success and info stay white: the tone lives in the icon")
  for (const [type, token] of [["success", "success"], ["info", "info"], ["warning", "warning"], ["error", "danger"]]) {
    assert.match(css, new RegExp(`:root \\[data-sonner-toast\\]\\[data-type="${type}"\\] \\[data-icon\\] \\{\\s*color: var\\(--${token}\\);\\s*\\}`), `${type} icon color`)
  }
  assert.match(css, /:root \[data-sonner-toast\]:focus-visible \{\s*outline: 2px solid var\(--ring\);\s*outline-offset: 2px;\s*\}/)
  assert.match(css, /:root \[data-sonner-toast\]\[data-styled="true"\] \[data-close-button\] \{[^}]*width: 24px;\s*height: 24px;[^}]*border-radius: 6px;[^}]*\}/)
  assert.match(css, /:root \[data-sonner-toast\]\[data-styled="true"\]:hover \[data-close-button\]:hover \{/, "0,6,0 beats sonner's 0,5,0 hover rule")
  assert.match(css, /:root \[data-sonner-toast\]\[data-styled="true"\] \[data-close-button\]:focus-visible \{\s*box-shadow: none;\s*outline: 2px solid var\(--ring\);\s*outline-offset: 1px;\s*\}/)
  assert.match(css, /@media print \{\s*:root \[data-sonner-toaster\] \{\s*display: none;\s*\}\s*\}/)

  const rules = css.match(/[^{}]*data-sonner[^{}]*\{[^{}]*\}/g) ?? []
  assert.ok(rules.length >= 15, `expected the toast rules, found ${rules.length}`)
  const text = rules.join("\n")
  assert.doesNotMatch(text, /!important/)
  assert.doesNotMatch(text, /line-height: \d+px/, "line heights are ratios")
  assert.doesNotMatch(text, /#[0-9a-f]{3,8}\b|rgba?\(/i, "toast colors come from the :root tokens")
  for (const rule of rules) {
    const at = css.indexOf(rule)
    const layerOpen = css.lastIndexOf("@layer", at)
    assert.ok(layerOpen === -1 || css.indexOf("\n}", layerOpen) < at, `outside every @layer, or sonner's unlayered CSS wins: ${rule.slice(0, rule.indexOf("{")).trim().split("\n").pop()}`)
  }
})

test("busy comes from client state, and a busy Button never also gets disabled", () => {
  const offenders: string[] = []
  let busyButtons = 0
  for (const file of sources.filter((entry) => entry.path.endsWith(".tsx"))) {
    for (const tag of buttonOpeningTags(file.source)) {
      if (!/\sbusy[=\s/>]/.test(tag)) continue
      busyButtons += 1
      if (!isClientModule(file.source)) offenders.push(`${file.path}: busy in a server file (its click guard cannot cross to the client)`)
      if (/\sdisabled[=\s/>]/.test(tag)) offenders.push(`${file.path}: busy with disabled drops keyboard focus`)
    }
  }
  assert.ok(busyButtons >= 1, "the guard must see at least one busy Button (DataFreshnessBanner)")
  assert.deepEqual(offenders, [])
})
```

How the source-reading rules work:
- **The `stale` slice** starts at the last `  return (` in the file, which is the stale branch. The fresh branch's `    return (` comes earlier. The fresh branch keeps its own `disabled={refreshing}` and `animate-spin` (spec 7.5: the fresh state is not touched).
- **The toast-rule regex** takes each rule together with the comment text in front of it. So the comments in the Step 5 block must not contain braces, `!important`, a hex color or `rgb(`.
- **The busy guard was checked on a probe file.** A server `.tsx` with `<Button onClick={() => { if (x > 1) return }} busy={x}\n disabled={x}>` gave exactly 2 offenders. The `=>` and `>` inside braces do not end the tag. `aria-busy=` and `data-busy=` in `button.tsx` sit on `<Comp`, not on `<Button`, and `\sbusy` does not match them anyway.

```ts
import { CircleCheck, Info, OctagonAlert, TriangleAlert } from "lucide-react"

// One icon per tone, each a different shape, so the tone reads without color: toasts (error uses danger), Notice and
// ActionEmptyState. Older code imports the aliases AlertTriangle and CheckCircle2; they are the same icons and can stay.
export const toneIcons = {
  warning: TriangleAlert,
  danger: OctagonAlert,
  info: Info,
  success: CircleCheck,
} as const
```

lucide-react 1.14.0 exports all four. `TriangleAlert === AlertTriangle` and `CircleCheck === CheckCircle2`, so the 38 files that use the old alias names need no sweep.

- [ ] **Step 4: Create `src/components/ui/app-toaster.tsx` and render it from the locale layout**

```tsx
"use client"

import type { CSSProperties } from "react"
import { X } from "lucide-react"
import { useTranslations } from "next-intl"
import { Toaster } from "sonner"
import { toneIcons } from "@/components/ui/tone-icons"

const { success: SuccessIcon, info: InfoIcon, warning: WarningIcon, danger: DangerIcon } = toneIcons

// Each type has its own icon shape, so the tone reads without color. globals.css ("Quiet toasts") colors the icon and
// centers it on the first 22px line; the text stays foreground for every type.
const toastIcons = {
  success: <SuccessIcon className="size-4" />,
  info: <InfoIcon className="size-4" />,
  warning: <WarningIcon className="size-4" />,
  error: <DangerIcon className="size-4" />,
  close: <X className="size-4" />,
}

// sonner writes --width (356px) inline on its list, so the wider width that Thai text needs must come through style.
const toasterStyle = { "--width": "380px" } as CSSProperties

/**
 * The app's one toast region, rendered by src/app/[locale]/layout.tsx. No call site changes: toast.success / error /
 * warning keep working. Toasts sit 8px under the 64px topbar, stay 6 seconds (Thai sentences run longer; sonner pauses
 * on hover, focus and hidden tabs) and have a close button, because Escape only collapses the stack. The region and
 * the close button take their names from messages (notifications.title, common.close).
 */
export function AppToaster() {
  const tCommon = useTranslations("common")
  const tNotifications = useTranslations("notifications")

  return (
    <Toaster
      position="top-right"
      offset={{ top: 72, right: 16 }}
      mobileOffset={{ top: 72 }}
      duration={6000}
      closeButton
      containerAriaLabel={tNotifications("title")}
      toastOptions={{ closeButtonAriaLabel: tCommon("close") }}
      icons={toastIcons}
      style={toasterStyle}
    />
  )
}
```

These props were checked against `node_modules/sonner` 2.0.7 (`ToasterProps` in `dist/index.d.ts`, render code in `dist/index.mjs`):
- **Labels:** `containerAriaLabel` becomes the `<section>` label. Sonner appends the hotkey, so screen readers hear "การแจ้งเตือน alt+T". `toastOptions.closeButtonAriaLabel` replaces "Close toast".
- **Width:** the `<ol>` style is `{ '--width': '356px', …, ...style, ...offsets }`, so `style` overrides the width.
- **Offsets:** sides left out of `offset` default to 24px, and sides left out of `mobileOffset` to 16px.
- **Icons:** `icons.close` replaces sonner's X.
- **Defaults kept:** `theme`, `richColors`, `visibleToasts`, `expand` and `hotkey` are not passed (light theme, 3 toasts, collapsed stack, Alt+T).
- **Types:** `as CSSProperties` is needed because `--width` is not a known CSS property. `tsc` passes.

`useTranslations` works here because `NextIntlClientProvider` in the layout passes every message (`getMessages()`). The repo has no typed-messages augmentation, and other `ui/` client components use the same `useTranslations("common")` pattern.
