  font-family: var(--font-sans);
}

/* Quiet toasts (AppToaster). Kept apart from the font rule above, which a test pins as it is. Sonner injects its CSS
   unlayered at runtime, after this file, so every selector starts with :root to weigh more; nothing is marked important.
   Colors come from the token :root block and line heights are ratios. The text stays foreground for every type and the
   tone lives in the icon; only warning and error get a soft fill. The 380px width is set by the style prop of
   AppToaster, because sonner writes its width inline. Its reduced-motion rule already stops every toast animation. */
:root [data-sonner-toaster][data-sonner-theme="light"] {
  --normal-bg: var(--card);
  --normal-border: var(--border);
  --normal-text: var(--foreground);
  --border-radius: 12px;
}

/* Right padding 40px leaves room for the close button. */
:root [data-sonner-toast][data-styled="true"] {
  padding: 12px 40px 12px 16px;
  gap: 10px;
  align-items: flex-start;
  font-size: 14px;
  line-height: calc(22 / 14);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-overlay);
}

:root [data-sonner-toast][data-styled="true"] [data-title] {
  font-weight: 500;
  line-height: calc(22 / 14);
}

/* The original text, under the Thai message, as a small second line. */
:root [data-sonner-toast][data-styled="true"] [data-description] {
  color: var(--muted-foreground);
  font-size: 13px;
  line-height: calc(20 / 13);
}

/* A 16px icon centered on the first 22px line. */
:root [data-sonner-toast][data-styled="true"] [data-icon] {
  width: 16px;
  height: 22px;
  margin: 0;
}

:root [data-sonner-toast][data-styled="true"] [data-icon] svg {
  margin: 0;
}

:root [data-sonner-toast][data-type="success"] [data-icon] {
  color: var(--success);
}

:root [data-sonner-toast][data-type="info"] [data-icon] {
  color: var(--info);
}

:root [data-sonner-toast][data-type="warning"] {
  --normal-bg: var(--warning-soft);
  --normal-border: var(--warning-border);
}

:root [data-sonner-toast][data-type="warning"] [data-icon] {
  color: var(--warning);
}

:root [data-sonner-toast][data-type="error"] {
  --normal-bg: var(--danger-soft);
  --normal-border: var(--danger-border);
}

:root [data-sonner-toast][data-type="error"] [data-icon] {
  color: var(--danger);
}

/* An outline, not a box-shadow ring, so focus stays visible in forced-colors mode (same as the base focus rule). */
:root [data-sonner-toast]:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--ring);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

/* 24px close button inside the top-right corner (6px radius, the control radius). */
:root [data-sonner-toast][data-styled="true"] [data-close-button] {
  left: auto;
  right: 8px;
  top: 8px;
  transform: none;
  width: 24px;
  height: 24px;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 6px;
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--muted-foreground);
}

/* Specificity 0,6,0 beats sonner's 0,5,0 hover rule. */
:root [data-sonner-toast][data-styled="true"]:hover [data-close-button]:hover {
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--foreground);
}

:root [data-sonner-toast][data-styled="true"] [data-close-button]:focus-visible {
  box-shadow: none;
  outline: 2px solid var(--ring);
  outline-offset: 1px;
}

@media print {
  :root [data-sonner-toaster] {
    display: none;
  }
}
```

Keep the blank line that already follows the font rule; the "Base surface rules" `@layer base` block still comes after it. Notes:
- **Why each rule wins:** each one outweighs its sonner rule by one pseudo-class (`:root`).
  - `[data-sonner-toast][data-styled='true']` 0,2,0 → ours 0,3,0
  - the light theme block 0,2,0 → 0,3,0
  - `[data-icon] svg` 0,3,1 → 0,4,1
  - close button 0,3,0 → 0,4,0, and its `:focus-visible` 0,4,0 → 0,5,0
  - close hover 0,5,0 → 0,6,0
  - Sonner's `[data-sonner-toast]` sets `outline: none` (0,1,0) and comes later in the page. Our `:focus-visible` rule (0,3,0) still wins, which the layered base focus rule cannot.
  - Sonner's own focus ring (`[data-sonner-toast]:focus-visible` box-shadow, 0,2,0) loses to our `box-shadow: var(--shadow-overlay)` (0,3,0).
- **Tone fills:** `--normal-bg`/`--normal-border` are set on the toast `<li>` itself for warning and error. Sonner reads them there (`background: var(--normal-bg)`), and the close button now has a transparent background.
- **Every rule stays outside `@layer`:** unlayered sonner CSS would beat any layered rule, whatever the specificity. The new test checks this.
- **Compiled with Tailwind 4.2.4** (`@tailwindcss/postcss`, on a copy with Task 1's `globals.css`): the 18 `data-sonner` rules come through unchanged, and `--shadow-overlay` is emitted in `:root`.
- Contrast for every pair (spec 7.2) was computed in Task 1's contrast test: foreground on warning-soft/danger-soft 15.50/15.08, muted-foreground 7.63/7.42, icons on their own soft fill 5.50/5.83.

- [ ] **Step 6: Replace `src/components/ui/action-empty-state.tsx`**

Replace the whole file with:

```tsx
import Link from "next/link"
import type React from "react"
import { Inbox, ShieldAlert } from "lucide-react"
import { buttonVariants } from "@/components/ui/button-variants"
import { toneIcons } from "@/components/ui/tone-icons"

type ActionEmptyStateTone = "empty" | "error" | "permission"

type ActionEmptyStateProps = {
  icon?: React.ReactNode
  title: string
  description?: string
  actionHref?: string
  actionLabel?: string
  /** "secondary" draws the outlined button (for example "clear filters"), keeping the primary color for the page's main task. */
  actionVariant?: "primary" | "secondary"
  action?: React.ReactNode
  details?: React.ReactNode
  tone?: ActionEmptyStateTone
}

const DangerIcon = toneIcons.danger

// No frame and no fill, so the empty state never becomes a box inside its panel or table. A 24px icon carries the tone:
// faint for empty, red for an error, orange for a missing permission.
const toneStyles: Record<ActionEmptyStateTone, { icon: React.ReactNode; iconClassName: string }> = {
  empty: { icon: <Inbox />, iconClassName: "text-faint-foreground" },
  error: { icon: <DangerIcon />, iconClassName: "text-danger" },
  permission: { icon: <ShieldAlert />, iconClassName: "text-warning" },
}

export function ActionEmptyState({ icon, title, description, actionHref, actionLabel, actionVariant = "primary", action, details, tone = "empty" }: ActionEmptyStateProps) {
  const stateIcon = icon ?? toneStyles[tone].icon
  const actionNode = action ?? (actionHref && actionLabel ? (
    <Link href={actionHref} className={buttonVariants({ variant: actionVariant === "secondary" ? "outline" : "default", size: "md" })}>
      {actionLabel}
    </Link>
  ) : null)

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center px-4 py-10 text-center">
      <div aria-hidden="true" className={`mb-3 flex [&>svg]:size-6 ${toneStyles[tone].iconClassName}`}>{stateIcon}</div>
      <div className="text-base font-semibold text-foreground text-balance">{title}</div>
      {description ? <p className="mt-1 max-w-md text-sm text-muted-foreground text-pretty">{description}</p> : null}
      {details ? <div className="mt-4 max-w-md">{details}</div> : null}
      {actionNode ? <div className="mt-4">{actionNode}</div> : null}
    </div>
  )
}
```

What stays and what changes:
- **Pinned strings stay as they were:** `const actionNode = action ??`, `<div className="mt-4">{actionNode}</div>` and the `details` wrapper. `tests/system-state-views.test.ts:10-11` reads the first two.
- **No `"use client"`:** server pages render this component, and `buttonVariants` and `toneIcons` have no directive.
- **Icons:** call sites that pass their own icon use `className="h-6 w-6"` (19 lines in `src`), the same 24px that `[&>svg]:size-6` sets. Lucide icons render `aria-hidden="true"` by default.
- **Link height:** the link is 44px below `md` and 36px from `md` up, the same 36px as before. It was 36px from `sm` up; at 640-767px it is now 44px (spec 7.11).
- **Link colors:** primary `bg-primary text-primary-foreground`, or outline with the `#C3CDDA` border.
- **The 5 sites inside `<td>`:** they keep the td's padding (`px-4 py-6` or `py-8`) on top of `py-10` until 2B sets those cells to `p-0`. The 5 are `audit/findings/page.tsx:401`, `audit/rounds/[id]/page.tsx:711`, `audit/rounds/[id]/pending/page.tsx:201`, `disposal/page.tsx:365` and `asset-register-table.tsx:601`.

- [ ] **Step 7: Create `src/components/ui/empty-note.tsx`**

```tsx
import type React from "react"
import { Check } from "lucide-react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

type EmptyNoteProps = React.ComponentProps<"p"> & {
  /** "clear" means nothing is left to do and shows a check in the text color (not green). Default "empty". */
  tone?: "empty" | "clear"
  /** Replaces the default icon ("empty" has none). */
  icon?: React.ReactNode
  /** Adds the mockup padding (16 x 20) when the wrapper has none: a table cell with p-0, a row list. Not inside ContentPanel. */
  padded?: boolean
}

/**
 * One-line empty note (mockup `.empty`): 13/20 faint text with no frame and no fill, for lists, panel sections and
 * empty table rows. Use ActionEmptyState when there is a title, a description or a button. No default live region:
 * when a filter changes the note live, the call site adds a status role.
 */
export function EmptyNote({ tone = "empty", icon, padded = false, className, children, ...props }: EmptyNoteProps) {
  const noteIcon = icon ?? (tone === "clear" ? <Check aria-hidden="true" /> : null)

  return (
    <p
      className={cn(
        "flex items-center gap-2 text-meta text-faint-foreground text-pretty [&>svg]:size-4 [&>svg]:shrink-0",
        padded && "px-5 py-4",
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {noteIcon}
      {children}
    </p>
  )
}
```

With Task 1's `cn()`, the class string comes back whole, `px-5 py-4` included (checked). The test bans the words `border` and `role=` anywhere in this file, comments included.

- [ ] **Step 8: Create `src/components/ui/notice.tsx`**

```tsx
import type React from "react"
import type { LucideIcon } from "lucide-react"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { toneIcons } from "@/components/ui/tone-icons"

export type NoticeTone = "warning" | "danger" | "info" | "neutral" | "success"

type NoticeProps = Omit<React.ComponentProps<"div">, "title"> & {
  /** Required on purpose, so no notice turns orange by default. */
  tone: NoticeTone
  title?: React.ReactNode
  /** A Button with size="md", or a link, on its own row under the text. */
  action?: React.ReactNode
  /** Replaces the tone's icon. */
  icon?: React.ReactNode
}

// warning and danger are boxes, for something the user has to deal with now; they are the only notices that sit inside
// a panel. info, neutral and success sit inline, with no frame, fill or padding. The text stays foreground or
// muted-foreground and the tone lives in the icon. Live-region attributes are the call site's job (an alert for an
// error after a command, a status for a background result); tests read them from the call site.
const noticeTones: Record<NoticeTone, { fill: string | null; Icon: LucideIcon; iconColor: string }> = {
  warning: { fill: "border-warning-border bg-warning-soft", Icon: toneIcons.warning, iconColor: "text-warning" },
  danger: { fill: "border-danger-border bg-danger-soft", Icon: toneIcons.danger, iconColor: "text-danger" },
  info: { fill: null, Icon: toneIcons.info, iconColor: "text-info" },
  neutral: { fill: null, Icon: toneIcons.info, iconColor: "text-muted-foreground" },
  success: { fill: null, Icon: toneIcons.success, iconColor: "text-success" },
}

export function Notice({ tone, title, action, icon, className, children, ...props }: NoticeProps) {
  const { fill, Icon, iconColor } = noticeTones[tone]
  const boxed = fill !== null
  // In a box with a title, the body is the small second line; without a title, the body is the message itself.
  const bodyClassName = boxed ? (title ? "text-meta text-muted-foreground" : "text-foreground") : "text-muted-foreground"

  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        boxed
          ? "grid grid-cols-[20px_minmax(0,1fr)] gap-x-2.5 rounded-md border px-4 py-3 text-sm text-foreground text-pretty"
          : "flex items-start gap-2 text-sm text-pretty",
        fill,
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {/* 22px tall: the icon centers on the first 22px line of text-sm. */}
      <span aria-hidden="true" className={cn("flex h-5.5 shrink-0 items-center", iconColor)}>
        {icon ?? <Icon className={boxed ? "size-4.5" : "size-4"} />}
      </span>
      <div className={cn("grid min-w-0 flex-1 justify-items-start", boxed ? "gap-2" : "gap-1")}>
        {title ? <div className="font-semibold text-foreground">{title}</div> : null}
        {children ? <div className={bodyClassName}>{children}</div> : null}
        {action ? <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">{action}</div> : null}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

Notes:
- The box follows the mockup's `.notice` (`base.css:412-413`): a 20px icon column, a 10px gap, 12/16 padding, 8px corners and an 18px icon centered on the first line.
- The test bans `text-faint-foreground`, `/NN` tints, `role=` and `aria-live` anywhere in this file, comments included.
- Task 1's class-string guard (no status soft fill together with `text-faint-foreground` in one string) passes, and `cn()` keeps every class:
  - `cn(box, "border-warning-border bg-warning-soft", "mb-3")` returns the three strings joined, with nothing dropped (checked).
  - Tailwind 4.2.4 emits CSS for `size-4.5`, `h-5.5`, `grid-cols-[20px_minmax(0,1fr)]`, `gap-x-2.5` and `justify-items-start` (checked by compiling).

| register | hover a row, then scroll sideways: sticky cells stay solid · in the ⋯ menu, arrow keys reach an unavailable item, and Enter on it keeps the menu open · column picker (`.cbox`) · sort menu (radio) · export menu · status column 132px and condition column 96px do not wrap · mobile list with 3 badges per row |
| topbar and popover | language menu (radio · each language named in its own language · no flags · 240 wide) · user menu · notification count `font-semibold` · notifications popover with 12px corners |
| global search | selected row is `primary-soft` · icons without boxes · footer shows `Kbd` in 3 groups |
| forms with `SearchableSelect` (asset new, checkout) | white trigger, 8px corners, `ChevronDown` · halo when open · the search box sits inside the panel · check mark at the end · hand-written fields sit next to shared ones (I22) |
| audit scan (375) | `xl` search field is 48px tall with 16px text at every width · room-list segmented control · whether a toast covers the sticky search |
| forms with `ScannerTextInput` | camera button as tall as the field (44 / 40) · camera select |
| `FileDropzone` | Tab shows a focus ring · hover · disabled does not fade |
| confirm dialogs (`confirm-text`, `operation-review`, delete via `useConfirm`) | 8px buttons · footer on the `background` fill · disabled or busy buttons are grey with faint text |
| empty states | access-denied · error boundary · empty disposal list (inside a `td`) · filtered supplier list with no match · empty review list |
| mismatched list with stale data | warning `Notice` and the `busy` refresh button |
| toasts | success · error with a description (`apiError.toast`) · validation error (`asset-form`) · warning (`scanner-text-input`), at 1440 and 375 · no toast covers the topbar · the title does not run under the close button |
| dashboard, work center, login | link-card hover · expanded panels have no nested ring · placeholder |
| print | A4 documents still print on 1 page (reset `emulateMedia({ media: null })` before `page.pdf`) · thermal label unchanged · no toast in print |

  - Do the check at 1440, 375, 700 and 768, in Thai, and do one pass in English.
  - Zoom: 100% and 200%.
  - Thai text in the 13px meta size and the 22px badges must not clip stacked vowels or tone marks (Review Focus 4).

- [ ] **Step 4: Keyboard and screen reader.**
  - Keyboard:
    - Tab through every button: each shows the focus ring.
    - Arrow through the menus.
    - Alt+T, then Tab, reaches the toast and its close button.
    - A `busy` button keeps focus (Review Focus 2).
    - The crumbs links work.
  - With NVDA:
    - an unavailable menu item reads its reason and "ใช้ไม่ได้" (Review Focus 5)
    - the language menu reads as `menuitemradio`, and "English" is read with an English voice
    - the toast region reads "การแจ้งเตือน" and its close button reads "ปิด"
    - the crumbs read "เส้นทางหน้า" and announce the current page
    - `SearchableSelect` reads the selected value

- [ ] **Step 5: axe.**

    - the dashboard
    - the register with a menu held open
    - asset new
    - settings
    - a page with a warning or error toast held open
  - Expected: 0 violations. The pre-existing "เอกสารล่าสุด" span is a known exception; note it separately.

- [ ] **Step 6: Forced colors and reduced motion.**
  - Forced colors (Playwright `browser_emulate_media` with `forcedColors: "active"`):

    - `<select>` shows the system arrow
    - the badge markers still show

  - With `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`:

### Task 13: Design documentation (repo docs only; the controller does the wiki in Task 14)

Spec §14 lists every place to change; spec I19 puts all of it in this one task, so no earlier task touched these five files. §14's line numbers are for `081d538`, and the five docs have not changed since (`git diff --stat 081d538 HEAD -- DESIGN.md .impeccable/design.json docs/14_UI_UX_DESIGN_SYSTEM.md DEVELOPER_HANDOFF.md docs/99_CHANGELOG.md` prints nothing at `41eff3f`). Match on the quoted strings, not on line numbers.

The docs describe what shipped, with the rulings applied:
- **I15 (radius):** buttons `md`/`lg`/`default`/`icon` and every field use `rounded-md` 8px; `sm`/`icon-sm` buttons, segmented buttons, chips and status badges use `rounded-control` 6px; menus, popovers, the command panel, toasts, dialogs and panels use `rounded-lg` 12px. This replaces the `{rounded.control}` and 6px wording for `button-primary`, `button-secondary`, `input` and `ds-btn-*` in spec §4.12 and §6.11. Menu items and the in-menu search box keep 6px from spec §5.3 and §5.8.
- **I14:** faint text is allowed on `primary-soft` (hovered or selected rows and menu items, 4.99:1) and banned only on the 4 status soft fills.
- **Rulings made in this task:**
  - DESIGN.md's frontmatter follows Stitch's schema: component tokens may use only 8 props (`backgroundColor`, `textColor`, `typography`, `rounded`, `padding`, `size`, `height`, `width`; `.agents/skills/impeccable/reference/document.md:47`). So the outline button's #C3CDDA border (spec §4.12 "secondary เพิ่มสีขอบ") goes into the `.impeccable/design.json` CSS and the §5 prose, not into the YAML. Spec §14's "ขนาด sm ใช้ `rounded.control`" becomes a new `button-small` component.
  - `button-danger` is added beside it, one entry per variant, as the impeccable reference asks.
  - No `typography` ref is used on badges or `button-small`: both are `text-meta` at weight 500, while the `meta` token is 400.

**Files:**
- Modify: `DESIGN.md` (CRLF in the working tree): frontmatter `colors`, `typography`, `rounded`, `components`; §1 (line 142), §2 (lines 164, 176-181, 189, 193-195, 201), §3 (lines 217, 223-224, 229-230, 236), §4 (lines 252, 257, 261), §5 (lines 267, 271-274, 281, 285-287, 296, 300-301, 303, 308-311, 321, 326, 340), §6 (lines 356, 375), §7 (lines 383, 392)
- Modify: `.impeccable/design.json` (CRLF): through a one-off script kept in your temp folder
- Modify: `docs/14_UI_UX_DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` (CRLF): new section before line 50, new registry row after line 66
- Modify: `DEVELOPER_HANDOFF.md` (CRLF): line 245 (shadcn notes), new section before `## Open Go-Live Decisions`
- Modify: `docs/99_CHANGELOG.md` (CRLF): new `## 2026-10-11` heading and entry above `## 2026-10-10`
- Not in this task: verify numbers (Task 14 adds them to the CHANGELOG entry and the handoff section), the wiki (Task 14), `docs/07_UAT_CHECKLIST.md` (spec §14 does not list it).

All five files are `i/lf w/crlf` (`git ls-files --eol`). Edit with exact-string replacement and keep CRLF. The check script in Step 1 fails on any line that ends in a bare LF.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Tasks 1-12 committed. Step 1 confirms in the code that every name and value this task writes shipped.
  - Task 12's report (the controller pastes it into your brief): what was checked on the app, every problem found, and the commit that fixed it.
  - The plan file `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-ui-phase2a.md`.
- Produces:
  - the `## 2026-10-11` → `### รากฐานคอมโพเนนต์กลาง (UI รอบ 4 เฟส 2A)` entry in `docs/99_CHANGELOG.md`
  - the `## UI Round 4 Phase 2A Shared Components (2026-10-11)` section in `DEVELOPER_HANDOFF.md`
  - Task 14 adds one verify bullet to each of them.
  - Nothing that code uses.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the docs check and confirm it fails only on the docs**

Run: `git ls-files docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-ui-phase2a.md`
Expected: it prints `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-ui-phase2a.md`. If it prints nothing, find the 2A plan with `git ls-files docs/superpowers/plans | grep 2026-10-11`. Then use that path wherever this task writes `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-ui-phase2a.md` (3 places: the registry row in docs/14, which links it as `superpowers/plans/…`, the handoff section and the changelog entry). If that prints nothing either, stop and report it: the three docs link the plan, so the controller has to commit it first.

Save this script in your temp folder as `check-docs-2a.mjs` and never commit it. Part 1 checks that the code shipped under the names the docs use. Part 2 checks the docs. `js-yaml` 4.3.2 is already in `node_modules` (a dependency of `@eslint/eslintrc`).

```js
// Task 13: check that the phase 2A code shipped under the names the docs use, and that the 5 docs describe it.
// Run from the repo root: node <your temp folder>/check-docs-2a.mjs [--docs-only]   (never commit this file)
// Exit 0 only when every check passes. Prints one line per failed check.
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs"
import { createRequire } from "node:module"
import { join } from "node:path"

const root = process.env.DOCS_ROOT ?? process.cwd()
const codeRoot = process.env.CODE_ROOT ?? process.cwd()
const docsOnly = process.argv.includes("--docs-only")
const read = (base, path) => (existsSync(join(base, path)) ? readFileSync(join(base, path), "utf8") : "")
const failures = { shipped: [], docs: [] }
let counts = { shipped: 0, docs: 0 }
const check = (group, ok, label) => {
  counts[group]++
  if (!ok) failures[group].push(label)
}
const has = (group, base, path, needle) => {
  const text = read(base, path)
  check(group, typeof needle === "string" ? text.includes(needle) : needle.test(text), `${path} has ${needle}`)
}
const lacks = (group, base, path, needle) => {
  const text = read(base, path)
  check(group, text !== "" && (typeof needle === "string" ? !text.includes(needle) : !needle.test(text)), `${path} no longer has ${needle}`)
}

// 1. Shipped code (Tasks 1-11). If any of these fail, stop: the docs below would describe something that did not ship.
if (!docsOnly) {
  const S = "shipped"
  const c = codeRoot
  for (const token of ["--faint-foreground: #56677F;", "--control-border: #C3CDDA;", "--border-soft: #E7EBF1;", "--row-hover: #F3F6FB;", "--neutral-ink: #2A4468;", "--radius-control: 6px;", "--text-meta: 0.8125rem;", "--shadow-field-focus: 0 0 0 4px rgb(16 133 141 / 0.16);", "--shadow-field-error: 0 0 0 4px rgb(179 38 30 / 0.14);", "@utility select-chevron", "user-select: all;", "--normal-bg: var(--card)"]) has(S, c, "src/app/globals.css", token)
  has(S, c, "src/lib/utils.ts", "extendTailwindMerge")
  for (const needle of ["rounded-control", "md:h-8", "md:h-9", "md:h-10", "rounded-md", "border-danger-border bg-surface text-danger", "active:bg-muted", "export const linkVariants"]) has(S, c, "src/components/ui/button-variants.ts", needle)
  lacks(S, c, "src/components/ui/button-variants.ts", /opacity|variant[^\n]*secondary:|\blink: "/)
  has(S, c, "src/components/ui/button.tsx", "busy?: boolean")
  has(S, c, "src/components/ui/button.tsx", "aria-busy")
  lacks(S, c, "src/lib/design-system.ts", "getTouchIconButtonClasses")
  has(S, c, "src/lib/design-system.ts", "getFieldControlClasses(options")
  for (const needle of ["export const fieldControlVariants", "border-input", "bg-surface", "rounded-md", "shadow-field-focus", "shadow-field-error", "md:h-9", "h-12", "checkboxVariants"]) has(S, c, "src/components/ui/field-variants.ts", needle)
  has(S, c, "src/components/ui/native-select.tsx", "select-chevron")
  has(S, c, "src/components/ui/field.tsx", 'role="alert"')
  has(S, c, "src/components/ui/search-field.tsx", "fieldSize")
  for (const needle of ["rounded-lg", "min-w-60", "focus:bg-primary-soft", "rounded-control", "md:min-h-9", "unavailable", "DropdownMenuItemDescription"]) has(S, c, "src/components/ui/dropdown-menu.tsx", needle)
  has(S, c, "src/components/ui/kbd.tsx", "font-sans")
  for (const needle of ["h-5.5", "text-meta", "rounded-control", "bg-neutral-ink"]) has(S, c, "src/components/ui/badge-variants.ts", needle)
  for (const needle of ['aria-current="page"', "text-meta text-faint-foreground"]) has(S, c, "src/components/ui/breadcrumbs.tsx", needle)
  has(S, c, "src/components/ui/nil.tsx", 't("noValue")')
  has(S, c, "src/lib/display-format.ts", 'new Intl.NumberFormat("th-TH"')
  for (const needle of ["closeButton", "duration={6000}", "top: 72", '"--width": "380px"']) has(S, c, "src/components/ui/app-toaster.tsx", needle)
  has(S, c, "src/components/ui/notice.tsx", "border-warning-border bg-warning-soft")
  has(S, c, "src/components/ui/empty-note.tsx", "text-meta text-faint-foreground")
  has(S, c, "src/components/ui/action-empty-state.tsx", "actionVariant")
  has(S, c, "src/components/ui/data-freshness-banner.tsx", "busy={refreshing}")
  const th = JSON.parse(read(c, "messages/th.json") || "{}")
  const at = (key) => key.split(".").reduce((node, part) => node?.[part], th)
  const thai = { "nav.openMenu": "เปิดเมนู", "nav.toggleSidebar": "ย่อหรือขยายเมนูข้าง", "nav.languageMenu": "เปลี่ยนภาษา ({code})", "nav.userMenu": "เมนูผู้ใช้: {name}", "nav.closeMenu": "ปิดเมนู", "common.breadcrumb": "เส้นทางหน้า", "common.noValue": "ไม่มีข้อมูล", "common.baht": "บาท" }
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(thai)) check(S, at(key) === value, `messages/th.json ${key} = ${value}`)
}

// 2. Docs (this task).
const D = "docs"
const r = root
const design = read(r, "DESIGN.md").replace(/\r\n/g, "\n")
const front = design.match(/^---\n([\s\S]*?)\n---\n/)?.[1] ?? ""
let yaml = {}
try {
  yaml = createRequire(join(codeRoot, "package.json"))("js-yaml").load(front) ?? {}
} catch (error) {
  check(D, false, `DESIGN.md frontmatter parses (${error.message})`)
}
const colors = { "faint-foreground": "#56677F", "neutral-ink": "#2A4468", "row-hover": "#F3F6FB", "border-soft": "#E7EBF1", "control-border": "#C3CDDA" }
for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(colors)) check(D, yaml.colors?.[name] === value, `DESIGN.md colors.${name} = ${value}`)
const type = { meta: ["0.8125rem", 400, 1.5385, "0"], metric: ["1.75rem", 500, 1.1429, "-0.02em"], count: ["1.25rem", 600, 1.2, "-0.01em"] }
for (const [name, [size, weight, line, spacing]] of Object.entries(type)) {
  const t = yaml.typography?.[name]
  check(D, t?.fontSize === size && t?.fontWeight === weight && t?.lineHeight === line && t?.letterSpacing === spacing, `DESIGN.md typography.${name} = ${size} ${weight} ${line} ${spacing}`)
}
check(D, yaml.rounded?.control === "6px" && yaml.rounded?.md === "8px", "DESIGN.md rounded.control = 6px, md = 8px")
const comp = (name) => yaml.components?.[name] ?? {}
check(D, comp("button-primary").rounded === "{rounded.md}" && comp("button-primary").height === "40px", "DESIGN.md button-primary: rounded.md, 40px")
check(D, comp("button-small").rounded === "{rounded.control}" && comp("button-small").height === "32px", "DESIGN.md button-small: rounded.control, 32px")
check(D, comp("button-danger").textColor === "{colors.danger}", "DESIGN.md button-danger: danger text")
check(D, comp("input").backgroundColor === "{colors.surface}" && comp("input").rounded === "{rounded.md}", "DESIGN.md input: surface fill, rounded.md")
check(D, comp("badge-status").rounded === "{rounded.control}" && comp("badge-status").height === "22px", "DESIGN.md badge-status: rounded.control, 22px")
for (const needle of ["### Feedback: Toasts, Notices and Empty States", "### Empty Values and Numbers", "**Faint** (`--faint-foreground` #56677F", "spec ruling I14", "`rounded-control`) only for small controls", "`linkVariants({ size })`", "**Busy:**", "`<DropdownMenuItem unavailable>`", "`fieldSize`", "316 fields in 70 files", "about 79 code call sites", "`select-chevron`", "WCAG 2.5.8", "native-select", "until phase 2D", "`R2 only floating layers, camera controls and paper previews cast a shadow`", "`--radius-control: 6px`", "**Breadcrumbs**"]) check(D, design.includes(needle), `DESIGN.md has ${needle}`)
for (const needle of ["touch icon", "About 384", "119 asset-tag", "Some dashboard and admin page-level cards still carry", "Disabled states reduce opacity", "Soft System Background fill", "until phase 2, info", "Font sizes are Tailwind's defaults. Line"]) check(D, !design.includes(needle), `DESIGN.md no longer has ${needle}`)

let sidecar = {}
try {
  sidecar = JSON.parse(read(r, ".impeccable/design.json"))
} catch (error) {
  check(D, false, `.impeccable/design.json parses (${error.message})`)
}
check(D, sidecar.generatedAt === "2026-10-11T00:00:00+07:00", "design.json generatedAt 2026-10-11")
for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(colors)) check(D, sidecar.extensions?.colorMeta?.[name]?.canonical === value && sidecar.extensions.colorMeta[name].tonalRamp?.length === 8, `design.json colorMeta.${name} = ${value} with 8 ramp steps`)
const css = (ref) => sidecar.components?.find((component) => component.refersTo === ref)?.css ?? ""
check(D, css("button-secondary").includes("border: 1px solid #C3CDDA") && !/opacity/.test(css("button-primary") + css("button-secondary")), "design.json buttons: #C3CDDA outline, no opacity")
check(D, css("input").includes("background: #FFFFFF") && css("input").includes("rgb(16 133 141 / 0.16)"), "design.json input: white fill and focus halo")
check(D, css("badge-status").includes("height: 22px") && css("badge-status").includes("border-radius: 6px"), "design.json badge: 22px, 6px")
check(D, !(sidecar.narrative?.dos ?? []).some((line) => line.includes("touch icon")), "design.json dos no longer name the touch icon button")

const guide = read(r, "docs/14_UI_UX_DESIGN_SYSTEM.md")
for (const needle of ["## Shared Components", "2026-10-11-ui-phase2a-design.md", "`NativeSelect`", "`Notice` with `tone`"]) check(D, guide.includes(needle), `docs/14 has ${needle}`)

const handoff = read(r, "DEVELOPER_HANDOFF.md").replace(/\r\n/g, "\n")
const section = handoff.slice(handoff.indexOf("## UI Round 4 Phase 2A Shared Components (2026-10-11)"), handoff.indexOf("## Open Go-Live Decisions"))
check(D, handoff.indexOf("## UI Round 4 Phase 2A Shared Components (2026-10-11)") > handoff.indexOf("## UI Round 4 Phase 1 Foundation (2026-10-10)"), "DEVELOPER_HANDOFF.md 2A section after phase 1, before Open Go-Live Decisions")
for (const needle of ["Do not deploy 2A alone (I22)", "No new tokens after 2A (I21)", "`fieldSize`", "`Button busy`", "`DropdownMenuItem unavailable`", "`Notice` needs `tone`", "`StatusBadge` has no `size`", "`cn()`", "Dev-app check (Task 12)"]) check(D, section.includes(needle), `DEVELOPER_HANDOFF.md 2A section has ${needle}`)
check(D, handoff.includes("`field-variants.ts`"), "DEVELOPER_HANDOFF.md shadcn notes name field-variants.ts")

const log = read(r, "docs/99_CHANGELOG.md").replace(/\r\n/g, "\n")
const entry = log.slice(log.indexOf("## 2026-10-11"), log.indexOf("## 2026-10-10"))
check(D, log.indexOf("## 2026-10-11") !== -1 && log.indexOf("## 2026-10-11") < log.indexOf("## 2026-10-10"), "CHANGELOG ## 2026-10-11 sits above ## 2026-10-10")
check(D, entry.includes("### รากฐานคอมโพเนนต์กลาง (UI รอบ 4 เฟส 2A)") && entry.includes("ตรวจบนแอป (Task 12)"), "CHANGELOG 2A entry with the Task 12 bullet")
check(D, !entry.includes("npm run verify"), "CHANGELOG 2A entry has no verify numbers yet (Task 14 adds them)")
check(D, !/\bTBD\b|<short hash>|<what it fixed>|<สิ่งที่แก้>/.test(entry + section), "no placeholder text left in the new CHANGELOG entry or handoff section")

for (const path of ["DESIGN.md", ".impeccable/design.json", "docs/14_UI_UX_DESIGN_SYSTEM.md", "DEVELOPER_HANDOFF.md", "docs/99_CHANGELOG.md"]) {
  const text = read(r, path)
  check(D, text.includes("\r\n") && !/[^\r]\n/.test(text), `${path} keeps CRLF on every line`)
}

for (const group of docsOnly ? ["docs"] : ["shipped", "docs"]) {
  console.log(`${group}: ${counts[group] - failures[group].length}/${counts[group]} ok`)
  for (const label of failures[group]) console.log(`  FAIL ${label}`)
}
process.exit(failures.shipped.length + failures.docs.length ? 1 : 0)
```

Replace:

Replace:

```md
- **Soft System Background** (`--background` #F6F8FB): Field fills, dialog bodies and empty boxes.
- **Surface White** (`--card`, `--popover` #FFFFFF): Panels, forms, popovers, tables, topbar and sidebar.
- **Ink** (`--foreground` #0B1D35): Main text and data values.
- **Border** (`--border` #D7DEE8): Dividers, table shells and panel boundaries. A base rule sets it as the default border color.
- **Input** (`--input` #7C8BA0): Field borders. It passes 3:1 (WCAG 1.4.11) on card, background and canvas.

with:

```md
- **Soft System Background** (`--background` #F6F8FB): Dialog bodies and footers, quiet wells inside panels (the solid replacement for `bg-muted/20` to `/50`), the hover fill of outline and ghost buttons and of link cards, and the disabled and read-only look of buttons and fields.
- **Surface White** (`--card`, `--popover` #FFFFFF): Panels, forms, fields, popovers, menus, toasts, tables, topbar and sidebar.
- **Row Hover** (`--row-hover` #F3F6FB, `bg-row-hover`): The solid hover and focus fill of table and list rows. It is opaque, so sticky cells stay solid while a table scrolls sideways under them.
- **Ink** (`--foreground` #0B1D35): Main text and data values.
- **Faint** (`--faint-foreground` #56677F, `text-faint-foreground`): The third text level: placeholders, hints under fields, table headers, breadcrumbs, sub-lines, stat footers, tab counts, menu trails and reasons, units, the `Nil` dash, disabled text, field icons and the select arrow. AA on every neutral surface (4.95:1 on muted to 5.77:1 on white), and allowed on `primary-soft` for a hovered or selected row or menu item (4.99:1, spec ruling I14). Never on a status soft fill (`bg-{success,warning,danger,info}-soft`; use the tone ink or `muted-foreground`), never for a main data value, and never as the only text that says what a control is. `--sidebar-muted` #586A84 is nearly the same color but belongs to the sidebar; do not swap them.
- **Neutral Ink** (`--neutral-ink` #2A4468, `bg-neutral-ink`): The bar marker of neutral and muted status badges. AA on every surface a badge sits on (8.48:1 on muted to 9.88:1 on white), so later phases may use it for text.
- **Border** (`--border` #D7DEE8): Dividers, table shells and panel boundaries. A base rule sets it as the default border color.
- **Border Soft** (`--border-soft` #E7EBF1, `border-border-soft`, or `bg-border-soft` for a 1px line): Inner dividers such as menu separators and row dividers. A line color, not a fill.
- **Control Border** (`--control-border` #C3CDDA, `border-control-border`): The border of outline buttons. It is decorative (1.61:1 on white; the label or icon says it is a button), so it is never the only border of a field, checkbox, toggle or drop zone.
- **Input** (`--input` #7C8BA0): Field, checkbox and drop-zone borders, and breadcrumb separators. It passes 3:1 (WCAG 1.4.11) on card, background and canvas. On white the lines go border-soft 1.20 < border 1.35 < control-border 1.61 < input 3.47:1 (`tests/design-tokens-contrast.test.ts`).
- **Ring** (`--ring` #10858D): The only focus color.
```

Replace:

Teal is for info status, the focus ring and the selected menu icon. Green, orange and red appear only inside status badges. Everything else is neutral. Do not use these colors as decorative fill across inactive cards or marketing-style sections.

with:

```md
**The Where Color Lives Rule.** Navy #083161 lives only in the logo and the selected menu row. Actions and links use Primary #1E4F94. Teal is for info status, the focus ring and the selected menu icon. Green, orange and red appear only inside status badges, as the icon of a toast, a `Notice` or an `ActionEmptyState` (error = danger, permission = warning), and on destructive buttons and menu items (danger). Of these, only warning and danger toasts and `Notice` boxes get a soft fill: tint only what the user must deal with. Everything else is neutral. Do not use these colors as decorative fill across inactive cards or marketing-style sections.

Known exception: until phase 2D, info notices, role chips and a few info icons also use the `info` tokens and so appear teal. Phase 2A changed only their borders (`border-info/30` → `border-info-border`); the `Notice` sweep in 2D moves them to neutral.

Replace:

```md
**The Token Contrast Rule.** Status text uses the status ink (`text-success`, `text-warning`, `text-danger`, `text-info`) on white, the page background, canvas, muted, accent, or its own soft surface (`bg-{tone}-soft`). Never tint with opacity (`bg-warning/10`, `bg-primary/10`) and never lighten solid fills on hover (`/90`): use `bg-{tone}-soft` and `hover:bg-{tone}-hover`. `{tone}-foreground` means text on the solid fill. `tests/design-tokens-contrast.test.ts` enforces every text pair at 4.5:1 and the ring, input and selected-menu-icon pairs at 3:1.
```

with:

```md
**The Token Contrast Rule.** Status text uses the status ink (`text-success`, `text-warning`, `text-danger`, `text-info`) on white, the page background, canvas, muted, accent, row hover, or its own soft surface (`bg-{tone}-soft`). Never tint with opacity (`bg-warning/10`, `bg-primary/10`) and never lighten solid fills on hover (`/90`): use `bg-{tone}-soft` and `hover:bg-{tone}-hover`. `{tone}-foreground` means text on the solid fill. Tone borders use `border-{tone}-border`, never `border-{tone}/NN`; neutral fills are solid tokens (`bg-background`, `bg-row-hover`, `border-border-soft`), never `bg-muted/NN` or `bg-accent/NN` outside table headers. Faint text is AA on every neutral surface and on `primary-soft`, never on a status soft fill. Button outlines (`control-border`, `danger-border`, `primary-border`) are decorative and need no 3:1; fields, checkboxes and drop zones keep `input` at 3:1. `tests/design-tokens-contrast.test.ts` enforces every text pair at 4.5:1 and the ring, input and selected-menu-icon pairs at 3:1; the ratchet guards `R1 tone colors never fade with opacity` and `R7 neutral fills are opaque tokens outside table headers` in `tests/visual-foundation-guards.test.ts` block new tints.
```

Replace:

```md
- **Label** (500, 0.875rem, 22px, 0 letter spacing): Field labels, button text, filter labels, navigation labels, and compact UI controls. Avoid all-caps labels except very short badges where the existing system already uses them.
- **Small** (`text-xs`, 16px; 18px on Thai pages): Badges, section headings in the sidebar, the mobile bottom bar labels.
```

with:

```md
- **Label** (500, 0.875rem, 22px, 0 letter spacing): Button text, filter labels, navigation labels, and compact UI controls. Field labels from `FieldLabel` use Meta at weight 500 instead; hand-written field labels move to it in phase 2C. Avoid all-caps labels except very short badges where the existing system already uses them.
- **Small** (`text-xs`, 16px; 18px on Thai pages): Section headings in the sidebar and in menus, the mobile bottom bar labels, `Kbd` keys.
- **Meta** (inherits its weight, 0.8125rem / `text-meta`, 20px in every language): Secondary text: breadcrumbs, meta lines under page titles, table headers, sub-lines in rows, field labels, hints and errors under fields, status badge text, `sm` buttons, chips, tab counts, menu trails and reasons, stat labels and footers, one-line empty notes.
- **Metric** (500, 1.75rem / `text-metric`, 32px, −0.02em): The value in a stat. Numbers only.
- **Count** (600, 1.25rem / `text-count`, 24px, −0.01em): Counts in rows and compact stats. Numbers only.

`text-metric` and `text-count` carry their own weight and tracking; `font-semibold` or `tracking-normal` on the same element still wins. A class string with either one must also contain `num` (guard), and a Thai unit beside the number goes in an inner `text-base` span, never on its own line in the metric size. Do not write `text-[13px]` or `text-[28px]`; use the tokens (guard).

**Three text levels:** Ink (`text-foreground`) for data values, names, titles, button text and field labels · Secondary (`text-muted-foreground`) for meta lines under titles, stat labels, calm badge text and ghost buttons · Faint (`text-faint-foreground`) for the uses listed under Neutral in §2.
```

Replace:

- `tag` (Plex Mono, `calc(1em - 1px)`, `nowrap`): asset tags and document numbers. Defined now; the 119 asset-tag call sites move to it in phase 2.
- Toasts use the app font through an unlayered `:root [data-sonner-toaster]` rule (Sonner's own CSS is unlayered).

with:

```md
- `tag` (Plex Mono, `calc(1em - 1px)`, weight 400, `nowrap`, normal numerals, `user-select: all`): asset tags, document numbers and serials. One click or tap selects the whole code; inside `a`, `button`, `label` and `summary` selection is normal. Never bold (Plex Mono loads 400, 500 and 600 only, no 700; the row's own id is `tag font-medium text-foreground`) and never resized on the same element: put `tag` on an inner `<span>` and size the wrapper (guard). First user: breadcrumb codes (`code: true`); about 79 code call sites move to it in phase 2B.
- `select-chevron`: the arrow of a native `<select>` (a 16px faint chevron 10px from the right edge; the select adds its own `pr-9`). Forced-colors mode shows the system arrow. Never put it in `getFieldControlClasses()`, which also styles inputs.
- Toasts use the app font through an unlayered `:root [data-sonner-toaster]` rule (Sonner's own CSS is unlayered). The quiet toast look is a separate set of unlayered `:root [data-sonner-…]` rules (see §5 Feedback); keep them out of the font rule, which a test reads as is.

Replace:

```md
Sheets open in 250ms and close in 200ms with ease-out. Scrims and dialog, alert-dialog and sheet content use `motion-reduce:animate-none!` (the `!` beats the `data-[state]:animate-*` classes), so reduced motion turns the animation off.
```

with:

```md
Sheets open in 250ms and close in 200ms with ease-out. Dropdown menus, submenus and popovers fade in with a 4px slide over 160ms (ease-out) and fade out in 100ms, with no zoom. Scrims, dialog, alert-dialog and sheet content, menus and popovers use `motion-reduce:animate-none!` (the `!` beats the `data-[state]:animate-*` classes), so reduced motion turns the animation off. A menu item's highlight appears at once and fades out in 150ms. Toasts follow sonner's own reduced-motion rule. The spinners of `Button busy` and `SearchField` use `animate-spin motion-reduce:animate-none`.
```

Do not add `shadow-sm` to panels or cards; only floating layers use `shadow-overlay`. Phase 2A removed `shadow-sm` from every panel, card and section. Six stay on purpose: 3 controls that float over a camera image or a photo (`scanner-text-input.tsx` ×2, `audit-scan-check-form.tsx`) and 3 paper previews (`asset-label-print.tsx`, `operation-document-print.tsx`, the label sample in `system-settings-form.tsx`). Six larger shadows wait for their owners: the 2 sticky save bars (2C), the 2 floating add buttons (2D), the mobile bulk-action bar of the register (2B) and the round scan button (kept since phase 1). The guard `R2 only floating layers, camera controls and paper previews cast a shadow` (`tests/visual-foundation-guards.test.ts`) holds these 12 in a per-file budget that may only go down.

Replace:

```md
Components should feel consistent, restrained, and task-first. The system already uses shared helpers for panels, action buttons, fields, tables, mobile cards, empty states, and touch icon buttons; preserve those helpers when building new surfaces.
```

with:

```md
Components should feel consistent, restrained, and task-first. Shared primitives and helpers live in `src/components/ui/` and `src/lib/design-system.ts`: buttons, fields, menus, toasts, notices, empty states, panels, tables and mobile cards. Build new surfaces from them; do not copy their class strings into pages.
```

Replace the whole Buttons section:

```md
- **Shape:** Gently curved rectangles (8px radius).
- **Primary:** Primary #1E4F94 background with white text, medium weight, 40px desktop height, and 44px minimum touch height on mobile.
- **Hover / Focus:** Solid buttons darken on hover with the hover token (`hover:bg-primary-hover`, `hover:bg-danger-hover`) — never fade with opacity. Focus uses the teal ring `#10858D` (`ring-ring`) with offset. Disabled states reduce opacity and block pointer actions. Build buttons with `Button` / `buttonVariants` from `src/components/ui/` (shadcn); `getActionButtonClasses` maps legacy variants onto the same classes.
- **Secondary / Ghost / Tertiary:** Secondary buttons use Surface White, Border, and Ink. Ghost buttons use no border at rest and an Accent hover. Danger buttons use Danger Red only for destructive actions.
```

with:

```md
- **Build them with** `Button` / `buttonVariants` (`src/components/ui/button.tsx`, `button-variants.ts`). A `<Link>` or `<a>` takes `className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size }), …)}` (server or client) or `<Button asChild>` (client). Keep only layout classes (`w-full sm:w-auto`, `flex-1`, `self-end`, `min-w-0`) in `className`.
- **Shape:** Gently curved rectangles: 8px (`rounded-md`) for `md`, `lg`, `default` and `icon`, the same as fields; 6px (`rounded-control`) for the small `sm` and `icon-sm`. Every variant has a 1px border, so all variants have the same box. Icon and label sit 6px apart (`gap-1.5`); the cursor is a pointer.
- **Sizes** (height from `md` up; below `md` every size is at least 44px tall, `min-h-11`):

| Size | Height | Side padding | Text |
|---|---|---|---|
| `sm` | 32px | 10px | `text-meta` 13/20 |
| `md` | 36px | 14px | `text-sm` |
| `lg` | 40px | 16px | `text-sm`; buttons in a row of 40px fields |
| `default` | 40px, the same as `lg`, until phase 2D makes it 36px | 16px | `text-sm` |
| `icon-sm` | 32 × 32px | — | — |
| `icon` | 40 × 40px until phase 2D makes it 36 × 36px | — | — |

- **Variants** (every text pair passes AA; `tests/design-system.test.ts` pins the classes):

| Variant | At rest | Hover | Pressed | Use |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `default` | Primary #1E4F94 fill, white text | `primary-hover` #173E76 | `brand-navy` #083161 | The main action |
| `outline` | White, `control-border` #C3CDDA border, ink text | `background` fill, `input` border | `muted` fill | Secondary actions |
| `ghost` | No fill or border, `muted-foreground` text | `background` fill, ink text | `muted` fill | Quiet and icon-only actions. Not on a `bg-background` footer, where its hover fill is invisible: use `outline` there |
| `danger` | White, `danger-border` border, `danger` text | `danger-soft` fill | `danger` border | A destructive action placed in a page |
| `destructive` | Solid danger fill, white text | `danger-hover` | — | Only the confirm button of a destructive dialog |
| `warning` | Solid warning fill, white text | `warning-hover` | — | Saving an audit check that differs, and `ConfirmTextDialog` with tone warning |

- There is no `secondary`, `link` or `success` variant: use `outline`, `linkVariants` and `default`.
- **Disabled:** never faded with opacity. Every variant turns to the `background` fill, `border` border and faint text with a not-allowed cursor (`disabled:` and `aria-disabled:`); the disabled look wins over every hover and pressed state. `aria-disabled` also blocks pointer events, for `asChild` links and busy buttons.
- **Busy:** pass `busy` while the action runs. The button gets `aria-busy` and `aria-disabled`, shows a spinner (`Loader2`, stopped under reduced motion) in place of its leading icon or in front of its label, and ignores clicks, Enter, Space and implicit form submission, but keeps keyboard focus. Do not pass `disabled` with `busy` (focus would drop to `<body>`). `busy` cannot be combined with `asChild` (type error), and it must come from client state: a server file cannot pass it. The caller still writes the label ("กำลังบันทึก…").
- **Focus:** `focus-visible:ring-2 ring-ring ring-offset-2`, teal #10858D on every variant, danger included.
- **Text links:** `linkVariants({ size })`: primary text whose underline appears on hover and keyboard focus. `size: "meta"` (13px, no wrap, the default) for a stand-alone command link such as "ล้างทั้งหมด"; `size: "inherit"` for a link inside a sentence. It has no 44px box; a stand-alone link on mobile adds `inline-flex min-h-11 items-center md:min-h-0`.
- **Legacy:** `ActionButton` and `getActionButtonClasses` are deprecated; phase 2D moves their 10 call sites to `Button` and deletes them. `ActionButton variant="danger"` is the solid `destructive`, not `Button variant="danger"`. `getSafeActionLinkClasses()` builds on `buttonVariants` and lets long labels wrap. `getTouchIconButtonClasses` was removed: use `Button size="icon"`.
- **Hand-written buttons** (about 410) keep their own classes until 2B, 2C and 2D move them. Moving one must not change its desktop height: `h-8` → `sm`, `h-9` → `md`, `h-10` beside 40px fields → `lg`, any other `h-10` → no size (`default`).
```

Replace:

```md
- On the selected menu row the ring sits outside the row on white (`ring-offset-2 ring-offset-sidebar`, 4.41:1), because an inset ring on navy reaches only 2.94:1.
```

with:

```md
- On the selected menu row the ring sits outside the row on white (`ring-offset-2 ring-offset-sidebar`, 4.41:1), because an inset ring on navy reaches only 2.94:1.
- Dropdown menu items take the base outline inside the row (`focus-visible:-outline-offset-2`), because rows touch the panel edge (the ring reaches 3.82:1 on `primary-soft` and 3.93:1 on `danger-soft`).
- Fields use `focus:border-ring focus:ring-1 focus:ring-ring` plus the 4px `shadow-field-focus` halo; a field with `aria-invalid` turns back to teal while focused. Never write `focus:border-danger`.
- Toasts and their close button use `outline: 2px solid var(--ring)`, which stays visible in forced-colors mode.
- `FileDropzone` hides its file input with `sr-only`, which clips an outline, so its label draws the outline instead (`has-focus-visible:outline-2 has-focus-visible:outline-offset-2 has-focus-visible:outline-ring`).
```

Replace:

```md
- **Style:** One `StatusBadge` (`src/components/ui/status-badge.tsx`, variants in `badge-variants.ts`) in two tiers, each with a marker whose shape carries the meaning, so color-blind users can tell states apart.
  - **Calm tier** (no fill, no side padding, so it lines up with other text in a column; muted-foreground text): `info` = hollow ring in info teal (open, planned, reported, and unknown asset states) · `success` = solid dot in success green (including Ready and In Use) · `primary` = solid dot in primary (approved, done, received) · `neutral` / `muted` = short bar in muted-foreground.
  - **Attention tier** (soft fill, border and tone ink text): `warning` = triangle · `danger` = diamond.
```

with:

```md
- **Style:** One `StatusBadge` (`src/components/ui/status-badge.tsx`, variants in `badge-variants.ts`) in one size and two tiers, each with a marker whose shape carries the meaning, so color-blind users can tell states apart.
  - **Size:** 22px tall in every language (`h-5.5`, the table line), `text-meta` 13/20 at weight 500, 6px corners (`rounded-control`), no vertical padding. There is no `size` prop any more.
  - **Calm tier** (no fill, no side padding, so it lines up with other text in a column; muted-foreground text): `info` = hollow ring in info teal (open, planned, reported, and unknown asset states) · `success` = solid dot in success green (including Ready and In Use) · `primary` = solid dot in primary (approved, done, received) · `neutral` / `muted` = short bar in `neutral-ink` #2A4468.
  - **Attention tier** (soft fill, border, 8px side padding and tone ink text): `warning` = triangle · `danger` = diamond.
```

with:

```md
- **Menus never contain dialogs.** A menu item only opens a dialog or drawer that is rendered outside the menu, and that dialog returns focus to the menu trigger.
- **Menu panels** (`DropdownMenuContent`, `PopoverContent`, `Command`): 12px corners (`rounded-lg`), white, `border`, `shadow-overlay`. Dropdown panels are at least 240px wide (`min-w-60`, so callers do not set narrower widths), with `p-1.5` and 1px between items; the popover keeps `w-72 p-4`.
- **Menu items:** 36px from `md` up and 44px below, 6px corners, `text-sm` ink, 16px icons in `muted-foreground`. Hover and arrow keys highlight with `primary-soft`; pressing shows `muted`; `variant="destructive"` is danger text with a `danger-soft` highlight. Group labels are 12px medium `muted-foreground`, separators are `border-soft` lines, and `DropdownMenuShortcut` is the faint 13px trail at the end.
- **An item that cannot run now keeps its reason readable and reachable:** use `<DropdownMenuItem unavailable>` with `<DropdownMenuItemText>{title}<DropdownMenuItemDescription>{reason}</DropdownMenuItemDescription></DropdownMenuItemText>`. It sets `aria-disabled` without Radix `disabled`, so arrow keys and type-ahead still reach it and a screen reader reads the reason; Enter or a click neither closes the menu nor runs the action. Faint text, no fill, no opacity. `unavailable` cannot be combined with `asChild` (type error: the link would navigate first). Radix `disabled` stays for short states whose reason is on screen (while deleting, the last visible column).
- **Checkbox and radio items:** a checkbox item draws a 16px box in front of its label (`input` border; checked = primary fill with a white check); a radio item shows a primary check at the end; the chosen item is `font-medium`. No leading dots.
- **Command lists** (`SearchableSelect`): the search box is a box inside the panel (6px corners, `background` fill, focus halo, `text-base md:text-sm` so iOS does not zoom), the highlighted option is `primary-soft`, and only the chosen option shows a check, at the end. Global search rows use the same highlight and a footer of `Kbd` hints (↑↓ เลือก · Enter เปิด · Esc ปิด).
- **`Kbd`** (`src/components/ui/kbd.tsx`): a 20px key cap with 4px corners, `font-sans` (preflight makes `kbd` monospace) and `text-xs` on a 16px line, which is safe because keys hold only Latin letters and symbols.
- **Language menu:** a radio group; each language is written in its own language with a `lang` attribute ("ภาษาไทย", "English"); no flags. No emoji anywhere in `src` or `messages` (guard). Screen-reader names in the topbar come from messages (`nav.openMenu`, `nav.toggleSidebar`, `nav.languageMenu`, `nav.userMenu`, `nav.closeMenu`).

### Feedback: Toasts, Notices and Empty States

- **Toasts** (`AppToaster` in `src/components/ui/app-toaster.tsx`, mounted once in `src/app/[locale]/layout.tsx`; call sites keep `toast.*` and `useApiError().toast`): quiet. A white card with a `border`, 12px corners and `shadow-overlay`, 380px wide, below the 64px topbar (top 72px, right 16px; phones top 72px), shown for 6 seconds. The tone is the icon (`toneIcons`: success `CircleCheck`, info `Info`, warning `TriangleAlert`, error `OctagonAlert`); only warning and error toasts get a soft fill and a tone border. The title is 14/22 at weight 500 in ink, the description 13/20 `muted-foreground`. Every toast has a 24px close button ("ปิด", `common.close`), and the region is named "การแจ้งเตือน" (`notifications.title`); Alt+T then Tab reaches them. Toasts are hidden in print. No `richColors`. The CSS is a set of unlayered `:root [data-sonner-…]` rules in `src/app/globals.css` tied to sonner 2's data attributes; check them in the browser again after a sonner upgrade.
- **`Notice`** (`src/components/ui/notice.tsx`, `tone` required): `warning` and `danger` are boxes (soft fill, tone border, 8px corners, 18px icon) for something the user must deal with now; `info`, `neutral` and `success` are inline lines with a 16px icon and no box. With a `title`, the body is 13/20 `muted-foreground`; without one, the body is 14/22 ink. `action` holds a `Button size="md"`. Never faint text, `/NN` tints or a tone-colored paragraph inside.
- **`ActionEmptyState`** (`src/components/ui/action-empty-state.tsx`): empty, error and permission states of a page or table that have a title, a description or an action. No box, border or fill (centered, `py-10`); a 24px icon without a circle (empty faint, error danger, permission warning); a 16px semibold title; the default action is `buttonVariants({ size: "md" })`, and `actionVariant="secondary"` makes it an outline button (use it for "ล้างตัวกรอง").
- **`EmptyNote`** (`src/components/ui/empty-note.tsx`): one quiet 13/20 faint line for lists, panel sections and empty table rows. `tone="clear"` adds a check in the text color (not green) for "nothing to do"; `padded` adds `px-5 py-4` when the container has no padding.
- **Which one:** one line of text → `EmptyNote`; a title, a description or a button → `ActionEmptyState`.
- **Live-region roles are written at the call site, never derived from the tone:** `role="alert"` for an error after the user acted (a save or a login failed); `role="status"` or `aria-live="polite"` for a result that appears by itself (saved, queued offline, copied); nothing for text that is there when the page loads.

Their classes come from `fieldControlVariants({ fieldSize })` in `field-variants.ts`; `getFieldControlClasses(options?)` (`src/lib/design-system.ts`) returns the same classes for controls that cannot use a primitive, such as the `SearchableSelect` trigger. The size prop is `fieldSize`, never `size` (`size` is a numeric HTML attribute of `<input>` and `<select>`).
- **Style:** 8px corners (`rounded-md`), `border-input` #7C8BA0, white fill (`bg-surface`), 12px side padding. The border reaches 3:1 on card, background and canvas; never place a field on `bg-muted`, where it reaches only 2.97:1. `control-border` is for buttons only.
- **Sizes:** `fieldSize` `md` 36px, `lg` 40px (the default) and `xl` 48px from `md` up. Below `md` every size is 44px tall (`xl` 48px) with 16px text, so iOS does not zoom; from `md` up the text is 14px (`xl` keeps 16px).
- **Focus:** `focus:border-ring focus:ring-1 focus:ring-ring` plus a 4px halo (`shadow-field-focus`, the ring at 16%). An open `SearchableSelect` (`aria-expanded`) looks focused.
- **Error:** set `aria-invalid` (`Field` does it when `error` is set): a `danger` border, 1px ring and 4px halo (`shadow-field-error`, danger at 14%). While focused the field turns back to teal, and the error text under it stays. Never write `focus:border-danger` or `aria-invalid:focus:ring-danger` (one focus color).
- **Disabled:** `background` fill, `border` border, faint text, not-allowed cursor, no opacity. **Read-only** (`Input` and `Textarea` only): `background` fill and `border` border with normal text, still focusable and copyable.
- **Placeholder:** the faint ink from one base rule (`::placeholder`, 5.77:1 on white); never add `placeholder:text-*` (guard).
- **`Field`:** the label (13/20, weight 500, ink) sits above the control; the hint (13/20 faint) and the error (13/20 `text-danger`, `role="alert"`, under the hint) sit below it, and `aria-describedby` reads the error first. `required` shows a warning-colored `*` with `aria-hidden` and sets `aria-required`, not the native `required` (that would start the browser's own form validation). Do not wrap `SearchableSelect` in `Field` (it has its own label), and never put `Field` inside a `<label>`.
- **Native select arrow:** `select-chevron` (§3 Utilities); forced-colors mode shows the system arrow.
- **Checkboxes and radios** stay native: the primary color comes from `accent-color: var(--primary)`, focus from the base outline, and `checkboxVariants` sets the size; the 44px target is the row's `<label>`.
- **`FileDropzone`:** a dashed `border-input` box with 8px corners on white, `background` on hover, the focus outline on its label, and `border` + `background` when disabled (no opacity).
- **Migration:** 316 fields in 70 files are still hand-written, and 282 of them use `border-border`; phase 2C moves them to the primitives. Until then some forms show both kinds, so 2A ships only with the first 2C batch.
- Text selection uses `primary-border` behind ink.
```

### Empty Values and Numbers

Money always has 2 decimals. Outside tables pass the unit, `formatMoney(x, { unit })` with `common.baht` ("บาท" / "THB"), to get "64,900.00 บาท" with a non-breaking space; in tables the unit sits in the column header ("มูลค่า (บาท)") and the cells hold only the number. Line digits up with `num`.
- Phase 2A only adds these helpers; 2B uses them on screens (156 `"-"` fallbacks, 44 `toLocaleString()` calls without a locale). Print pages, PDFs and label data keep `"-"`, and `formatCurrency` (`฿` prefix) stays until 2D.

Expected: exit 1 with `docs: 47/74 ok`. The 27 failures left are the 10 `design.json`, 4 `docs/14`, 11 `DEVELOPER_HANDOFF.md` and 2 `CHANGELOG` checks. No `DESIGN.md` line may be in the list. If one is, the replacement it names did not apply; check the old string.

- [ ] **Step 9: `.impeccable/design.json`**

Save this script in your temp folder as `update-design-json.mjs` and never commit it.
- It checks every target first and writes nothing on a mismatch.
- It reuses phase 1's ramp formula, and first proves the formula against the existing `input` ramp.
- It keeps CRLF.

```js
// Task 13: bring .impeccable/design.json in line with DESIGN.md after phase 2A.
// Run from the repo root: node <your temp folder>/update-design-json.mjs [path]   (never commit this file)
// Checks every target first and writes nothing if one is missing. Keeps the file's CRLF line endings.
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs"

const path = process.argv[2] ?? ".impeccable/design.json"
const raw = readFileSync(path, "utf8")
const crlf = raw.includes("\r\n")
const d = JSON.parse(raw)
const problems = []
const check = (ok, message) => {
  if (!ok) problems.push(message)
}

// Same ramp formula as phase 1: OKLCH hue of the canonical color, 8 fixed lightness steps.
function hexToOklch(hex) {
  const n = parseInt(hex.slice(1), 16)
  const lin = (c) => {
    c /= 255
    return c <= 0.04045 ? c / 12.92 : ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4
  }
  const r = lin(n >> 16)
  const g = lin((n >> 8) & 255)
  const b = lin(n & 255)
  const l = Math.cbrt(0.4122214708 * r + 0.5363325363 * g + 0.0514459929 * b)
  const m = Math.cbrt(0.2119034982 * r + 0.6806995451 * g + 0.1073969566 * b)
  const s = Math.cbrt(0.0883024619 * r + 0.2817188376 * g + 0.6299787005 * b)
  const A = 1.9779984951 * l - 2.428592205 * m + 0.4505937099 * s
  const B = 0.0259040371 * l + 0.7827717662 * m - 0.808675766 * s
  let h = (Math.atan2(B, A) * 180) / Math.PI
  if (h < 0) h += 360
  return { C: Math.sqrt(A * A + B * B), h }
}
const Ls = [21, 28, 37, 47, 60, 73, 86, 96]
const Cf = [0.6, 0.8, 0.95, 1, 0.9, 0.7, 0.35, 0.1]
const ramp = (hex) => {
  const { C, h } = hexToOklch(hex)
  return Ls.map((L, i) => `oklch(${L}% ${+(C * Cf[i]).toFixed(3)} ${Math.round(h)})`)
}
const meta = (displayName, canonical) => ({ role: "neutral", displayName, canonical, tonalRamp: ramp(canonical) })

// Phase 1 ramps must still come out of the same formula, or the new ramps would not match the old ones.
check(JSON.stringify(d.extensions.colorMeta.input.tonalRamp) === JSON.stringify(ramp("#7C8BA0")), "the ramp formula no longer reproduces colorMeta.input")

const swap = (text, from, to, label) => {
  const count = text.split(from).length - 1
  check(count === 1, `${label}: expected 1 x ${JSON.stringify(from.slice(0, 60))}, found ${count}`)
  return text.replace(from, () => to)
}

const font = `"IBM Plex Sans", "IBM Plex Sans Thai", system-ui, sans-serif`
const byRef = (ref) => d.components.find((component) => component.refersTo === ref)
for (const ref of ["button-primary", "button-secondary", "input", "badge-status"]) check(byRef(ref), `component ${ref} not found`)
const rule = (name) => d.narrative.rules.find((item) => item.name === name)
for (const name of ["The Where Color Lives Rule", "The Token Contrast Rule", "The Thai Line-Height Rule", "The Border First Rule"]) check(rule(name), `rule ${name} not found`)
check(d.narrative.dos[0].includes("touch icon button"), "dos[0] is not the old touch icon text")
for (const name of ["faint-foreground", "neutral-ink", "row-hover", "border-soft", "control-border"]) check(!(name in d.extensions.colorMeta), `colorMeta.${name} already exists`)

if (problems.length) {
  console.error(`ABORT, nothing was written:\n- ${problems.join("\n- ")}`)
  process.exit(1)
}

d.generatedAt = "2026-10-11T00:00:00+07:00"

// colorMeta: the 5 phase 2A neutrals go right after "input", in the order of the :root block.
const added = {
  "faint-foreground": meta("Faint Ink", "#56677F"),
  "neutral-ink": meta("Neutral Ink", "#2A4468"),
  "row-hover": meta("Row Hover", "#F3F6FB"),
  "border-soft": meta("Border Soft", "#E7EBF1"),
  "control-border": meta("Control Border", "#C3CDDA"),
}
const colorMeta = {}
for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(d.extensions.colorMeta)) {
  colorMeta[name] = value
  if (name === "input") Object.assign(colorMeta, added)
}
d.extensions.colorMeta = colorMeta

Object.assign(d.extensions.typographyMeta, {
  meta: { displayName: "Meta", fontFamily: "IBM Plex Sans, IBM Plex Sans Thai, system-ui, sans-serif", purpose: "text-meta 13/20: breadcrumbs, meta lines, table headers, hints and errors, status badge text, sm buttons, chips and menu trails." },
  metric: { displayName: "Metric", fontFamily: "IBM Plex Sans, IBM Plex Sans Thai, system-ui, sans-serif", purpose: "text-metric 28/32, weight 500, -0.02em: stat values. Numbers only, always with num." },
  count: { displayName: "Count", fontFamily: "IBM Plex Sans, IBM Plex Sans Thai, system-ui, sans-serif", purpose: "text-count 20/24, weight 600, -0.01em: counts in rows and compact stats. Numbers only, always with num." },
})

d.extensions.shadows = [
  {
    name: "overlay",
    value: "0 12px 32px -8px rgb(8 49 97 / 0.18), 0 2px 6px -2px rgb(8 49 97 / 0.1)",
    purpose: "shadow-overlay: dialogs, sheets, popovers, dropdown menus and toasts only. Panels, cards, and the topbar are flat.",
  },
  {
    name: "field-focus",
    value: "0 0 0 4px rgb(16 133 141 / 0.16)",
    purpose: "shadow-field-focus: the halo around a focused field (with a 1px ring) and an open SearchableSelect. Not elevation.",
  },
  {
    name: "field-error",
    value: "0 0 0 4px rgb(179 38 30 / 0.14)",
    purpose: "shadow-field-error: the halo around an unfocused field with aria-invalid. Not elevation.",
  },
]

d.extensions.motion.splice(1, 0, {
  name: "menu-open",
  value: "160ms ease-out fade + 4px slide in; 100ms fade out; no zoom",
  purpose: "Dropdown menus, submenus and popovers. motion-reduce:animate-none! turns it off.",
})

const buttonCss = (cls, rest, hover, active) =>
  `.${cls} { min-height: 44px; padding: 0 16px; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; gap: 6px; ${rest} border-radius: 8px; font: 500 14px/1.5714 ${font}; cursor: pointer; transition: background-color 150ms ease-out, border-color 150ms ease-out, color 150ms ease-out; } @media (min-width: 768px) { .${cls} { min-height: 0; height: 40px; } } .${cls}:hover { ${hover} } .${cls}:active { ${active} } .${cls}:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid #10858D; outline-offset: 2px; } .${cls}:disabled, .${cls}[aria-disabled="true"] { background: #F6F8FB; border-color: #D7DEE8; color: #56677F; cursor: not-allowed; } .${cls}[aria-disabled="true"] { pointer-events: none; }`

const primary = byRef("button-primary")
primary.description = "Primary action button (Button variant default) for save, create, submit, approve, or workflow-forward actions. Disabled and busy buttons turn grey with faint text instead of fading."
primary.css = buttonCss("ds-btn-primary", "border: 1px solid #1E4F94; background: #1E4F94; color: #FFFFFF;", "background: #173E76; border-color: #173E76;", "background: #083161; border-color: #083161;")

const secondary = byRef("button-secondary")
secondary.description = "Secondary action (Button variant outline) for filters, navigation-adjacent actions, and lower-priority commands. Its #C3CDDA border is decorative; the label says it is a button."
secondary.css = buttonCss("ds-btn-secondary", "border: 1px solid #C3CDDA; background: #FFFFFF; color: #0B1D35;", "background: #F6F8FB; border-color: #7C8BA0;", "background: #E9EEF5; border-color: #7C8BA0;")

const input = byRef("input")
input.description = "Standard form field (Input, NativeSelect or Textarea inside Field) for asset, employee, location, search, and settings fields. White fill, 3:1 border, focus halo, error through aria-invalid."
input.html = `<div class="ds-field"><label class="ds-field-label" for="ds-asset-tag">Asset tag</label><input id="ds-asset-tag" class="ds-input" placeholder="Search asset tag" aria-describedby="ds-asset-tag-hint" /><p class="ds-field-hint" id="ds-asset-tag-hint">Letters, digits and dashes</p></div>`
input.css = `.ds-field { display: grid; gap: 6px; font-family: ${font}; color: #0B1D35; } .ds-field-label { font-size: 13px; line-height: 1.5385; font-weight: 500; } .ds-field-hint { margin: 0; font-size: 13px; line-height: 1.5385; color: #56677F; } .ds-input { box-sizing: border-box; height: 44px; width: 100%; border: 1px solid #7C8BA0; border-radius: 8px; background: #FFFFFF; color: #0B1D35; padding: 0 12px; font: 400 16px/1.5 ${font}; outline: none; transition: border-color 150ms ease-out, box-shadow 150ms ease-out; } @media (min-width: 768px) { .ds-input { height: 40px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5714; } } .ds-input::placeholder { color: #56677F; } .ds-input:focus { border-color: #10858D; box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px #10858D, 0 0 0 4px rgb(16 133 141 / 0.16); } .ds-input[aria-invalid="true"] { border-color: #B3261E; box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px #B3261E, 0 0 0 4px rgb(179 38 30 / 0.14); } .ds-input[aria-invalid="true"]:focus { border-color: #10858D; box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px #10858D, 0 0 0 4px rgb(16 133 141 / 0.16); } .ds-input:disabled { background: #F6F8FB; border-color: #D7DEE8; color: #56677F; cursor: not-allowed; }`

const badge = byRef("badge-status")
badge.description = "One StatusBadge in one size (22px, 13/20 text, 6px corners) and two tiers with shape-coded markers. Calm tier (info ring, success/primary dot, neutral-ink bar): no fill or side padding, muted-foreground text. Attention tier (warning triangle, danger diamond): soft fill, border, 8px side padding and tone ink. Every badge carries a text label; database status colors are not shown. In high-contrast mode markers use the system text color."
let badgeCss = badge.css
badgeCss = swap(badgeCss, ".ds-status-badge { display: inline-flex; width: fit-content;", ".ds-status-badge { box-sizing: border-box; display: inline-flex; height: 22px; width: fit-content;", "badge box")
badgeCss = swap(badgeCss, "border-radius: 8px; padding: 4px 0;", "border-radius: 6px; padding: 0;", "badge radius")
badgeCss = swap(badgeCss, `font: 500 14px/1.5714 ${font};`, `font: 500 13px/1.5385 ${font};`, "badge font")
badgeCss = swap(badgeCss, "border-radius: 1px; background: #3C4F6B; }", "border-radius: 1px; background: #2A4468; }", "neutral marker")
badgeCss = swap(badgeCss, ".ds-status-warning { padding: 4px 10px;", ".ds-status-warning { padding: 0 8px;", "warning padding")
badgeCss = swap(badgeCss, ".ds-status-danger { padding: 4px 10px;", ".ds-status-danger { padding: 0 8px;", "danger padding")
badge.css = badgeCss

rule("The Where Color Lives Rule").body =
  "Navy #083161 lives only in the logo and the selected menu row. Actions and links use Primary #1E4F94. Teal is for info status, the focus ring (#10858D) and the selected menu icon. Green, orange and red appear only inside status badges, as the icon of a toast, a Notice or an ActionEmptyState, and on destructive buttons and menu items; only warning and danger toasts and Notice boxes get a soft fill. Until phase 2D, info notices, role chips and a few info icons also use the info tokens and so appear teal (phase 2A changed only their borders)."
rule("The Token Contrast Rule").body =
  "Status text uses the status ink (text-success, text-warning, text-danger, text-info) on white, the page background, canvas, muted, accent, row-hover, or its own soft surface (bg-{tone}-soft). Never tint with opacity (bg-warning/10, bg-primary/10, border-warning/30, bg-muted/40) and never lighten solid fills on hover (/90): use the -soft, -border and -hover tokens and the solid neutrals (bg-background, bg-row-hover, border-border-soft). Faint text (#56677F) is AA on every neutral surface and on primary-soft, never on a status soft fill. tests/design-tokens-contrast.test.ts enforces every text pair at 4.5:1 and rings, field borders and meaningful icons at 3:1; button outlines are decorative."
rule("The Thai Line-Height Rule").body =
  "text-sm is 22/14 in every language; Thai pages raise text-xs, text-xl, text-2xl and text-3xl to 18/12, 30/20, 36/24 and 44/30. text-meta (20/13) needs no Thai override; text-metric and text-count hold numbers only. A4 operation documents keep text-sm at 20/14 so the signature row stays on page 1."
rule("The Border First Rule").body =
  "Use a border and tonal surface for grouping. Panels, cards, sections, and the topbar are flat; only dialogs, sheets, popovers, menus, and toasts use shadow-overlay (dialogs and sheets over a bg-scrim backdrop). Six shadow-sm stay on controls over a camera image or photo and on paper previews; a per-file budget guard keeps the rest from coming back."
d.narrative.dos[0] =
  "Do preserve existing tokens and shared primitives such as Button, the field primitives (Input, NativeSelect, Textarea, Field, SearchField), Notice, EmptyNote, ActionEmptyState, and the panel, table shell and mobile card list helpers."

const text = JSON.stringify(d, null, 2) + "\n"
writeFileSync(path, crlf ? text.replace(/\n/g, "\r\n") : text)
console.log(`updated ${path}: colorMeta ${Object.keys(d.extensions.colorMeta).length} colors, shadows ${d.extensions.shadows.length}, motion ${d.extensions.motion.length}, typographyMeta ${Object.keys(d.extensions.typographyMeta).length}`)
for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(added)) console.log(`${name} ${value.canonical} ${value.tonalRamp.join(" | ")}`)
```

Replace:

```md
## Current-Surface Registry
```

with:

```md
## Shared Components

Build screens from the shared primitives in `src/components/ui/`. Their look and rules live in [`DESIGN.md` §5](../DESIGN.md#5-components); do not copy their class strings into pages.

| Need | Use | Remember |
| --- | --- | --- |
| A button, or a link that looks like one | `Button`, or `buttonVariants({ variant, size })` on a `Link` | `danger` for a destructive action in a page, `destructive` only to confirm it in a dialog; `busy` while working, never together with `disabled` or `asChild` |
| A text link | `linkVariants({ size })` | `meta` when it stands alone, `inherit` inside a sentence |
| A text field, select or textarea | `Input`, `NativeSelect`, `Textarea` with `fieldSize` | `getFieldControlClasses()` for a control that cannot use them |
| A label with hint and error | `Field` | sets `id`, `aria-describedby`, `aria-invalid` and `aria-required`; never around `SearchableSelect`, never inside a `<label>` |
| A search box | `SearchField` | `onClear` and `clearLabel` only from a client component |
| A searchable picker | `SearchableSelect` | has its own label, hint and error |
| An action menu | `DropdownMenu` | an item that cannot run now uses `unavailable` with `DropdownMenuItemDescription` for the reason |
| A keyboard hint | `Kbd` | Latin keys and symbols only |
| A status | `StatusBadge` | one size; there is no `size` prop |
| A breadcrumb trail | `Breadcrumbs` | `code: true` for an asset tag or document number |
| An empty value | `Nil`, `orNil` | `0` is a value, not blank |
| A count or an amount of money | `formatCount`, `formatMoney` (`src/lib/display-format.ts`) | always th-TH and 2 decimals for money; the unit (`common.baht`) goes after the number, or in the column header of a table |
| A toast | `toast.*` or `useApiError().toast` | `AppToaster` styles every toast; do not pass `richColors` |
| A message box | `Notice` with `tone` | write `role` or `aria-live` at the call site |
| An empty state | `EmptyNote` for one line, `ActionEmptyState` for a title, description or action | |

Phase 2A built these; phases 2B to 2D move the hand-written controls onto them. The ratchet guards in `tests/visual-foundation-guards.test.ts` let their per-file budgets go down, never up.

## Current-Surface Registry
```
